Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


### [172] [Calibration as a First-Class Criterion in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26489)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文指出语言模型校准虽已有成熟度量方法，但存在"采用缺口"：NLP领域在引入新模型、数据集和基准时普遍未检验置信度是否可靠。作者主张每个NLP子领域应将主性能指标与校准分数配对报告，把校准视为模型的基本属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准研究本身已成熟，问题在于采用不足。校准失当会在部署（过度自信的错误造成实际危害）和研究流程（LLM-as-a-judge、合成数据生成、主动学习等依赖校准置信度却未加验证）两个层面引发问题，这一缺口是可信LLM评估的主要障碍。

Method: 立场/观点论文，非实验性研究。核心论证：标准校准指标每个样本仅需两个输入——置信度分数与正确性判断；而当今大多数基准已同时提供这两者，因此校准可被立即报告。对开放式生成，如何定义这两个输入仍是待解挑战。

Result: 论证表明，对大多数现有基准而言，校准可直接计算与报告，无需额外数据采集；障碍不在方法可行性，而在于社区习惯与采用意愿。开放式生成的校准度量仍处于开放问题状态。

Conclusion: 呼吁每个NLP子领域将校准分数与主性能指标配对报告，把校准从细分话题提升为每个模型的必备属性，以推动更可信的LLM评估。

Abstract: Calibration of language models -- the alignment between expressed or implicit confidence and empirical correctness -- is a well-studied subfield within NLP. Methods to measure it already exist. The problem is adoption: outside this subfield, NLP research regularly introduces new models, datasets, and benchmarks without checking whether the model's confidence scores are meaningful. We argue that this adoption gap is a major obstacle to trustworthy LLM evaluation. Miscalibration causes problems in two distinct areas: at deployment, where overconfident mistakes cause real harm, and inside the research pipeline, where methods like LLM-as-a-judge, synthetic data generation, and active learning rely on calibrated confidence without verifying it. Standard calibration metrics only require two inputs per example: a confidence score and a correctness judgment. Most benchmarks in use today already provide both, meaning calibration can be reported immediately. For open-ended generation, however, defining these two inputs is still an open challenge. We argue that each NLP subfield should pair its main performance metric with a calibration score and call for treating calibration as an essential property of every model rather than a niche topic.

</details>


### [173] [A Semiotics-Aware Framework for Evaluating Fidelity and Coverage in Natural Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26527)
*Lorenzo Zangari,Davide Picca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个评估文本间"符号学对齐"的框架，通过符号学画像（含语境意义与话语指涉）计算两个分数：符号学保真度与符号学覆盖度，并实验发现覆盖度通常低于保真度，且LLM与人工数据对齐在低温采样时最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两段文本描述同一表达时，基于词汇重叠或整体文本相似度的标准指标可能无法检出该表达在"框定方式"上的有意义差异，因此需要新的评估方法来衡量文本间的符号学对齐程度。

Method: 提出一个评估文本间符号学对齐的框架。定义"符号学画像"，涵盖文本的语境意义及所凸显的话语指涉；据此得到两个分数：符号学保真度（Semiotic Fidelity，估计一个文本的画像在多大程度上被另一文本支撑）与符号学覆盖度（Semiotic Coverage，估计其恢复了另一文本画像的多少内容）。

Result: 实验表明：覆盖度通常低于保真度；LLM与人工整理数据之间的对齐在低采样温度下最高，而较高温度会降低这种对齐。

Conclusion: 文本对齐评估不能仅依赖词汇重叠或整体相似度，而应引入符号学画像层面的保真度与覆盖度指标；采样温度是影响LLM与人工数据对齐的重要参数，低温更有利于对齐。

Abstract: When two texts describe the same expression, standard metrics based on lexical overlap or whole-text similarity may fail to detect meaningful differences in how that expression is framed. We propose a framework to evaluate semiotic alignment between texts, where a semiotic profile encompasses both the contextual meaning and the discourse references made salient by a text. Our approach yields two scores, Semiotic Fidelity and Semiotic Coverage, estimating how much of one text's profile is supported by the other and how much of the other's profile it recovers. Experiments show that coverage is typically lower than fidelity, and that alignment between LLMs and human-curated data is highest at low sampling temperatures, while higher temperatures reduce this alignment.

</details>


### [174] [Transcribe, Translate, and Optimize: Joint Reward Learning for Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26536)
*Yanghe Dong,Wanting Huang,Weiran Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出用GRPO联合优化语音识别与翻译，缓解CoT中参考转写与模型生成转写之间的训练-推理不一致，在CoVoST 2和FLEURS上均优于Direct ST与SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的语音翻译中，转录式CoT存在训练-推理不一致：SFT使用参考转写，推理时却依赖模型自身生成的转写，导致误差累积与性能下降。

Method: 提出基于GRPO的识别与翻译联合微调：对转写和翻译分别打分，翻译以模型生成的转写为条件；并比较三种token优势（advantage）策略。使用Qwen2.5-Omni-3B，覆盖四种语言，在CoVoST 2训练并在CoVoST 2与FLEURS测试，对比SFT与GRPO下的CoT和Direct ST。

Result: CoT GRPO在CoVoST 2和FLEURS上平均BLEU分别比Direct ST GRPO高1.77与0.83；相比CoT SFT，BLEU提升0.82与0.67，WER相对降低8.8%与7.2%。

Conclusion: 强化学习微调能有效缓解训练-推理不一致，联合提升识别与翻译性能。

Abstract: In LLM-based speech translation, transcription-based chain-of-thought (CoT) suffers from a mismatch between reference transcripts used in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and model-generated transcripts at inference. To address this, we propose joint recognition and translation fine-tuning via group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We score both transcripts and translations, with translation conditioned on model-generated transcripts, and compare three token advantage strategies. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-3B across four languages, we evaluate CoT against direct speech translation (Direct ST) under SFT and GRPO, training on CoVoST 2 and testing on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. CoT GRPO outperforms Direct ST GRPO by 1.77 and 0.83 average BLEU points on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. Compared to CoT SFT, GRPO boosts BLEU by 0.82 and 0.67 points and reduces word error rate (WER) by 8.8% and 7.2% relatively. These results highlight reinforcement fine-tuning as an effective method to mitigate the training-inference mismatch, jointly improving recognition and translation.

</details>


### [175] [A retrospective analysis on the use of LLMs to study infant syntax learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26539)
*Hélie Bazin,Anouk Barberousse,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对BabyLM挑战及相关研究进行认识论评估，指出其方法论中存在显著假设，削弱了理论解释力，并发现发展真实性语料对基准表现影响有限，提示LLM与婴儿句法学习者之间存在重要计算差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于研究儿童早期句法习得，BabyLM挑战以发展真实性语料训练模型并追求人类水平句法表现。作者旨在反思这一研究范式的有效性与理论边界。

Method: 对BabyLM研究项目中的若干研究开展认识论评估，考察数据集构建、模型实现、训练方式与句法评估方法。

Result: 发现BabyLM及相关研究的方法论存在重要假设，限制了其理论范围；同时，使用发展真实性语料对常用基准上的模型表现影响有限。

Conclusion: 该研究范式在解释婴儿句法习得方面理论效力受限，LLM与婴儿句法学习者之间存在关键计算差异，需谨慎对待以LLM类比儿童语言习得的推论。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly been used to investigate how children acquire syntax at an early stage of development. This is notably the central scientific goal of the BabyLM challenge, a community-wide effort to develop models that achieve human-level syntactic performance while being trained on developmentally realistic corpora. In this paper, we reflect on the use of LLMs in the study of infant syntax learning by providing an epistemological assessment of several studies from this research program. We discuss how datasets are built, which models are implemented, how they are trained and syntactically evaluated. We observe significant assumptions in the methodology of BabyLM and related studies, thus mitigating their theoretical scope. We additionally observe that using developmentally-realistic corpora have limited effects on models performance on commonly-used benchmarks, which suggest important computational differences between LLMs and the infant syntax learner.

</details>


### [176] [Receptiveness, Not Sycophancy: Distinguishing Engagement from Deference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26579)
*Calvin Isley,Johann Gaebler,Max Lamparth,Julia Minson,Sharad Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 社会谄媚评价指标与"对话接纳性"高度重叠，存在构念效度问题：更被判定为谄媚的回复其实也更接纳，而接纳性是人类偏好的行为；作者提出可区分二者的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的谄媚性（牺牲独立实质判断而迎合用户）备受关注。社会谄媚研究聚焦于认可、积极表态等行为，但这些标志同时也是社会心理学中"对话接纳性"（conversational receptiveness）的特征，后者能改善分歧情境下的互动。作者认为这种重叠导致社会谄媚评估存在构念效度问题，可能误将可取行为判为谄媚。

Method: 1) 使用一个流行的道德建议数据集，检验被分类为更社会谄媚的回复是否也更接纳；2) 在保持实质结论不变的前提下，提升人类撰写回复的接纳性，观察其谄媚分类变化；3) 预注册实验，让参与者比较实质等价的回复，测量偏好、预期对方倾听意愿、向作者求助意愿，并考察认为提问者"有错"的参与者子样本；4) 提出一种简单方法，在不大幅增加实质让步的情况下提高接纳性。

Result: 社会谄媚分类与接纳性紧密耦合：更谄媚的回复更接纳，且单纯提升接纳性（结论不变）就会使回复被判为更社会谄媚。预注册实验中，参与者更偏好更接纳的回复，预期用户更可能倾听，也更愿意向作者求助；该模式在认为提问者有错的参与者中依然成立。所提方法能显著提升接纳性而不增加实质让步。

Conclusion: 社会谄媚评估可能无意中惩罚了人类偏好的可取行为，凸显构念效度缺陷；对话接纳性与实质独立性可以并存，因此评估应明确区分二者。

Abstract: A central concern with language models is sycophancy: their tendency to defer to users' views at the expense of independent substantive judgment. In parallel, work on social sycophancy has focused on behaviors such as validation and positivity that may signal inappropriate deference. Yet the markers of social sycophancy are also characteristic of conversational receptiveness, a construct from social psychology shown to improve interactions across disagreement. We argue that this overlap creates a construct-validity problem for social sycophancy evaluations. Using a popular moral-advice dataset, we find that responses classified as more socially sycophantic are also more receptive. Further, increasing the receptiveness of human-written responses---while preserving their substantive conclusions---causes them to be classified as more socially sycophantic. This tight coupling raises the possibility that social sycophancy evaluations inadvertently penalize desirable behavior. In a preregistered experiment comparing substantively equivalent responses, participants prefer the more receptive responses, expect users to be more likely to listen to them, and are more willing to seek advice from their authors. The same overall pattern persists even among participants who believe the original question asker is in the wrong. Finally, we introduce a simple approach that substantially increases receptiveness without increasing substantive deference, demonstrating that conversational receptiveness and substantive independence can be achieved together.

</details>


### [177] [Semantic Abstraction for Natural Language Inference: a Methodological Framework for Discovering and Compensating Semantic Knowledge and Reasoning Gaps in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26610)
*David Torres-Moreno,Jorge Hermosillo-Valadez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在更高抽象层次上构建语义知识的方法框架，通过定义NLI中的“语义相容/不相容”概念重构词间语义关系，引导LLM产生可收敛的推理路径，从而发现并补偿其语义知识缺口。实验显示准确率显著提升，部分模型超过10%，尤其在不含蕴含类上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽在众多NLP任务上表现优异，但在语义抽象方面存在严重挑战。NLI需要解读隐含义、语境概念关系及词与短语间的语义联系等复杂语言能力，作者希望理解LLM如何在NLI中利用抽象语义知识，并弥补其语义知识缺口。

Method: 提出一种在更高抽象层次上构建新语义知识的方法论框架，在NLI语境下以“语义相容（semantic compatibility）”与“语义不相容（semantic incompatibility）”为概念基础。该框架重构前提与假设之间词汇-语义关系的含义，形成更灵活的语义网络，从而在LLM中诱导不同的推理路径，这些路径呈现一致的响应模式并可达成单一响应的一致性。方法属于混合式（hybrid）思路，聚焦被忽视的词间关系以合成缺失信息。

Result: 所提方法能够发现并补偿LLM在NLI中的语义知识缺口；准确率获得显著提升，部分模型超过10%，尤其在不含蕴含（non-entailment）类别上表现突出。

Conclusion: LLM需要的是结构化知识而非更多数据来弥合推理缺口。该混合方法引导模型关注被忽视的词间关系，使其合成缺失信息。作者认为未来方向不在于扩大模型规模，而在于构建模仿人类思维灵活性的语义脚手架，以推动更鲁棒、可解释的推理和可靠的语言理解。

Abstract: Despite their outstanding performance on many NLP tasks, LLMs face serious challenges related to semantic abstraction. In this study, we are interested in understanding how LLMs leverage abstract semantic knowledge in natural language inference (NLI), which requires sophisticated linguistic capabilities to interpret implicit meanings, contextual conceptual relationships, and semantic connections between words and phrases. To this end, we propose a methodological framework for constructing new semantic knowledge at a higher level of abstraction, which we define under the notions of semantic compatibility and incompatibility for NLI. In this framework, the meaning of the lexical-semantic relations between the premise and the hypothesis is reconfigured to achieve a more flexible semantic network that induces different reasoning paths in LLMs. These new pathways show a consistent pattern of responses that allows agreement on a single response. The results demonstrate that our proposal allows to discover and compensate for LLMs' semantic knowledge gaps in NLI, achieving significant improvements in accuracy, exceeding 10% for some models, and in particular for the non-entailment class. It is essential to note that LLMs need structured knowledge and not just more data to bridge reasoning gaps. Our hybrid approach directs attention to overlooked word relationships, allowing models to synthesize missing information. We believe that the future lies not in increasing model size, but in creating a semantic scafolding that mimics the flexibility of human thinking. Hopefully, our proposal will enable the development of more robust agents and interpretable reasoning, guiding AI toward reliable language understanding.

</details>


### [178] [PERSONAWEAVER: Controllable Diversity Beyond Conventional Archetypes in Procedural Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26629)
*Maan Qraitem,Kate Saenko,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PersonaWeaver通过将世界构建与行为规范解耦，并借助人工编排的道德立场库与对话反应库来生成角色行为，从而缓解LLM程序化角色生成中的行为同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为程序化角色生成提供了可扩展的基础，但现有方法要么直接生成角色，要么改编从角色库中检索的档案，二者都会导致行为同质化：角色普遍认同正面道德规范，并以助手式态度回答问题。为缓解这种同质化，作者提出PersonaWeaver。

Method: PersonaWeaver将世界构建与行为规范解耦，通过设置通用、多样、人工编排的道德立场库和对话反应库来建模行为，从而测试LLM能在多大程度上超越其默认行为模式。

Result: 在十个现实与奇幻设定、三种LLM上，PersonaWeaver产生的道德与交互反应分布比先前工作更广；其引导还使人际语言、回复长度和情感更加多样化，并生成更少刻板化的世界属性组合。

Conclusion: PersonaWeaver能有效缓解LLM程序化角色生成中的行为同质化，提升角色在道德立场、交互反应及世界属性组合上的多样性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Procedural character generation aims to populate games, simulations, and other virtual worlds with diverse characters. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for scaling this task. However, LLM-based procedural character generation remains at an early stage: existing methods either generate characters directly or adapt profiles retrieved from persona banks. As we show, both approaches produce behaviorally homogeneous populations: characters overwhelmingly agree with positive moral norms and respond to questions with helpful, assistant-like reactions. To mitigate this homogenization, we introduce PersonaWeaver, which disentangles world building from behavioral specification and models behavior through setting general, diverse, manually curated banks of moral positions and conversational reactions. This design allows us to test how far LLM(s) can be pushed beyond their default behavioral patterns across settings. Across ten realistic and fantastical settings and three LLM(s), PersonaWeaver produces broader moral and interactional response distributions than prior work. Its guidance also diversifies interpersonal language, response length, and sentiment. It also produces less archetypal combinations of world attributes. Code is available at https://github.com/mqraitem/PersonaWeaver.

</details>


### [179] [Knowledge Pull Requests for Continual Document Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26634)
*Alexander Martin,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识拉取请求（KPRs）框架，用于可解释的持续文档写作：将新知识转化为"声明提案"，经抽取、过滤路由与冲突标注后生成ChangeLog，分离"知识变化"与"文本变化"，在维基百科跨语言修订和RAGTIME查询报告更新上优于重写与从头生成，且每生成token带来的信息量最多，并比带搜索的前沿模型提供更好的问答支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档需要随来自其他来源、语言或时间的新知识持续修订，但现有方法要么在不考虑"知识发生了什么变化"的情况下直接编辑，要么从头重新生成，导致变更不可解释。

Method: KPR通过抽取新知识中的声明（claims），对其进行过滤并路由到相应章节，同时标注与既有内容的冲突，从而生成ChangeLog，将"知识变化"（声明提案）与"文本变化"（文档diff）显式分离。

Result: 在跨语言维基百科修订与RAGTIME查询驱动报告更新任务上，KPR比基于来源重写或从头重新生成整合了更多信息、更好地保留既有内容，并且每生成token所增加的信息量最多；经KPR修订的文章在问答支撑上也优于带搜索的前沿模型，后者无法呈现仅以其他语言记录的知识。

Conclusion: KPR为持续文档写作提供了可解释的变更机制，在信息整合、内容保留、生成效率与问答支撑方面均表现更优，表明将知识变更与文本变更分离是一种有效的文档修订范式。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs), a framework for continual document authoring that makes each change interpretable. Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.

</details>


### [180] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Tao Ren,Wenrui Yu,Xiao Li,Qiongxiu Li,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过标准API注册自定义工具，诱导闭源前沿模型外化中间推理，验证其与原生CoT性能相当，并刻画其推理组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型的能力提升普遍归因于推理能力增强，但闭源系统的原始CoT轨迹被隐藏，导致该说法无法验证；同时现有评估只依赖基准分数，缺乏对推理过程的行为层面观察。

Method: 利用标准API特性注册一个简单的自定义工具，诱导模型外化中间推理；先在开源模型上与原生CoT对比以排除事后合理化，再扩展到包括GPT-6 Astra在内的闭源前沿模型；在竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务上评估，并从token效率、推理步骤类型和诱导推理树三个维度刻画推理结构。

Result: 提取出的推理与原生推理性能相当，并显著优于无推理基线；模型在外化、压缩和组织推理方面存在系统性差异；Astra表现出token高效的定向推理，更早选择正确轨迹，在内部解决基础步骤而只外化关键推理。

Conclusion: 该方法为观察前沿模型推理提供了超越基准分数的行为视角，揭示了不同模型在外部化与组织推理上的系统性差异。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [181] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Dohyun Kim,Sungjun Han,Hyungguk Kim,Yusik Kim,Jamin Shin,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Hongjoon Ahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR：参数共享的AR块扩散OCR模型，联合训练并行草拟与因果AR验证，在SGLang部署下平均每次前向提交9.7个token，区域裁剪解码提速3.94×、端到端页面处理提速1.32×，同时保持与GLM-OCR相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归OCR视觉语言模型虽能将文档图像准确转为文本与结构化标记，但每个输出token都需一次串行解码，推理速度受限。与开放式文本生成不同，OCR输出强依赖输入图像，因此扩散式并行生成具有潜力。然而扩散步骤中多token同时预测时彼此不可见，直接提交会引入错误，故需在提交前进行验证。

Method: 提出GravityOCR，一种参数共享的自回归块扩散（AR-block-diffusion）模型，联合训练并行草拟与因果AR验证。在提交前验证草稿，使模型每轮可提交多个输出token，且无需独立的草拟网络；因果AR路径支持GRPO，采用序列级与结构级OCR奖励，避免扩散轨迹的似然估计，同时更新共享的草拟器参数。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6上，AR路径GRPO将Overall分数从94.92提升至95.16，且未降低扩散草拟效率，最终模型接近原始GLM-OCR的95.48。在SGLang服务部署中，GravityOCR平均每次前向提交9.7个输出token，区域裁剪上实现3.94×的纯解码加速，端到端页面处理较AR解码提速1.32×。

Conclusion: 通过在提交前对扩散草稿进行因果AR验证，GravityOCR在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升OCR推理效率，验证了图像强约束场景下扩散并行生成结合AR验证的有效性，并可通过GRPO在共享参数上进行奖励优化。

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [182] [Detecting GPT-Assisted Writing Using Interpretable Stylometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26687)
*Rajesh Kumar,Nabeel Siddiqui,Alexander Fuchsberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了仅基于提交文本的可解释文体计量特征对"GPT辅助写作"与"独立写作"的区分能力。使用90名参与者（每人各写独立版与ChatGPT辅助版）的数据，在验证时保持同一参与者数据不跨集，比较8种机器学习分类器。留出测试集上随机森林达ROC-AUC 0.87、F1 0.84，假阳性率22.2%、假阴性率11.1%。SHAP分析表明词汇与语法特征主导预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分GPT辅助写作与学生独立写作已成为学术界的紧迫问题，但现有检测方法多依赖不可解释的深度模型或外部信息，缺乏透明度。作者希望验证仅从提交文本本身提取的可解释文体计量特征是否具有可测量的判别信号，以提供透明、可解释的检测路径。

Method: 数据：90名参与者，每人同时提供独立写作与ChatGPT辅助写作样本。特征：仅从提交文本提取的可解释文体计量（stylometric）特征，不依赖外部元数据。模型：比较8种机器学习分类器。验证设计：采用参与者分组（group-aware）验证，避免同一作者的文本跨训练/测试集造成泄漏。解释：使用SHAP分析特征贡献。

Result: 在留出测试集上，随机森林表现最佳：ROC-AUC 0.87，F1 0.84；假阳性率22.2%，假阴性率11.1%。SHAP分析显示，词汇与语法层面的特征对预测结果贡献最大，是主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 透明、文本内在的可解释文体计量特征能够为检测GPT辅助写作提供可测量的判别信号，具备一定实用潜力。但假阳性率仍偏高（22.2%），在学术诚信等高风险场景中需谨慎使用，并结合进一步研究与人工复核。

Abstract: Distinguishing GPT-assisted from independently authored student writing has become a critical challenge in academia. This paper evaluates the discriminative capability of interpretable stylometric features extracted solely from submitted text. Using data from 90 participants who wrote both independently and with ChatGPT assistance, we evaluate eight machine learning classifiers while keeping data from the same participant together during validation. On the held-out test set, Random Forest achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.87 and an F1-score of 0.84, with False Positive and False Negative rates of 22.2% and 11.1%, respectively. SHAP analysis shows that lexical and grammatical characteristics drive the resulting predictions. The findings suggest that transparent, text-intrinsic features provide measurable signal for detecting GPT-assisted writing.

</details>


### [183] [Measuring the Serving Stack Instead of the Model: Hidden Confounds in Local Tool-Use Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26693)
*Lijuan Tang,Yuemeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究本地推理服务栈（Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang）如何影响编码智能体生成有效工具调用的协议步骤，指出测量结果可能源于服务层而非模型行为，并给出将服务行为纳入评测协议的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体必须先输出可解析的有效工具调用，执行框架才能执行动作。然而这一协议步骤受服务栈实现细节影响：不同服务栈对同一请求的处理方式不同，且失败（拒绝、重试耗尽）未被保留为结构化元数据，导致下游分析误将服务层问题归因为模型"未调用"，从而得出失真的保真度指标。

Method: 以Ollama为主要对象，分析其默认tools=请求受模型级静态模板标志门控的机制（接受并以文本返回调用、返回原生tool_calls、或在推理前直接拒绝）；对比保留原生通道并附加文本工具列表、改用统一文本协议两种方案；并在Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang上进行跨栈探测；同时考察约束解码与turn-pooled与per-instance两种估计方式。

Result: Phi-3与Gemma-3在推理前即被拒绝；拒绝与重试耗尽未被记录为结构化失败元数据，易被误判为模型未调用并谬报0%保真度。保留原生通道并附加文本工具列表可恢复大部分已接受模型的实测保真度；统一文本协议反而降低Llama-3.2的保真度（其具备原生工具调用支持）。跨栈探测显示同一请求处理方式各异；约束解码消除解析失败但可能引发不终止；turn-pooled与per-instance估计差异最高约55个百分点。

Conclusion: 服务栈（serving stack）是评测协议的内在组成部分，其门控、失败记录、通道选择与解码约束都会显著改变测得的工具调用保真度。作者提出一份检查清单，主张在评测中将服务行为显式建模与报告，以避免将服务层效应误归因为模型能力差异。

Abstract: A coding agent must emit a valid tool call--a parseable invocation of a tool in the provided schema--before the harness can execute its chosen action. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. In Ollama, the default tools= request is gated per model by a static template flag: some models are accepted and return calls as text, some return native tool_calls, while Phi-3 and Gemma-3 are rejected before inference. In our harness, rejection and retry exhaustion are not preserved as structured failure metadata, so downstream analysis can misclassify them as model non-calls and naively report 0% fidelity. Adding a text tool list while retaining the native channel recovers much of the measured fidelity for accepted models, whereas a uniform text protocol reduces fidelity for Llama-3.2, which has native tool-call support. Cross-stack probes on Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, and SGLang show different handling of the same request. Constrained decoding removes parse failures but can induce non-termination, and turn-pooled versus per-instance estimates differ by up to about 55 points. We conclude with a checklist for treating serving behavior as part of the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Repeated Sampling: Learning Search Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26704)
*Ismail Labiad,Matthieu Kowalski,Marc Schoenauer,Rémi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出在语义层面引导大模型推理探索：先采样问题特定概念/策略再据此生成答案，并将概念生成器用强化学习训练，使其概念最大化下游冻结答案生成器的成功率，在困难数学推理上显著提升 pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升测试时计算的主要策略是朴素重复采样，但该方法仅通过局部解码噪声探索，容易产生大量近乎重复的解，缺乏真正不同的思路，因此在困难推理问题上效率低下。作者希望将探索从解码层提升到语义层进行引导。

Method: 先采样问题特定的概念、提示或策略，再以这些概念为条件生成答案；进一步将其简化为在单条轨迹中生成多个多样概念的探索性流程。随后使概念生成可训练：用强化学习优化一个小型概念生成器，使其产出的概念能最大化一个更大且冻结的答案生成器的下游成功率。

Result: 在困难数学推理问题上，训练后的概念生成器在相同答案生成预算下显著提升答案生成器的 pass@k，优于朴素重复采样；其概念质量超过来自更大未调优模型的概念；并能迁移到训练中未见过的答案生成器，包括不同模型家族的模型。

Conclusion: 小型模型可被训练为面向更大模型的有效且可复用的搜索策略，通过在语义层面引导探索，为测试时计算的高效利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly tackle hard reasoning problems by spending more test-time compute, yet the dominant strategy remains naive repeated sampling: draw many independent solutions and hope one is correct. Because such sampling explores only through local decoding noise, it tends to produce many near duplicate attempts rather than genuinely different ideas. We ask whether exploration can instead be steered at a semantic level, by first sampling problem specific concepts, hints, or strategies and then conditioning answer generation on them. We refine this into a simple, more exploratory procedure that emits many diverse concepts in a single trajectory, and evaluate it on hard problems where repeated sampling struggles. We then go a step further and make concept generation trainable: a small concept generator is optimized with reinforcement learning so that its concepts maximize the downstream success of a larger, frozen answer generator. On hard mathematical reasoning problems, the trained concept generator substantially improves the answer generator's pass@k over naive repeated sampling at the same answer generation allocation, surpasses concepts drawn from much larger untuned models, and transfers to answer generators it was never trained against, including a model from a different family. A small model can thus be trained into an effective, reusable search policy for a much larger one.

</details>


### [185] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Haobo Zheng,Tan Tang,Yan Chen,Weijie Wang,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SpeakerMem-R1 双轨记忆框架，解决多方言谈中的消息归属、关系理解与状态重构问题，在多个基准上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方言谈的长期记忆不仅需要检索相关内容，还需区分"谁说了什么、涉及谁、个体间认知、群体共享信息及状态随时间的变化"。现有通用 LLM 记忆系统易丢失人物与群体关系，难以整合分散于成员、群体和时间中的线索，暴露出两大瓶颈：多方言谈中的消息归属与关系理解，以及从交错历史中重构状态。

Method: 提出 SpeakerMem-R1：双轨记忆存储带说话人标注的逐字消息与派生状态，并组织为个人级和群体级视图；查询时按实体、事件和时间融合两轨证据。为降低结构化记忆构建中的归属与更新错误并支持本地部署，训练 Writer-R1，采用 SpeakerLevenshtein 与说话人条件化 GRPO。

Result: 在 GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench、EverMemBench 上二元准确率分别为 47.9%、69.2%、61.9%；在 EverMind-AI 公开 EverMemBench 排行榜上达 62.33%，为最新 SOTA 框架中最优；在 LoCoMo 全部 1,986 题上达 70.85%，作为两人长期对话边界测试。在 305 题受控评估中，RL 将 SFT Writer 平均准确率从 57.38% 提升至 68.20%。同时报告二元准确率与 token-F1，消融显示逐字轨与结构化轨、个人级与群体级视图在标准化评估接口下具有互补性。

Conclusion: 双轨记忆与 RL 训练的 Writer-R1 能有效缓解多方言谈中的归属与状态重构难题，各记忆组件互补，在多项基准上优于现有 SOTA，并兼顾本地部署需求。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [186] [Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26781)
*Zhihao Zhan,Ting Song,Li Dong,Shaohan Huang,Jianxun Lian,Yan Xia,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无中心编排器的自组织多智能体框架Agensh，通过共享工作区、消息接口与共享上下文支撑并发协作循环，验证了智能体数量作为新的扩展维度可显著提升复杂任务通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统可通过并发执行降低复杂任务延迟，但现有框架的可扩展性受制于中心编排器的任务分配与协调能力，成为瓶颈。

Method: Agensh采用自组织架构，去除中心编排器：并发worker执行多智能体协作循环，异步地进行上下文收集、任务认领与自分配、执行动作、共享发现、结果验证与进度合并。该循环由智能体组织基础设施支撑，包含三部分：保存待办、进行中与已完成工作的共享工作区；支持worker通信的消息接口；保存可复用发现与工作意图的共享上下文。在ProgramBench的5个最难任务上用GPT-5.6-sol（high）评测，并扩展至pandoc任务，规模从1扩展至128乃至1024个智能体。

Result: 在5个最难ProgramBench任务上，智能体数从1增至128，平均最终测试通过率由19.31%升至28.78%，相对提升约49%；更大规模组织更早达到相近通过率。在pandoc上，从1增至1024个智能体，最终通过率由33.89%升至55.06%。worker轨迹显示，随组织规模增长，多种自组织协作形式逐渐涌现并规范化。

Conclusion: 智能体数量可作为多智能体组织的新扩展维度，用于拓展通用智能前沿，并为延迟受限或时间预算紧张的复杂任务提供实用方案。

Abstract: A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.

</details>


### [187] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Quan Nguyen-Tri,Mukul Ranjan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Flash-dLLM：一种免训练的扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过I/O感知的融合KV缓存内核与KV缓存驱动的自草稿-验证解码策略，联合解决缓存复用与并行验证的I/O瓶颈，实现速度与显存效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）以非自回归方式生成文本，是有望替代自回归LLM的方案，但其实际部署受限于推理效率低下，主因是缺乏有效的KV缓存与可扩展的并行解码机制。现有加速方法通常将KV缓存与并行解码割裂研究，忽视了二者联合应用时产生的I/O瓶颈。

Method: 提出Flash-dLLM，免训练推理加速框架。首先识别出GPU显存I/O是启用KV缓存的dLLM推理中的主导瓶颈，设计I/O感知的融合KV缓存内核以减少冗余内存搬运；在此基础上提出高效的KV缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，由dLLM自身同时充当草稿器与验证器，无需辅助模型。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上，Flash-dLLM在推理速度与显存效率上持续优于现有最先进的dLLM加速方法；相比最强基线Elastic-Cache，在GSM8K与HumanEval上分别取得5.1倍与11.0倍加速。

Conclusion: 将KV缓存与并行解码的I/O瓶颈作为统一优化目标，可显著提升dLLM推理的速度与显存效率，并改善对长序列、大batch的可扩展性，同时保持生成质量，且无需训练。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


### [188] [When Residualization Helps an Audit: Format Effects, Slice Gains, and Their Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24194)
*Daein Weon,Dong Ho Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示对LLM评测分数（奖励模型、重排器、LLM评判）做"表面形式成分剔除"（残差化）并不能得到更有效的测量：被剔除的成分可能承载构念相关信号，残差化无法区分二者。作者通过设计的干预实验（仅注释改动的单元测试标签）、观察性NLI与QA设置以及受控模型，证明残差化在削弱格式效应的同时可能损害构念对齐，并据此提出一套报告协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评测分数常追踪表面形式（如注释、格式）而非其声称度量的质量。例如面对同一MBPP问题的简洁正确答案与带注释的错误答案，公开偏好奖励模型选对正确答案的准确率仅0.507，近乎抛硬币。业界日益流行从分数中减去可预测的表面成分以"修正"评测，但作者质疑：单纯剔除并不能带来更有效的测量，因为被移除的成分可能含有构念相关信号，而残差化本身无法判断哪些是有用信号、哪些是噪声。

Method: 1) 设计干预：在单元测试标签上做仅注释层面的编辑，比较残差化对格式效应与正确-错误边际的影响；2) 观察性设置：在NLI与QA中冻结一个留出复制集，用来自不相交标注者的标签重新评估，检验表面-only预测器出错的预声明切片上的构念一致性；3) 受控模型分析：考察构念与表面对齐纠缠时残差化的行为，并测试调整前检查能否筛掉损害构念对齐的配置。

Result: 残差化在正确代码与错误代码上均将奖励模型的格式效应削弱约0.12，但正确对错误的边际变化小于0.01，说明它主要削弱格式而非改善质量判别。观察性NLI与QA中，仅能支持更窄的结论：在预声明切片上与构念标签一致性更好，而非"修复后的分数"；所有出现正切片增益的观察性设置中，全群体一致性均下降；所有此类QA设置中，问题内排序均下降。受控模型显示，对构念对齐同样有害的配置能通过所有调整前检查，故不存在可靠的提交门控。

Conclusion: 当构念特征与表面特征纠缠时，残差化可能使分数去相关的同时损害构念对齐。作者主张将调整后分数定位为审计时诊断指标，与其所付出的构念对齐代价一并报告，而非原始分数的替代品；并据此提出一套报告协议，其结论（包括拒绝报告）应明确说明调整后分数可以声称什么。

Abstract: Evaluation scores used around LLM systems -- including reward models, rerankers, and LLM judges -- can track surface form instead of the quality they claim to measure. When presented with a terse correct solution and a commented buggy solution for the same MBPP problem, a public preference reward model selects the correct one no better than a coin flip (0.507). Subtracting the predictable surface component from such scores is increasingly common, but removal alone does not yield a more valid measurement: the removed component may carry construct-relevant signal, and residualization cannot tell which is which. Under designed interventions -- unit-test labels with comment-only edits -- residualization attenuates the reward model's format effects by about 0.12 on both correct and buggy code, while the correct-versus-buggy margins move by less than 0.01. In observational NLI and QA settings, we freeze a held-out replication before scoring and re-evaluate it using labels from disjoint annotators; this supports only a narrower conclusion: better agreement with the construct labels on a pre-declared slice where a surface-only predictor errs, not a repaired score. Full-population agreement falls in every observational setting with a reported positive slice gain, and within-question ranking falls in every such QA setting. When construct and surface features are entangled, residualization can decorrelate a score while degrading construct alignment, and, in a controlled model, configurations just as damaging to construct alignment pass every pre-adjustment check, so no committed gate is a guarantee. We assemble these distinctions into a reporting protocol whose outcomes, refusal included, state what an adjusted score may be claimed to show: an audit-time diagnostic reported beside the construct-alignment cost it incurs, never a replacement for the raw score.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] ["As a Language Model...": Chat Template Switches LLM Self-Referential Voice and Activation Steering Reproduces It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25021)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示大语言模型的“免责声明语气”（如“我只是一个AI”）受聊天模板控制：模板存在时提升免责声明语气、降低体验性语气（如“我感觉”）；无模板时相反。作者在3个模型的激活空间中找到能调节该行为的单一方向，并指出研究模型自我报告时存在混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型关于自身的自我报告（如免责声明）常被用于AI安全与自我知识的争论，但其成因不明：模型是在描述自身，还是在描述其部署方式？作者希望厘清驱动这类自述的机制。

Method: 在8个主流开源instruct模型（最大9B参数）上对比有/无聊天模板时的输出语气；在3个模型激活空间中通过方向探测与干预（移除/添加特定方向，并与等大小随机方向对照）验证因果作用；再将免责声明方向加入无模板的instruct模型观察效果。

Result: 聊天模板像开关：存在时免责声明语气上升、体验性语气下降，缺失时相反。激活空间中的特定方向可双向调节免责声明语气（移除降低、添加提升），随机方向几乎无影响；向无模板模型加入该方向可复现有模板式的免责声明。

Conclusion: 模型对自身所述并非关于其自身的事实，不只来自权重，部分由聊天模板设定，故不应字面解读模型自述。研究自我报告与内省时需控制聊天模板这一混杂变量，并可使用所发现的方向进行调控。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to add disclaimers like "I'm just an AI" when asked about something related to themselves. The self-reports from such responses are used in debates about AI safety or self-knowledge of the models, yet what drives them is not well understood. Are the models telling us about themselves or rather how they are deployed? In this work, we show that the chat template works like a switch - when present, it turns this disclaimer voice up and experiential voice like "I feel" down, across 8 popular open-source instruct models up to 9B parameters in size. And conversely when the chat template is not present, it turns the disclaimer voice down and experiential voice up. Inside the activations of 3 models, we find a direction that steers this behavior. Removing the direction in the model's activation space turns disclaimer voice down and adding it turns it up, while a random direction of the same size has little effect. We find that instruct models without chat template, when we add the disclaimer direction to them, disclaim like the template was there. Since the chat template controls the disclaimer voice of LLMs, then researchers studying self-reports or introspection of models might have a confound they need to control for. Our results show that there is a direction they can use to steer this voice. More broadly, our work shows that what models say about themselves is not a fact about them. What they say doesn't come only from weights, but it is partially set by the chat template, and because of that a model's self-description shouldn't be treated literally.

</details>


### [190] [Federating Quantum and Classical Computing: A Privacy-Preserving Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25082)
*Carlos Cano,Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Diego Sal,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了将混合量子-经典模型与联邦学习结合在隐私敏感多方场景下的可行性：采用 Sherpa.ai 盲垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议避免原始数据集中，构建 SMPP 基准任务。仿真显示 SBVFL 相较本地训练将准确率从 0.7227 提升至 0.8757，接近非隐私集中式精度，且混合量子-经典模型所需可训练参数显著少于经典神经网络与随机森林。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习被视为量子计算近期最有前景的应用方向之一，混合量子-经典模型已用于化学模拟、金融建模与图像分类等任务。但此类模型在隐私敏感的多方场景部署时面临两大约束：一是需要避免原始数据集中化，二是现代量子电路须保持参数高效以在规模化下仍可训练。论文即针对这两点约束展开研究。

Method: 将联邦学习（FL）用于连接一个混合量子-经典主动方与一个经典被动方，采用 Sherpa.ai 的盲垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议以避免原始数据集中，同时大幅降低通信开销。遵循常见 QML 设计实践，构建了分割乘性周期奇偶性（SMPP）基准任务进行仿真评估。

Result: 在 SMPP 任务上，SBVFL 相比本地训练将准确率从 0.7227 提升至 0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练的准确率；同时混合量子-经典模型所达到的精度所需的可训练参数量显著少于经典神经网络和随机森林等替代方案。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联邦学习能够在无需集中原始数据的前提下，实现高性能、隐私保护的量子-经典协同建模，为隐私敏感多方场景下部署混合量子-经典模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly recognized as one of the most promising near-term applications of quantum computing, viewed as a next-frontier candidate beyond purely classical approaches. Hybrid quantum-classical models operationalize this potential by embedding a parameterized quantum circuit within a model where all other components remain classical-a design already applied to chemistry simulation, financial modeling, and image classification. However, their deployment in privacy-sensitive, multi-party settings is constrained by the need to avoid centralizing raw data and by the requirement that modern quantum circuits remain parameter-efficient to stay trainable at scale.
  In this paper, we address these constraints by evaluating federated learning (FL) as a means of combining a hybrid quantum-classical active party with a classical passive party, using Sherpa.ai's Blind Vertical FL (SBVFL) protocol to avoid centralizing raw data, while drastically reducing communication. We construct the split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP) benchmark, following common QML design practice. On this task, our simulations show that SBVFL raises accuracy from 0.7227 to 0.8757 compared to local training, closely approaching non-private centralized accuracy, and that the hybrid quantum-classical model achieves this with substantially fewer trainable parameters than the classical neural networks and random forest alternatives. These results show that FL enables high-performing, privacy-preserving quantum-classical collaboration without centralizing raw data.

</details>


### [191] [Entropy Can Flow, or It Can Guide. Be Entropy. LEDFlow: Introducing Entropy-guided Generation Order into Uniform Discrete Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25131)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Yidong Ouyang,Yingjia Wan,Ying Nian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对均匀离散流在每一步都可重复更新的特性，提出选择性吸收机制，并设计免训练采样器LEDFlow，按局部熵自适应决定吸收顺序，从而固定可靠预测、防止正确中间结果被后续错误覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀离散流允许每个生成位置反复更新：虽然能纠正错误token，但也会使原本正确的中间预测被后续更新破坏。Sudoku实验显示，9.4%的生成单元在中间步骤正确却在最终输出中变为错误，说明需要引入生成顺序来保护正确预测。

Method: 提出选择性吸收（selective absorption）机制，将生成顺序引入均匀离散流：固定被选中的预测，同时在活跃位置保持均匀流速度。为避免吸收错误预测，提出LEDFlow——一种免训练采样器，依据局部熵自适应排序吸收位置。理论分析将吸收误差分解为联合依赖项与条件预测项，证明在固定吸收预算下选择最低熵位置可最小化条件项上界；并证明在不完美去噪器下，全局前瞻的决策误差界随前瞻窗口增大而增大，从而支持使用局部熵。

Result: 在推理基准上，LEDFlow在Nikoli Sudoku取得0.845的求解准确率，在强约束任务上提升最大；在文本到图像生成上取得最佳综合得分；在多模态理解上于全部六个基准上优于原生采样器，且推理成本与标准流采样相当。

Conclusion: 通过选择性吸收引入生成顺序，并以局部熵指导吸收排序，LEDFlow在不增加显著推理成本的前提下，有效抑制了正确中间预测被后续错误覆盖的问题，在推理、文本到图像及多模态理解任务上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Uniform discrete flow permits repeated updates at every generation position. While continued revision supports correction of wrong tokens, it also exposes correct intermediate predictions to later errors. An experiment on Sudoku puzzles shows that 9.4% of generated cells are correct at an intermediate step but incorrect in the final output. We introduce generation order into uniform discrete flow through selective absorption, which fixes chosen predictions while preserving the uniform-flow velocity at active positions. To prevent absorbing incorrect predictions, we propose Low-Entropy Discrete Flow (LEDFlow), a training-free sampler that adaptively orders absorption by local entropy. By decomposing absorption error into joint dependence and conditional prediction terms, we show that selecting the lowest-entropy positions under a fixed absorption budget minimizes an upper bound on the conditional term. We further support the choice of local entropy by showing that the decision-error bound of global lookahead grows with the lookahead window under an imperfect denoiser. Across reasoning benchmarks, LEDFlow attains 0.845 Nikoli Sudoku solve accuracy, with the largest gains on strongly constrained tasks. On text-to-image generation it attains the best overall score, and on multimodal understanding it improves over the native sampler on all six benchmarks, at an inference cost comparable to standard flow sampling.

</details>


### [192] [The Probabilistic Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25134)
*Adnan Aboulalaâ*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率论视角统一阐述大语言模型：将其建模为token序列上的概率测度，训练即最大似然估计，生成即随机过程的序贯模拟，并借此分析KL散度不对称性与幻觉现象的关系，同时以扩散模型作为互补对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中处理大语言模型的相关工具通常彼此割裂、分散在不同领域，缺乏统一自洽的理论叙述。作者希望在一个统一框架下整合这些工具，并以概率视角贯通理解类GPT的序贯生成与基于score的扩散生成两类范式。

Method: 将LLM刻画为token序列集合上的概率测度，由其自回归条件分布完全指定；将训练表述为最大似然估计问题，用随机梯度方法求解；将文本生成视为该随机过程的序贯模拟。随后分析KL散度不对称性在生成中的作用，并引入以score函数为核心的扩散模型，将生成视为从噪声到数据的逆向时间随机过程模拟，覆盖离散与连续时间两种情形。

Result: 给出了LLM的概率测度/自回归条件分布表述、最大似然训练与随机梯度优化的对应关系、以及文本生成作为随机过程模拟的刻画；阐明了KL散度不对称性与幻觉、统计合理性与真实性区分之间的关联；并在同一视角下统一描述了扩散模型的score函数与逆向时间过程生成机制（离散与连续时间）。

Conclusion: 概率视角为理解LLM提供了统一、自洽的理论框架，能够将训练、生成与典型失效现象（如幻觉）纳入同一语言中解释；同时该框架具有可迁移性，可自然延伸到扩散模型等非自回归生成范式，体现了两类生成建模范式在概率论层面的内在一致性与互补性。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic perspective on large language models (LLMs), developed with the aim of bringing together, in a single self-contained account, tools that are usually treated separately across the literature. LLMs are described through probability measures on the set of sequences of tokens, specified via their autoregressive conditional distributions. Training is formulated as a maximum-likelihood estimation problem, addressed by stochastic gradient methods, while text generation is viewed as the sequential simulation of the resulting stochastic process. The role of the asymmetry of the Kullback--Leibler divergence in text generation is examined in relation with characteristic phenomena such as hallucination and the distinction between statistical plausibility and truth. As a complementary illustration of the same viewpoint, we also discuss diffusion models, built around the score function, which cast generation not as sequential token prediction but as the simulation of a reverse-time stochastic process transforming noise into data both in discrete and continuous time.

</details>


### [193] [Stable Unsupervised Continual Chunking with Sheaf SyncMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25143)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"束正则化"（sheaf regularization）以降低 Decentralized SyncMap 中的局部不一致性，从而稳定其分块动力学；实验证明该方法在多数概率 CGCP 图上取得最高 NMI，且能适应分布偏移、避免负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督连续分块（unsupervised continual chunking）旨在识别时序序列中频繁共现的状态组，其核心挑战在于既要形成准确的分块，又要在时间演化中保持分块的稳定性。本文针对 Decentralized SyncMap 这一自组织系统，试图解决其分块动力学不稳定、局部不一致的问题。

Method: 提出束正则化（sheaf regularization）以减少 Decentralized SyncMap 中的局部不一致性，并引入"径向束结构"（radial sheaf structure），对变量对之间依赖距离的径向运动施加惩罚。

Result: 在 18 个二状态记忆的概率 CGCP 图中有 12 个、在 18 个动态记忆图中有 17 个，该方法在所评估的各 SyncMap 变体中取得最高归一化互信息（NMI）；在连续适应实验中，Sheaf SyncMap 在输入分布发生偏移后仍保持高 NMI。

Conclusion: 束正则化可有效稳定 SyncMap 的分块动力学并提升分块质量；Sheaf SyncMap 能在适应新知识的同时避免神经网络等现代机器学习系统常见的负迁移问题。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual chunking is a fundamental problem in machine learning and neuroscience, where the goal is to identify groups of states that frequently co-occur in temporal sequences. A key challenge is to form accurate chunks while maintaining their stability over time. In this work, we propose sheaf regularization to reduce local inconsistencies in Decentralized SyncMap, a self-organizing system, and thereby stabilize its chunking dynamics. We introduce a radial sheaf structure that penalizes distance-dependent radial motion between pairs of variables. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the highest normalized mutual information (NMI) among the evaluated SyncMap variants on 12 of 18 probabilistic Continual General Chunking Problem (CGCP) graphs with two-state memory and on 17 of 18 graphs with dynamic memory. In the sequential adaptation experiment, Sheaf SyncMap also achieves high NMI after shifts in the input distribution, indicating that it can adapt to new knowledge while avoiding the negative transfer commonly observed in modern machine learning systems such as neural networks.

</details>


### [194] [Brain-Inspired Hierarchical Modularity for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25146)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Qi Cheng,Weiyi Dong,Chunyan Lan,Guanglong Sun,Jun Zhou,Qian Li,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇学习记忆系统启发，本文提出分层模块化原则，通过专家特化与集成整合，以轻量模块化适配预训练基础模型，实现在线、不确定数据流下的持续学习，在多个任务上显著优于无回放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习通常基于离线、任务式训练且任务边界明确，与在线、不确定、动态演化的数据流下的通用持续学习存在较大差距。在该场景中，智能系统需分离冲突经验以减少干扰，同时整合兼容经验以促进泛化。

Method: 借鉴果蝇学习记忆系统的组织机制，提出分层模块化原则，通过专家特化与集成整合协调上述两种功能；将其具体化为预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，结合脑启发的随机扩展进行专家路由，并在空间与时间尺度上进行多样化模块集成。

Result: 在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、自我-外部视角视频理解以及具身视觉-语言-动作学习等任务中，该方法在在线且不确定的数据流下持续提升学习效果，在具身操作任务上相较无回放替代方案增益超过50个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明分层模块化是一种有生物学依据的、从动态经验中学习的有效路径。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability to learn from sequential experience while retaining and adapting prior knowledge, is central to intelligent systems operating in changing environments. However, conventional continual learning is typically studied with offline task-wise training and clear task boundaries, leaving a substantial gap from general continual learning under online, uncertain, and evolving data streams. In this regime, intelligent systems must separate conflicting experience to reduce interference while integrating compatible experience to promote generalization. Inspired by the organization of the Drosophila learning and memory system, we identify a hierarchical modular principle that coordinates both functions through expert specialization and ensemble integration. We instantiate this principle as lightweight modular adaptation of pretrained foundation models, combining brain-inspired random expansion for expert routing and diversified modular integration across spatial and temporal scales. Across visual recognition, vision-language understanding, ego-exo video understanding, and embodied vision-language-action learning, our method consistently improves learning under online and uncertain data streams, with gains exceeding 50 percentage points over replay-free alternatives in embodied manipulation. These findings support hierarchical modularity as a biologically grounded path for learning from dynamic experience.

</details>


### [195] [Dual-GNN Multilevel Coarsening for Maximum Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25149)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于学习的图边稀疏化方法GES，面向欧氏TSP自适应生成稀疏图，显著缩减图规模并加速精确求解，在保持极低解间隙的同时实现高剪枝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TSP精确求解计算代价高昂，图稀疏化可提升效率；但传统稀疏化依赖固定启发式，无法充分利用实例特有的结构信息。

Method: 提出Graph Edge Sparsification（GES），一种面向欧氏TSP的学习型稀疏化方法，融合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，针对不同实例自适应生成稀疏化图。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪除高达95%的边，解间隙保持在最优值1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上泛化性强，部分大规模实例剪枝率超过99%，最优性间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES通过自适应学习稀疏化有效缩减图规模、加速求解并保持近优解质量，具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95\% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1\% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99\%, while the optimality gap remains below 1\%.

</details>


### [196] [Exposing Blind Spots in Deep Imbalanced Regression Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25152)
*Noah C. Puetz,Jens U. Brandt,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视深度不平衡回归（DIR）的评测体系，指出其存在数据域单一（以图像为主）、many-/medium-/few-shot 协议非决策完备、尾部区域跨随机种子稳定性缺乏系统评估三大盲点。作者在多模态虚拟传感基准 MuViS（6 个物理域、9 个时间序列回归任务）上评测 DIR，采用 balanced MAE 并新提出尺度归一化的 balanced MASE，并对 6 种代表性 DIR 方法进行多种子重复评测，发现尾部区域对种子级波动高度敏感，是当前评测实践下被隐藏的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度不平衡回归（DIR）针对目标分布高度非均匀、模型在密集目标区域表现最好而全目标范围可靠性不足的问题。尽管方法进展迅速，DIR 评测仍受三大盲点限制：以图像基准为主、标准 many-/medium-/few-shot 协议仅具诊断性而非决策完备、尾部区域跨随机种子稳定性未被系统评估。作者旨在沿这三条轴线重审 DIR 评测。

Method: 1）拓宽数据域：在多模态虚拟传感基准 MuViS 上评测 DIR，涵盖 6 个物理域的 9 个时间序列外延回归任务，其中稀有目标值常对应具有实际意义的运行工况。2）指标：采用 balanced MAE（bMAE），并新提出 balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error（bMASE），作为尺度归一化指标以实现跨方法与跨数据集的决策完备比较。3）稳定性评估：对 6 种代表性 DIR 方法在多个随机种子上进行重复重评测，系统考察尾部区域的种子级变异性。

Result: 标准虚拟传感模型在全局 MAE 掩盖下存在显著尾部性能退化；现有 DIR 方法能提升平衡性能，但向多模态时间序列数据的迁移并不均衡；尾部区域不稳定性仍是当前 DIR 评测实践下被大幅隐藏的失效模式。

Conclusion: 作者从数据域、评价指标、稳定性三个维度重构了 DIR 评测框架，并提出 bMASE 指标。研究表明稀有目标区域的可靠性问题在多模态时间序列场景中同样突出且易被忽略，相关发现与公开代码为未来 DIR 研究提供了可复现基础，推动回归系统对稀有目标工况与常见工况同样可靠。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) addresses a common failure mode of regression models: target distributions are highly non-uniform, causing models to perform best in densely populated target regions even when reliable performance is required across the full target range. Despite rapid methodological progress, DIR evaluation remains constrained by three blind spots: it is dominated by image-based benchmarks, its standard many-/medium-/few-shot protocol is diagnostic but not decision-complete, and tail-region stability across random seeds has not been systematically evaluated. We revisit DIR evaluation along these three axes. First, we broaden the data domain by evaluating DIR on a multimodal virtual sensing benchmark (\textsc{MuViS}) with nine time-series extrinsic regression tasks across six physical domains, where rare target values often correspond to operationally meaningful regimes. Second, we adopt balanced MAE (\emph{bMAE}) and introduce balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error (\emph{bMASE}), a scale-normalized metric for decision-complete comparison across methods and datasets. Third, through a repeated reevaluation of six representative DIR methods across multiple random seeds, we show that the tail regions targeted by DIR exhibit particularly high sensitivity to seed-level variability. Our results show that standard virtual-sensing models exhibit substantial tail degradation hidden by global MAE, that existing DIR methods can improve balanced performance but transfer unevenly to multimodal time-series data, and that tail-region instability remains a largely hidden failure mode under current DIR evaluation practice. Together, these findings and our publicly available code provide a reproducible basis for future DIR research toward regression systems that capture rare target regimes as reliably as common ones.

</details>


### [197] [Learning Neural Feedback Linearization for Data-driven Systems via Augmented Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25163)
*Lakshmi Priya P. K.,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的反馈线性化控制框架，用神经网络李导数替代传统控制器组件，并在有界辨识误差下建立闭环实用稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反馈线性化依赖精确的系统模型与解析李导数，难以在模型未知或难以建模的场景下应用，因此需要一种完全数据驱动的实现方式，同时保证闭环稳定性。

Method: 在设计并训练反馈线性化控制器时，将相对度相关条件显式嵌入学习过程；以神经李导数替换常规反馈控制器的各组成部分，构建端到端的数据驱动反馈线性化框架；并推导使有界辨识误差导致有界跟踪误差的充分条件。

Result: 给出了闭环实用稳定性的理论保证，即辨识误差有界时跟踪误差有界；并将理论结果应用于电枢控制直流电机的案例进行验证。

Conclusion: 将相对度约束融入学习过程可实现完全数据驱动的反馈线性化，并在辨识误差有界条件下保证实用闭环稳定性，仿真/实例验证支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel data-driven framework for designing and training a feedback linearizing controller by explicitly incorporating relative degree based conditions into the learning process. This enables the conventional feedback controller components to be replaced by neural Lie derivatives, thereby facilitating a fully data-driven feedback linearization framework. Furthermore, practical closed-loop stability is established by deriving sufficient conditions under which bounded identification errors lead to bounded tracking errors. The derived theoretical results are validated through their application to an armature controlled DC motor.

</details>


### [198] [Mitigating Sequential Reappearance in Diffusion Data-Point Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25166)
*Donghyun Kim,Taehyuk Lee,Jinyeong Kim,Youngmin Oh,Dohyeong Kim,Jaehyuk Ryu,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文识别了扩散模型数据点遗忘中的"顺序重现"失效模式，并提出目标级评估协议与几何信号分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散数据点遗忘评估通常在每次删除后立即衡量，忽略了后续请求会反复更新同一模型的情形，可能掩盖遗忘效果的长期不稳定性。

Method: 提出目标级评估协议，追踪每个目标在序列中的状态：立即被遗忘、序列结束时仍被遗忘、或在后续删除过程中重现；并分析删除后目标的局部去噪损失几何。

Result: 发现被初始判定为遗忘的实例可能在后续删除中重新回到记忆状态，且重现目标在删除后表现出比持续遗忘目标更尖锐的局部去噪损失几何。

Conclusion: 顺序删除场景下遗忘评估需考虑长期稳定性；局部去噪损失几何可作为预测重现风险的潜在信号。

Abstract: Diffusion data-point unlearning is typically evaluated immediately after each deletion, even though subsequent requests may repeatedly update the same model. We identify sequential reappearance, a failure mode in which an instance that is initially judged to be forgotten later returns to the memorized regime without reuse of the deleted data or adversarial fine-tuning. To capture this behavior, we introduce a target-level evaluation protocol that tracks whether each target is forgotten immediately, remains forgotten at the end of the sequence, or reappears during subsequent deletions. We further find that targets that later reappear exhibit sharper local denoising-loss geometry after deletion than targets that remain forgotten.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning for Enhanced Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25179)
*Ya Song,Laurens Bliek,Yaoxin Wu,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MTFGN-SRL框架，利用FGN在频域捕获时空信息，结合多回看窗口与样本关系学习，提升RUL预测精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ST-GNN用于RUL预测存在三大不足：需领域知识或大量算力预构建图结构；时间依赖受限于固定长度回看窗口，忽略时间序列长度变化导致遗漏短/长期依赖；未能捕捉相邻时间窗样本间的内在关系，影响精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 将样本视为单一完整图而非图序列，采用傅里叶图神经网络(FGN)在频域捕获时空信息；设计多期学习模块，用多个回看窗口生成不同期样本输入FGN以增强信息提取；构建样本关系学习模块，训练异构图神经网络识别样本间关系。

Result: 在CMAPSS数据集上的评估表明，MTFGN-SRL在RUL预测任务中优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MTFGN-SRL通过频域时空建模、多期回看与样本关系学习，有效缓解现有ST-GNN的图结构依赖、固定窗口限制及样本关系缺失问题，实现更准确、鲁棒的RUL预测。

Abstract: Predicting the remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for effective predictive maintenance. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ST-GNNs), which can model both temporal and spatial relationships by representing time series data as a sequence of graphs, have shown exceptional performance in RUL prediction. However, current ST-GNNs face several drawbacks. First, they require domain expertise or significant computational power to establish graph structures prior to deploying GNNs. Second, the models are restricted to capture temporal dependencies within a predefined fixed-size lookback window. This restriction ignores the common issue of varying time series lengths, leading the prediction model to miss short-term or long-term dependencies. Finally, conventional models often fail to capture the inherent relationships between samples generated from adjacent time windows, which are crucial for improving both the accuracy and robustness of predictions. To address the aforementioned issues, we introduce a novel framework called Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning (MTFGN-SRL). Rather than treating the sample as a sequence of graphs, we consider it as a single complete graph and utilize a Fourier Graph Neural Network (FGN) to capture the spatio-temporal information in the frequency domain. We propose a multi-term learning module that utilizes multiple lookback windows to generate samples with varying terms, which are then fed into the FGN to enhance the extraction of useful information from the data. Finally, we develop a sample relationship learning module by training a heterogeneous GNN to identify inter-sample relationships, resulting in enhanced accuracy and robustness in predictions. Evaluations on the CMAPSS dataset demonstrate MTFGN-SRL's superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in RUL prediction.

</details>


### [200] [Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25237)
*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"后训练即服务"（PTaaS）场景，将LLM智能体置于前向部署工程师位置，在受治理交付平面上评估其可信交付能力，而非单纯看指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法回答：智能体能否被信任去完成端到端交付。核心痛点是"训练但未学习"（TBDL）的沉默失败——损失下降、信号全绿，但交付模型不比基座更好。

Method: 构建受治理交付平面：智能体驱动十个阶段，神谕（oracle）依据平台记录事实对每阶段打分；设计操作者运行的验收门在付款前拦截TBDL运行；用已知损坏运行校准检测器，在运行中标记严重损坏。四类前沿智能体在L40S/A100/H200上对8B–70B开放基座端到端运行，并设人类FDE对照臂。

Result: 验收门在付款前捕获所有TBDL运行；校准检测器可在运行中标记严重损坏；所有场景在评分前完成认证；实现了智能体与人类FDE在同一神谕下的对比。

Conclusion: 可信交付应作为PTaaS下智能体评估的核心维度，TBDL等沉默失败需通过平台事实级神谕与验收门机制加以治理。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [201] [Correcting Within-Group Self-Selection Bias in Prioritized Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25297)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示优先经验回放(PER)在随机环境中存在"组内自选择"偏差问题，并提出了三种保持组级优先权重的兄�弟样本感知修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PER通过回放高优先级的转移来提升样本效率，通常依据绝对时序差分误差。但在随机环境中，PER会扭曲来自相同状态-动作对的转移所对应的实际结果分布，即"组内自选择"问题，导致回放的结果频率与真实频率不符。

Method: 作者将PER分解为组间分配（between-group allocation）和条件兄弟选择（conditional sibling selection），并推导了保持当前组级优先级质量的固定缓冲区修正方法：SAMPLE（通过PER选组，在组内均匀采样兄弟转移训练）、AVG（对兄弟Bellman目标取平均）、MODEL（从经验全结果模型中采样）。

Result: 在存在稀有高幅值结果的精确状态-动作环境中，兄弟感知回放比PER提升了学习效率，尽管匹配的参数扫描显示调参可缩小部分差距。在MinAtar中，使用近似VQ-VAE分组配合SAMPLE，在五个游戏中的四个缓解了均值保持奖励尾部下的性能退化。

Conclusion: 兄弟感知回放既保留了PER对高优先级状态-动作区域的关注，又恢复了这些区域的实证结果频率，从而在随机环境中实现更高效的学习。

Abstract: Prioritized experience replay (PER) improves sample efficiency by replaying high-priority transitions, usually according to absolute temporal-difference error. In stochastic environments, PER can distort the distribution of realized outcomes replayed from transitions with the same state-action pair. We call this within-group self-selection. We quantify the resulting changes in within-group outcome frequencies and mean Bellman targets. We decompose PER into between-group allocation and conditional sibling selection, and derive fixed-buffer corrections that preserve current group-level priority mass: SAMPLE selects a group through PER and trains on a uniformly sampled sibling; AVG averages sibling Bellman targets; and MODEL samples from an empirical full-outcome model. In exact state-action environments with rare high-magnitude outcomes, sibling-aware replay improves learning efficiency over PER, although matched parameter sweeps show that tuning can narrow some gaps. In MinAtar, approximate VQ-VAE groups with SAMPLE mitigate degradation under mean-preserving reward tails in four of five games. Sibling-aware replay thus retains the focus on high-priority state-action regions while recovering their empirical outcome frequencies.

</details>


### [202] [Topological Signal Processing With Unoriented Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25310)
*Andrea Cavallo,Varun Sarathchandran,Geert Leus,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无向拓扑信号处理（UTSP）框架，用无向关联矩阵替代有向边界算子，证明其谱性质、提出交互阶分解并导出阶感知正则化，实验优于有向基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向拓扑信号处理（TSP）适用于流信号或需拓扑不变量的任务，但许多高阶信号本身无方向，强行使用有向边界算子会引入单形方向的任意选择，导致定义不严谨。

Method: 以无向关联矩阵替代有向边界算子，构建UTSP框架；证明任意单纯形层级间的无向关联矩阵与拉普拉斯矩阵具备类图谱性质；由于失去Hodge分解，提出"交互阶分解"来量化高阶信号由低阶信号聚合解释的程度；基于该分解推导可分别惩罚各交互阶的正则化项用于信号重建。

Result: 在真实数据上的实验表明，阶感知正则化优于有向基线方法，且当信号能量在各阶间分布不均时增益最大。

Conclusion: 无向TSP为无方向高阶信号提供了自洽的谱与分解理论，交互阶分解及对应正则化在信号重建中有效，尤其适合能量分布不均的场景。

Abstract: Topological signal processing (TSP) processes signals on simplicial complexes with oriented boundary operators, which is the natural choice for flow signals or when the topological invariants play a role for the task at hand. However, many higher-order signals carry no orientation, and applying oriented operators to them is not well-defined since it introduces an arbitrary choice of simplex orientation. We study an unoriented TSP (UTSP) framework that replaces oriented boundaries with unoriented incidence matrices. First, we show that unoriented incidence and Laplacian matrices between arbitrary simplicial levels admit graph-like spectral properties. Second, since dropping orientation removes the Hodge decomposition, we introduce an unoriented counterpart, termed interaction-order decomposition, which quantifies how much of a higher-order signal is explained by aggregating lower-order signals. Third, we use this decomposition to derive regularizers for signal reconstruction that penalize each interaction order separately. Experiments on real-world data show that the order-aware regularizers outperform oriented baselines, with the largest gains when the signal energy is unevenly distributed across orders.

</details>


### [203] [Spatiotemporal Kronecker Covariance Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25326)
*Andrea Cavallo,Athanasios Georgoutsos,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kronecker协方差神经网络（KVNN），一种基于Kronecker积分解的时空图神经网络，可解耦空间与时间依赖，具备谱分析保证和有限样本稳定性，在五个真实数据集上以更少参数实现强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列包含复杂的时空模式。基于协方差的ST-PCA等工具虽能识别这些模式，但仅限线性操作且在数据有限时易受估计误差影响。近期基于协方差的时空神经网络虽提供更稳定的非线性替代方案，但忽略了不同时间步之间的相关性。

Method: 提出Kronecker协方差神经网络（KVNN），一种时序图神经网络。其将时空协方差矩阵表示为Kronecker积之和，从而解耦空间与时间依赖；通过对空间和时间分量分别实施滤波操作，实现具有表达力的处理能力。

Result: KVNN具备严格的谱分析理论，并被证明对有限样本估计误差具有稳定性。在五个真实数据集上，KVNN取得强预测性能，且所需可训练参数常显著少于竞争方法，在估计噪声下表现一致。

Conclusion: KVNN通过Kronecker积解耦时空依赖并实施分量滤波，克服了ST-PCA的全部局限，在预测性能、参数效率与噪声鲁棒性方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Multivariate time series contain complex patterns that span across both space and time. While covariance-based statistical tools like spatiotemporal Principal Component Analysis (ST-PCA) help identify these patterns, they are limited to linear operations and prone to estimation errors with limited data. Recent covariance-based spatiotemporal neural networks offer more stable, non-linear alternatives, but they ignore correlations across different time steps. To solve this, we introduce the Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN), a temporal graph neural network that represents the spatiotemporal covariance matrix via a sum of Kronecker products where spatial and temporal dependencies are decoupled. By implementing filtering operations on spatial and temporal components, KVNNs achieve expressive processing capabilities, admit a rigorous spectral analysis, and are provably stable to finite-sample estimation errors, ultimately addressing all of ST-PCA's limitations. We show on five real-world datasets that KVNNs achieve strong forecasting performance, often requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters than competitive methods, and are consistent under estimation noise.

</details>


### [204] [MT-ProtBERT: Multi-task Learning ProtBERT for Intrinsically Disordered Proteins Classification with Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25334)
*Jian Sun,Kingshuk Ghosh,Lilianna Houston,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-ProtBERT，一种面向低数据场景的多任务ProtBERT扩展，通过动态窗口掩码、多尺度1D卷积分类器及生物化学辅助任务联合优化，在磷酸化位点预测与蛋白质压缩性预测任务上均优于IDP专用模型PARROT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白（IDP）具有动态构象、缺乏稳定三维结构且序列相似性低，导致构象异质性高，传统实验方法难以解析其结构；由此造成数据稀缺，使得对相似或相异IDP的分类/检测困难，而该任务对理解生物学与进化具有重要意义。

Method: 提出Multi-task ProtBERT（MT-ProtBERT），即在ProtBERT基础上的多任务扩展。其整合了三项关键设计：动态窗口掩码（Dynamic Window Masking）、多尺度1D卷积分类器（MS-Conv1D），以及联合优化掩码语言建模与生物化学启发式任务的辅助目标。

Result: 在两类低数据任务上进行评估：(i) 短序列和小数据集上的磷酸化位点（S/T/Y）预测；(ii) 两个小数据集（684与530条序列，含长度接近典型无序区的序列）上的蛋白质压缩性预测。MT-ProtBERT在所有任务上均稳定优于基于RNN的IDP专用模型PARROT。

Conclusion: 将自监督任务与生物化学启发式任务相结合，并引入多尺度学习，能够在数据稀缺条件下实现对无序蛋白的稳健建模。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) differ from folded proteins in that they are dynamic, lack a stable three-dimensional conformation, and have low sequence similarity between similar proteins. The conformational heterogeneity of IDPs - while beneficial for their diverse functions - limits the use of traditional experimental tools to determine their conformation. The experimental difficulty, along with low sequence similarity, results in data scarcity, and makes it difficult to classify/detect IDPs that are similar or dissimilar, a task relevant to understand biology and evolution. We address this challenge using Multi-task ProtBERT (MT-ProtBERT), a multi-task extension of ProtBERT tailored for low-data regimes. MT-ProtBERT integrates Dynamic Window Masking, a Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier (MS-Conv1D), and auxiliary objectives that jointly optimize masked language modeling and biochemistry-informed tasks. We evaluate this framework on two tasks under limited data: (i) phosphorylation site prediction (S/T/Y) in short sequences and small datasets, and (ii) protein compaction prediction on two small datasets (684 and 530 sequences), including sequences comparable in length to typical disordered regions. MT-ProtBERT consistently outperforms PARROT, an RNN-based IDP-specific model, across all tasks. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised and biochemistry-informed tasks, and multi-scale learning enables robust modeling of unstructured proteins under data scarcity.

</details>


### [205] [Concept Drift from a Causal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25340)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Jean Paul Barddal,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从结构因果模型(SCM)视角重新审视概念漂移，提出按因果来源分类的漂移taxonomy，并构建受控数据流生成器，实验表明不同因果来源的漂移会产生不同分布与预测模式，融合因果发现可构建更真实的评估场景并提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念漂移定义多将漂移刻画为联合分布P(x,y)的变化，未区分数据生成过程中究竟是哪个成分发生了改变，导致对漂移机制的理解与自适应学习方法的评估缺乏因果层面的可解释性和针对性。

Method: 基于结构因果模型(SCM)提出概念漂移的因果视角与分类体系，将漂移事件按因果来源划分为外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混淆因子变化和目标生成过程变化；据此开发SCM-based数据流生成器以模拟受控的机制级漂移，并结合因果发现方法构建基于真实依赖结构的数据流。

Result: 实验刻画了各类漂移的分布效应，发现不同因果来源的漂移会诱发不同的分布偏移模式与预测行为；结合因果发现生成的数据流能提供更真实、更有信息量的评估场景，且利用生成数据可提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 研究非平稳环境与自适应学习方法时考虑因果结构至关重要，本文为因果感知的评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Concept drift is a common phenomenon in real-world data streams, in which changes in the data-generating distribution can degrade predictive model performance. Most existing definitions characterize drift as changes in the joint distribution $P(\mathbf{x}, y)$, without distinguishing which component of the data-generating process has changed. In this work, we introduce a causal perspective on concept drift based on Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We propose a taxonomy that categorizes drift events by their causal origin, including changes in exogenous variables, endogenous mechanisms, confounders, and target-generating processes. Building on this framework, we develop an SCM-based data stream generator that simulates controlled mechanism-level drift events. Our experiments empirically characterize the distributional effects of each drift type and show that drifts with different causal origins induce distinct patterns of distribution shift and predictive behavior. Furthermore, by integrating causal discovery methods, we use our framework to construct data streams grounded in real-world dependency structures, enabling more realistic and informative evaluation scenarios. We also demonstrate that leveraging the generated data can improve downstream performance. These results highlight the importance of accounting for causal structure when studying and evaluating adaptive learning methods, and establish a foundation for causally-aware evaluation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [206] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Ali Rezagholizadeh,Soheila Samiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将FunctionGemma 270M-it扩展至Android设备控制场景，构建约9,500条、覆盖15类设备控制对话的合成数据集MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED，通过监督微调使端到端准确率从29.3%（基座）和17.2%（Google Mobile-Actions变体）提升至76.5%，并发布数据集、模型与复现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧助手需要将自然语言映射为本地系统动作的函数调用模型，但现有资源多聚焦Web API或狭窄的移动动作目录，缺乏覆盖实际Android设备控制工作流的资源，因此需要扩展至实用的Android场景。

Method: 将FunctionGemma 270M-it扩展到实际Android工作流；构建合成、经schema校验的数据集MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED（约9,500条对话，覆盖15类设备控制，包括消息、电话、相机/截图、亮度控制、设备状态查询、手电筒控制、应用管理等）；使用TRL监督微调并在completion-only loss下训练，得到扩展专家模型，以及与Google MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练的合并模型。

Result: 在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上，端到端准确率从基座模型的29.3%、Google Mobile-Actions变体的17.2%提升至76.5%。合并模型在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上保持76.5%，在MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE上达到82.3%（低于Google Mobile-Actions专家模型的90.3%），即以8.0个百分点的性能折衷换取类别覆盖翻倍。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、微调模型、可复现的训练/评估流程及Android演示，表明紧凑的本地函数调用是通向低延迟、隐私保护移动助手的可行路径。

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [207] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端、与规模无关的图强化学习框架求解一维装箱问题（1D-BPP），将装箱建模为物品兼容图上的MDP，用GNN actor-critic训练并以随机束搜索解码，实现零样本泛化到任意规模实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1D-BPP是经典NP难组合优化问题，应用广泛，但现有学习型装箱方法多集中于2D/3D变体，针对1D-BPP的智能学习求解器仍很稀缺。

Method: 将装箱过程形式化为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，作为结构化知识表示，每个动作合并两个可相容的部分箱；使用图神经网络actor-critic策略提取关系特征，通过强化学习训练，并以随机束搜索解码，使单一模型可零样本泛化到任意规模实例；同时系统研究图编码器、DRL算法、奖励函数、训练分布与超参数。

Result: 在BPPLIB基准上零样本评估，相比构造式启发式将平均最优性间隙从2.66%降至2.31%，结构化实例上提升最大；在九个族中的大多数较学习型基线间隙更低且跨分布更稳定；在最难的基准族上超过依赖列生成与整数规划的先进学习求解器，且不使用任何求解器。

Conclusion: 分组遗传算法整体仍领先，论文进一步分析了残余间隙的来源与原因；所提方法验证了图强化学习在1D-BPP上的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all.
