On OR-Bench, suppressing each model's recurring opening templates removes most of the score's association with the refusal proxy, while matched-volume deletion removes almost none and construct agreement stays at chance. Only three of eleven external contracts support the off-span control, and none of the routing studies we sampled released the generations it needs. We therefore ask that a proxy-based validation claim declare the span each label is read from, report the construct agreement beside the proxy agreement, and release the generations that let the proxy be re-read off the scored span.

</details>


### [242] [You Only Need 2/3 of the Chosen Experts: An Empirical Study of Dynamic Expert Pruning in Fine-Grained MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25809)
*Yuanteng Chen,Qiwei Lai,Chen Tianqi,Peisong Wang,Yuantian Shao,Nanxin Zeng,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对12个细粒度MoE模型进行系统实证研究，发现专家选择冗余度高：均匀保留约2/3专家即可保持98.8%性能并获得1.2-1.7倍加速；保守预算下动态分配收益有限，激进剪枝时最佳规则最多提升3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE架构（数百专家、每token选择更多专家）成为主流，动态专家剪枝被视为降低推理成本的途径。但现有证据多来自粗粒度架构和基于似然评分的多项选择基准，在细粒度场景下三个核心问题未解：per-token专家选择的冗余度、现有剪枝方法对该冗余的利用效率、以及模型对剪枝敏感性的决定因素。

Method: 对12个细粒度MoE检查点（涵盖9个架构家族）进行系统实证研究，使用11个基准的核心测试套件，覆盖知识问答、数学、代码生成和通用推理。对比均匀保留基线与现有动态分配规则，跨两个服务后端测量加速比。

Result: 专家选择冗余度远超领域假设：均匀保留约2/3的选中专家平均保留98.8%的未剪枝性能，仅需改动一个整数，在两个服务后端实现1.2-1.7倍实测加速。保守预算下动态分配空间极小，最佳已发布规则与均匀基线在匹配专家预算下差距不足1%。激进剪枝时价值显现，最佳规则相对均匀截断最多恢复3.0%，增益集中在退化最严重的生成任务。敏感性取决于模型：更大和思考型模型更鲁棒，多模态模型更脆弱。

Conclusion: 揭示了细粒度MoE可舍弃的专家计算量，明确了动态分配何时值得其复杂度，为实际部署和未来剪枝方法提供指导。

Abstract: Fine-grained mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures have become a mainstream design for open-weight LLMs, with hundreds of experts and increasingly many selected per token. This shift makes dynamic expert pruning an attractive route to cheaper inference. Yet existing evidence comes largely from coarser architectures and likelihood-scored multiple-choice benchmarks, leaving three central questions open in the fine-grained regime: how redundant per-token expert selection is, how effectively existing pruning methods exploit that redundancy, and what governs a model's sensitivity to pruning. We fill this gap with a systematic empirical study of twelve fine-grained MoE checkpoints spanning nine architecture families, with a core suite of eleven benchmarks covering knowledge QA, mathematics, code generation, and general reasoning. We find that expert selection is far more redundant than the field's operating points assume: uniformly retaining about two thirds of the selected experts preserves 98.8% of unpruned performance on average, requiring only a one-integer change and delivering 1.2-1.7x measured speedup across two serving backends. This simple baseline leaves little room for dynamic allocation at conservative budgets: even the best published rules differ from it by under 1% at matched expert budgets. Their value emerges under aggressive pruning, where the best rules recover up to 3.0% over uniform truncation, with gains concentrated in the generative tasks that suffer the sharpest degradation. Sensitivity to aggressive pruning also depends on the model: larger and thinking models are more resilient, whereas multimodal models are more vulnerable. Together, these findings reveal how much expert computation fine-grained MoEs can dispense with, and establish when dynamic allocation earns its complexity, informing both practical deployment and future pruning methods.

</details>


### [243] [Multi-View Fair Clustering Guided by Cross-View Sensitive Information Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25811)
*Mudi Jiang,Jiahui Zhou,Xinying Liu,Zengyou He,Zhikui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由跨视角敏感信息差异引导的多视角公平聚类框架，通过估计各视角敏感依赖度并设计偏见排序的非对称对齐机制，在聚类质量与群体公平性之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角聚类（MVC）虽能利用多视角互补信息提升聚类性能，但在社会敏感场景中公平性至关重要。现有多视角公平聚类方法将各视角置于统一的公平机制下，未区分不同视角在跨视角学习中敏感依赖程度的差异。实际中不同视角编码的敏感信息程度差异较大，若忽视这种跨视角差异，高敏感依赖视角会在跨视角学习中影响低敏感依赖视角，从而同时损害聚类性能与公平性。

Method: 1. 估计每个视角的敏感依赖程度；2. 设计偏见排序的非对称对齐机制，鼓励高敏感依赖视角向低敏感依赖视角学习；3. 利用跨视角差异自适应调节对齐过程；4. 在共识软分配上施加公平性正则化，以进一步促进群体公平。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在聚类质量与群体公平性之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并利用跨视角敏感信息差异来引导对齐，为多视角公平聚类提供了新思路，能够在保证聚类性能的同时提升群体公平性。

Abstract: Multi-view clustering (MVC) aims to uncover latent cluster structures by exploiting complementary information from multiple views. Despite substantial progress in clustering performance, fairness remains an important concern when MVC is applied to socially sensitive scenarios. Recent fair multi-view clustering methods have introduced fairness constraints into representation learning or clustering assignments. However, these methods generally treat different views under a largely uniform fairness mechanism, without explicitly distinguishing their varying levels of sensitive dependence during cross-view learning. In practice, different views may encode substantially different levels of sensitive information. Ignoring such cross-view discrepancy can allow highly sensitive-dependent views to influence less sensitive-dependent ones during cross-view learning, potentially degrading both clustering performance and fairness. To address this issue, we propose a novel multi-view fair clustering framework guided by cross-view sensitive information discrepancy. Specifically, we estimate the sensitive dependence of each view and develop a bias-ranked asymmetric alignment mechanism that encourages views with higher sensitive dependence to learn from those with lower sensitive dependence, while cross-view discrepancies are further exploited to adaptively regulate the alignment process. Moreover, fairness regularization is imposed on the consensus soft assignments to further promote group fairness. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a favorable balance between clustering quality and group fairness.

</details>


### [244] [CacheDyG: Decoupling Temporal Propagation for Efficient Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25814)
*PinHeng Zong,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 缓存解耦框架，兼顾高效与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图神经网络通常把时序传播耦合进每个优化轮次，并为每个“节点-时刻”对维护大规模可训练表示，导致大量重复计算与参数、训练开销。

Method: CacheDyG采用缓存-精炼框架：用时序依赖缓存把时序传播与参数更新解耦，将图感知的节点-时刻表示存入不可训练缓冲区；常规训练仅更新轻量缓存精炼器、自适应残差门与链接预测器，并辅以选择性缓存刷新以保持表示与监督目标对齐。

Result: 在五个动态图基准上，CacheDyG以显著更少的可训练参数和更低运行时间，取得优于基线的预测性能。

Conclusion: 基于缓存的解耦为可扩展动态图学习提供了有效原则。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are widely used to model time-evolving relational systems in real-world applications. Dynamic graph neural networks provide an effective framework for capturing both structural dependencies and temporal dynamics in such data. However, they typically intertwine temporal graph propagation with every optimization epoch and often maintain large trainable representations for each node-time pair. This design repeatedly recomputes largely unchanged historical structures, leading to substantial training and parameter overhead. To address this critical issue, we propose CacheDyG, a Cache-refine framework for efficient Dynamic Graph learning. Specifically, it decouples temporal propagation from routine parameter updates by constructing a time-ordered temporal dependency cache that stores graph-aware node-time representations in non-trainable buffers. During standard training epochs, CacheDyG reads from the cache and updates only a lightweight cache refiner, an adaptive residual gate, and the link predictor. Selective cache refresh further keeps cached representations aligned with the supervised objective while avoiding epoch-wise sparse propagation. Experiments on five dynamic graph benchmarks show that CacheDyG adopts substantially fewer trainable parameters and lower runtime to obtain more competitive predictive performance than baselines. These results demonstrate that cache-based decoupling provides an effective principle for scalable dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [245] [Protocol before progress: leakage-aware evaluation of AIS trajectory prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25827)
*Zobeir Raisi,Vali Mohammad Nazarzehi Had*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出AIS船舶轨迹预测的性能提升常被归因于新架构，但评估协议本身的影响未被量化；作者构建防泄漏协议（船舶/时间/区域不相交划分），在丹麦与美湾两套AIS数据集上审计TrAISformer、GATransformer及受AISFormer启发的编码器，发现协议效应常超过架构效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS轨迹预测文献普遍报告新架构带来的误差下降，但评估协议（数据划分方式）很少被作为误差来源加以度量，可能导致泄漏与虚高结果，缺乏公平可比基准。

Method: 构建泄漏感知（leakage-aware）评估协议，采用船舶、时间、区域三类不相交划分；在两个交通特征不同的语料上实验：31天丹麦全国AIS交通、30天美湾休斯顿与加尔维斯顿近海交通。审计TrAISformer、GATransformer，以及受AISFormer启发的受控重构模型（含仅0.43 M参数的紧凑编码器，基于局部偏移量）。

Result: 两类语料中均出现三种协议效应：(1) TrAISformer的best-of-16 oracle解码相对贪婪解码将误差降低2.1–3.2倍；(2) 共享船舶的划分使贪婪解码1小时误差降低23–25%，而0.43 M参数紧凑编码器仅≤2%；(3) 区域不相交划分使TrAISformer在美国语料的1小时误差由2.2 km升至24.6 km，因99.9%测试上下文落入训练未见经度区间，而基于局部偏移的编码器不受影响。架构机制影响较小：GATransformer图注意力在两类语料均无显著收益，其水道特征贡献12–22%；时间不相交划分效应跨语料不稳定（13% vs 2%）。

Conclusion: AIS轨迹预测中评估协议造成的误差差异常大于架构差异，须以船舶/时间/区域不相交划分作为标准防泄漏评估；作者公开划分与代码以促进可复现与公平比较。

Abstract: Reported gains in vessel-trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data are credited to new architectures, but the evaluation protocol is rarely measured as a source of error reduction. We build a leakage-aware protocol with vessel-, time- and region-disjoint splits and apply it to two corpora with different traffic: 31 days of Danish national AIS traffic and 30 days of US Gulf coast traffic off Houston and Galveston. On both, we audit TrAISformer, GATransformer, and controlled AISFormer-inspired reconstructions. Three protocol effects appear in both corpora. First, TrAISformer's best-of-16 oracle decoder lowers error by a factor of 2.1-3.2 relative to greedy decoding. Second, a split that shares vessels lowers its greedy error by 23-25% at one hour, against 2% or less for a compact 0.43 M-parameter encoder. Third, a region-disjoint split raises TrAISformer's one-hour error from 2.2 to 24.6 km on the US corpus, because 99.9% of the test contexts fall in longitude bins never seen in training; the encoder built on local offsets is unaffected by this. Architectural mechanisms matter less: GATransformer's graph attention gives no measurable benefit on either corpus, while its waterway feature is worth 12-22%. The effect of a time-disjoint split is not stable across corpora (13% versus 2%). We release the splits and code.

</details>


### [246] [In-Context Guidance: Learning Inter-Task Synergies via Numerical Foundational Models for Few-Shot Multitask Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25836)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Jiao Liu,Zhao Wei,Puay Siew Tan,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICG-MTO框架，利用冻结的数值基础模型通过上下文学习为多任务优化提供辅助引导，以改进少量评估预算下的任务间耦合估计，缓解负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化（MTO）需同时求解多个任务，但在评估预算有限时，任务间关系估计不准确，易导致负迁移。传统方法仅依赖稀缺的已观测数据，难以在早期数据匮乏阶段获得可靠的任务耦合估计。

Method: ICG-MTO包含三个阶段：(1) 由已评估解构建算法特定的上下文查询；(2) 用冻结的基础模型通过上下文学习推断刻画任务间预测关系的引导信号；(3) 将该信号转换为算法特定的引导，用于最大后验耦合估计。该方法在优化早期数据稀缺时提供正则化，随任务观测累积逐步放弃控制。作者在多任务贝叶斯优化中实例化为ICG-MTBO，使用方向性适应度类别查询引导任务间耦合估计；并在MFEA-II中实例化，使用决策空间重叠查询引导随机交配概率估计。

Result: 在合成基准和真实机械臂控制问题上的实验，以及在不同采集函数和进化多任务设置下的评估，均验证了ICG-MTO在少样本多任务优化中的有效性与通用性。

Conclusion: ICG-MTO通过基础模型上下文学习为少样本多任务优化提供任务间关系引导，可有效缓解负迁移，并具备跨算法、跨问题的通用性。

Abstract: Multi-task optimization (MTO) addresses a set of optimization tasks simultaneously, often suffering from inaccurate inter-task relationship estimation under limited evaluation budgets, leading to negative transfer. This paper introduces In-Context Guidance Multitask Optimization (ICG-MTO), a novel framework that leverages numerical foundational models to improve inter-task coupling estimation in few-shot scenarios. Unlike conventional methods that rely solely on scarce observed data, ICG-MTO employs a frozen foundational model to infer auxiliary guidance through in-context learning. The framework operates through three stages: constructing an algorithm-specific in-context query from evaluated solutions, using the foundational model to infer a guidance signal characterizing predictive relationships among tasks, and translating this signal into algorithm-specific guidance for maximum-a-posteriori coupling estimation. This approach provides regularization during the early, data-scarce stages of optimization and gradually relinquishes control as task-specific observations accumulate. We instantiate the framework in multitask Bayesian optimization as ICG-MTBO, using directional fitness-class queries to guide inter-task coupling estimation, and further instantiate it in MFEA-II using decision-space-overlap queries to guide random mating probability estimation. Experiments across synthetic benchmarks and a real-world robot arm control problem, together with evaluations under different acquisition functions and evolutionary multitasking, demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of ICG-MTO for few-shot multitask optimization.

</details>


### [247] [Gaussian Flow-Matching Schedules: Implications for Sampling and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25839)
*Arsène Claustre,Hugo Negrel,Claire Boyer,Kimia Nadjahi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对中心化且可交换的高斯分布，流匹配调度可分解为两个独立设计：决定中间分布与概率流的方差路径，以及不影响流但控制回归方差的分解方式。论文分析了有限步Euler采样的精度并给出精确N步采样的漂移下界，同时为任意固定路径推导出最小化时间平均回归方差或使其恒定的闭式分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中，调度(schedule)不仅影响采样动力学，还影响回归目标的方差，但二者的耦合关系缺乏清晰刻画。作者希望在特殊但可解析的设定下，厘清调度设计自由度，并分别从采样与训练两个角度给出理论指导。

Method: 以中心化可交换高斯为分析对象，将方向依赖的调度分解为方差路径与分解(factorization)两部分：前者完全决定中间律与概率流，后者保持流不变但改变回归方差。采样侧分析有限步Euler精度并推导精确N步采样的必要漂移界，将测地线与对数路径联系起来；训练侧对任意固定路径给出闭式分解。

Result: 证明了调度分解的独立性；得到有限步Euler精度分析及精确N步采样的必要漂移界，建立测地路径与对数路径的联系；对任意固定路径，导出两类闭式分解——一类最小化时间平均回归方差，另一类使回归方差沿路径保持恒定。

Conclusion: 流匹配调度设计中方差路径与因子分解可解耦：路径选择决定采样行为，而分解可独立优化训练目标。该结论为在高斯设定下同时改进采样精度与训练稳定性提供了理论依据与可操作的闭式方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching schedules affect both sampling dynamics and the variance of the regression target. For centered commuting Gaussians, we show that a direction-dependent schedule decomposes into two independent design choices: a variance path, which fully determines the intermediate laws and probability flow, and a factorization, which leaves this flow unchanged while controlling irreducible regression variance. On the sampling side, we analyze finite-step Euler accuracy and derive a necessary drift bound for exact N -step sampling, connecting the geodesic and the logarithmic path. On the training side, for any fixed path, we derive closed-form factorizations that either minimize time-averaged regression variance or make it constant along the path.

</details>


### [248] [Neural Approximation by Function Composition: Rigidity and Doubly Exponential Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25874)
*Wentao Huang,Haizhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究单标量生成函数迭代的几何加权和这一机制，证明分段线性生成元的刚性定理，并构造光滑生成元实现深度上的双指数误差衰减及多项式/幂级数逼近率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络通过仿射映射与非线性激活的复合获得逼近能力，但复合本身如何产生逼近力尚不完全清楚。作者聚焦一个基础机制：单标量生成函数迭代的几何加权和，该机制支撑了$x-x^2$的帐篷映射构造以及Yarotsky、W. E等人分析深度网络逼近力的递归表示。

Method: 1）对具有有限段数的连续分段线性生成元建立刚性定理，分析此类表示可产生的函数类及其几何因子下界；2）以精确余项恒等式为指导，构造光滑生成元，使其迭代在总深度上产生双指数误差衰减，并通过乘法模块推广到固定多项式；3）对$[-1,1]^d$上系数绝对可和的幂级数，按单项式次数分配深度，分析内部立方体上的一致逼近误差。

Result: 刚性定理表明：对有限段连续分段线性生成元，任何能以该方式表示的$C^3$函数至多为二次；对非仿射二次函数，几何因子至少为$1/4$。构造方面，光滑生成元的迭代对平方逼近及经乘法模块的每个固定多项式实现总深度上的双指数误差衰减；对系数绝对可和的幂级数，按单项式次数分配深度可在每个内部立方体上达到$O(e^{-cL^{1/d}})$的一致逼近误差。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了帐篷映射方法的局限，并与基于层次基和递归多项式构造的现有方法形成互补；同时说明生成元动力学与余项估计如何支配深度分配及深度网络的逼近速率。

Abstract: Deep neural networks approximate functions by composing affine maps with nonlinear activations, but how composition itself creates approximation power is not yet fully understood. We investigate a fundamental mechanism: geometrically weighted sums of iterates of a single scalar generator function. This mechanism underpins the classical tent-map construction of the function \(x - x^2\) and related recursive representations used by Yarotsky, W. E, et al., to analyze the approximation powers of deep neural networks.
  First, we establish a rigidity theorem: for continuous piecewise linear generators with a finite number of segments, any \(C^3\) function that can be represented in this way is at most quadratic. For non-affine quadratic functions, the geometric factor is at least $1/4$. This result both reveals limitations of the tent-map approach and complements existing methods based on hierarchical bases and recursive polynomial constructions. Second, using an exact remainder identity as guidance, we construct a smooth generator whose iterates yield doubly exponential error decay in total depth for square approximation and, through multiplication modules, for each fixed polynomial. For power series with absolutely summable coefficients on \([-1,1]^d\), distributing depth according to monomial degree yields a uniform approximation error of order \(O(e^{-cL^{1/d}})\) on each interior cube. These findings demonstrate how generator dynamics and remainder estimates govern depth allocation and approximation rates of deep neural networks.

</details>


### [249] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of SechKAN on 1D Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25876)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数、并采用1D投影压缩参数量的KAN变体SechKAN，在三个1D分类数据集上评估其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KART与神经网络设计的结合催生了KANs，已应用于STEM和AI任务。本文旨在探索一种参数量与MLP相当的KAN变体（SechKAN）的实际有效性。

Method: SechKAN以双曲正割函数为基函数，并通过1D投影将参数量降至与MLP相当的水平。在UCI HAR、ElectricDevices和Crop三个1D分类数据集上，与EfficientKAN、MLP、CNN1D、ResNet1D、DSCNN1D在近似参数量预算下进行对比，并开展消融实验。

Result: SechKAN在三个数据集上均取得有竞争力的性能，在Crop数据集上表现尤为突出。消融实验表明网格大小和归一化会影响性能。

Conclusion: SechKAN的有效性依赖于数据集与架构选择；代码与实验实现已公开。

Abstract: The connection between the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem (KART) and neural network design has led to the development of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), with applications ranging from STEM problems to AI tasks. In this paper, we investigate the effectiveness of a KAN variant, SechKAN, which relies on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions as basis functions, with a 1D projection to reduce the number of parameters to a level comparable to MLPs. We evaluate SechKAN on three 1D classification datasets: UCI Human Activity Recognition (UCI HAR), ElectricDevices, and Crop, and compare it with several effective networks, including EfficientKAN, MLP, CNN1D, ResNet1D, and DSCNN1D, using approximately comparable parameter budgets. The results indicate that SechKAN achieves competitive performance across the three datasets, with particularly strong performance on Crop. Ablation studies further show that grid size and normalization affect performance, suggesting that SechKAN's effectiveness depends on the dataset and architectural choices. Our source code and experimental implementation are publicly available at: https://github.com/hoangthangta/SechKAN_1D.

</details>


### [250] [AURA: Angular Update Rate Adaptation for training complex-valued neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25914)
*Enrico Ballini,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup,Tito Andriollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AURA（Angular Update Rate Adaptation），一种针对复数神经网络（CVNNs）的逐参数步长自适应方法，可叠加于任意一阶优化器之上且不改变其更新方向，通过度量相邻更新在长度、对齐和旋转方向上的"一致性"来动态调整步长。将其与Adam和Muon结合后，在四个复杂度递增的测试任务上进行验证，结果显示在多数情况下能改善基础优化器的收敛性，且每步开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数神经网络越来越多地用于处理复值数据，但仍沿用实数域的一阶优化器。这些方法的效率高度依赖步长，而现有步长规则忽略了复平面中可用的角度信息，未能充分利用复数参数的结构特性。

Method: 提出AURA（Angular Update Rate Adaptation），一种逐参数的步长自适应策略，可插入任意一阶优化器之上，也可移除，且不改变原优化器的更新方向。AURA度量每个复数参数相邻两次更新之间的一致性，包括长度、对齐程度和旋转方向：当更新一致时增大步长，不一致时减小步长。该方法无需额外的梯度计算，每步仅需廉价的向量运算。作者将AURA与Adam和Muon结合，在全连接神经网络上测试。

Result: 在四个复杂度递增的测试用例上（从标量复函数逼近到物理信息训练），将AURA+Adam、AURA+Muon与知名一阶优化器对比。除步长外所有超参数在各测试用例间保持固定；其中一个用例中还在相同预算下对各优化器的超参数进行了调优。实验表明AURA在多数情况下以很小的每步开销提升了基础优化器的收敛性，作者也识别出了AURA失效的条件。

Conclusion: AURA是一种通用、低开销的复数域步长自适应方法，能够即插即用地增强现有的一阶优化器，在多数情形下改善收敛性，但其有效性存在特定适用条件，并非在所有场景下都能带来提升。

Abstract: Complex-valued neural networks (CVNNs) are increasingly adopted for complex-valued data; however, they are often trained with first-order optimizers inherited from the real-valued case. The efficiency of these methods depends largely on the step size, and their step-size rules ignore the angular information available in the complex plane. We address step-size adaptation in the complex domain by introducing AURA (Angular Update Rate Adaptation), a per-parameter step-size adaptation that can be added on top of any first-order optimizer, and removed from it, without altering its update direction. AURA measures the agreement between consecutive updates of each complex parameter, in length, alignment, and sense of rotation, and enlarges the step when they are consistent and reduces it when they are not. It requires no additional gradient evaluations and only inexpensive vector operations per step. We combine AURA with Adam and Muon and compare the resulting methods with well-known first-order optimizers on four test cases of increasing complexity, ranging from the approximation of scalar complex functions to physics-informed training. Fully connected neural networks are used throughout this work. All hyperparameters other than the step size are held fixed across test cases; for one case, we also tune the hyperparameters of each optimizer under the same budget. Our empirical tests show that AURA improves the convergence of its base optimizer in most cases with a small per-step overhead, and we identify the conditions under which it fails to do so.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Scalar Sensitivity: Activation-Aware Mixed-Precision LLM Quantization with Cross-Layer Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25916)
*Akihiro Yoshida,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出混合精度权重量化中常用标量敏感度代理存在理论失真，并提出两阶段方法CASA，用激活感知的Kronecker分解Hessian度量替代标量代理，并结合跨层局部搜索，在超低比特下取得更优困惑度和零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度权重量化通常被建模为多选背包问题（MCKP），但现有求解器依赖标量敏感度代理，将每个权重矩阵的Hessian压缩为单一数值，并独立处理各模块。作者证明即使最优标量代理，相对于完整激活感知二次型也会产生最高达√(κ(A)κ(B))的乘性失真，该上界在典型LLM模块中从10^1到10^13变化，导致模块间敏感度排序不可靠。

Method: 提出Cross-layer Activation-aware Sensitivity Allocation（CASA）两阶段方法。Stage 1用基于Kronecker分解Hessian的激活感知度量替代标量代理，将MCKP化简为连续松弛具有闭式解的形式；Stage 2采用跨层感知的局部搜索，以端到端模型损失评估比特宽度更新。

Result: 在多个LLM和不同比特预算上的实验表明，CASA相比最新标量代理基线获得更低困惑度，尤其在超低比特（每权重<3比特）下优势明显。此外，零样本准确率的性能增益与各模型模块平均条件数相关，验证了失真上界作为标量代理失效的实际指标。

Conclusion: 标量敏感度代理在模块间敏感度排序上存在理论和实践缺陷；CASA通过激活感知的Kronecker分解度量与跨层局部搜索，有效提升混合精度量化性能，并以条件数作为预测标量代理失效的实用指标。

Abstract: Mixed-precision weight quantization is commonly formulated as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), yet existing solvers rely on scalar sensitivity proxies that collapse each weight matrix's Hessian into a single number and treat every module independently. We prove that even the optimal scalar proxy incurs multiplicative distortion up to $\sqrt{κ(\mathbf{A})κ(\mathbf{B})}$ relative to the full activation-aware quadratic, where $κ(\mathbf{A})$ and $κ(\mathbf{B})$ denote the condition numbers of the input- and output-side Hessian factors. This bound varies from $10^1$ to $10^{13}$ for typical LLM modules, making inter-module sensitivity ranking unreliable. To address these limitations, we propose Cross-layer Activation-aware Sensitivity Allocation (CASA), a two-phase method. In Stage 1, the scalar proxy is replaced by an activation-aware metric derived from the Kronecker-factored Hessian, reducing the MCKP to a form whose continuous relaxation admits a closed-form solution. In Stage 2, a cross-layer-aware local search evaluates bit-width updates using the end-to-end model loss. Experiments on multiple LLMs across different bit budgets show that CASA achieves lower perplexity than the latest scalar-proxy baselines, especially at ultra-low bit-widths ($<3$ bits per weight). Moreover, the performance gain in zero-shot accuracy tracks the per-model average condition-number over modules, confirming the distortion bound as a practical indicator of scalar-proxy failure.

</details>


### [252] [Certified Against Which Oracle? Execution Labels Set the Reported Risk of Conformal Abstention for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25938)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Spider-Realistic上基于一致性评分的conformal abstention certificate进行预注册干预，发现其可靠性受所依赖的正确性标签（oracle）支配：更换为多实例测试套件后，certificate的实际风险比其标签报告的风险高出2.73–10.23个百分点；专家盲标下名义0.10的certificate实际携带17.2–20.0个百分点风险，且16/16组合中一致性评分在其自身oracle下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到SQL的弃权证书（abstention certificate）由执行一致性推导置信度，而其正确性标签仅来自基准自带的单一数据库（shipped database），该oracle被指偏宽松。作者怀疑：证书的"真实性"实际上只是标签体系（oracle）的产物，而非模型真实语义正确性的度量，因此需要检验标签来源对风险报告、以及oracle对置信度评分评价的支配作用。

Method: 在Spider-Realistic上做预注册干预，将shipped database替换为基准蒸馏的多实例测试套件。使用四个SQL专用模型checkpoint与两种划分方案，比较certificate的held-out risk与其自身标签所报告风险的差异。引入两名SQL专家进行盲标与盲审（预注册），并辅以AI判定的拒绝样本普查（语义错误归因），同时比较在suite cluster与shipped-database cluster上构建执行一致性评分的AUROC及其专家置信区间。

Result: 替换oracle后，certificate的held-out risk比其标签自报风险高2.73–10.23个百分点；两个oracle均未复现专家判定的风险。专家盲标下，名义0.10的certificate在两个checkpoint上实际风险为20.0与17.2个百分点。更严格的oracle双向出错：其拒绝的答案多数并非错误，而接受的答案中有些确实错误；AI普查显示其拒绝样本中1/4到1/3存在语义错误，其余多归因于问题欠定义、合成实例或疑似的参考查询缺陷（经盲审专家审核支持）。评分评价亦被oracle决定：16/16组合中执行一致性评分在其自身构建集群的标签下表现更好；在专家标签下，用suite cluster替代shipped-database cluster构建评分，AUROC分别提升6.96与1.53个百分点，且后者上专家区间不包含suite标签报告的8.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 弃权证书必须同时报告两个oracle的结果；oracle之间的风险差异只有在基准被审计之后才能解读为语义风险。执行一致性评分应在其未参与构建的oracle下评估，否则其表现会被系统性高估。

Abstract: A conformal abstention certificate for text-to-SQL is only as truthful as the correctness labels it is calibrated on. The uncertainty pipelines that read confidence off execution consistency take those labels from the single database a benchmark ships, an oracle known to be lenient. We run a preregistered intervention on Spider-Realistic, swapping that database for the benchmark's distilled multi-instance test suite. Across four SQL-specialist checkpoints and two split schemes, the swap raises the certificate's held-out risk 2.73 to 10.23 points above the risk its own labels report. Neither oracle reports the risk experts assign. Under blinded labels from two SQL experts, a certificate calibrated at a nominal 0.10 carries 20.0 and 17.2 points of risk on two checkpoints. The stricter oracle errs in both directions: most of the answers it rejects are not judged wrong, and some of those it accepts are. An AI-assigned census of what it rejects finds a semantic error in a quarter to a third of them, depending on the population. It attributes most of the rest to underspecified questions, synthetic instances or suspected reference-query defects, a flag supported by a preregistered blinded expert audit. The oracle also decides how a confidence score is judged. Every execution-consistency score looks better under the labels of the oracle that built its clusters, in 16 of 16 combinations. Under expert labels, building such a score on suite clusters instead of shipped-database clusters raises its area under the ROC curve (AUROC) by 6.96 points on one checkpoint and 1.53 on the other. On the second, the expert interval excludes the 8.3 points the suite labels report. A certificate should be reported with both oracles, and an oracle-relative difference read as semantic risk only after the benchmark is audited. A consistency score should be evaluated under an oracle that did not build it.

</details>


### [253] [Exploring Solver-Level Warmstarting for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25962)
*Annelot Bosman,Minghao Liu,Marta Kwiatkowska,Holger Hoos,Jan van Rijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索在神经网络验证中采用求解器级热启动（warmstarting），利用先前求解信息，在扰动半径、输入数据和网络变化时显著缩短验证时间，并可解决无法从头求解的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证最坏情况下计算不可解，即使是常见对抗鲁棒性规范也是NP完全的，因此需要利用先前求解信息来提升验证效率。

Method: 应用求解器级热启动技术，设计具有通用性、可适配先进验证器的流水线，研究其在扰动半径、输入数据和网络本身变化时对运行时间的影响。

Result: 在大多数情况下，热启动可显著降低验证时间；且能成功验证在给定时间限制内无法从头求解的实例。

Conclusion: 求解器级热启动是提升神经网络验证效率的有效手段，具备通用性和推广潜力。

Abstract: Neural network verification has become a key tool for providing formal guarantees on the behaviour of neural networks. However, many verification problems remain computationally intractable in the worst case: even for common adversarial robustness specifications, verification is NP-complete. Here, we explore the application of solver-level warmstarting for neural network verification to exploit information from previous solutions. We study the effect on running time as several properties are modified, including perturbation radii, input data and the networks themselves, using a pipeline that is generalisable and potentially adaptable to state-of-the-art verifiers. Our results show that warmstarting can significantly reduce verification time in most cases. Moreover, warmstarting enables the successful verification of instances that could not be solved from scratch within the given time limit.

</details>


### [254] [GeoPair: Geometry-Preserving Cross-Layer Factorization for Training-Free Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25963)
*Baher Mohammad,Ammar Ali,Stamatios Lefkimmiatis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、可收敛的跨层权重配对与共享字典分解框架，替代启发式分组策略，结合结构化稀疏实现高效Transformer压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer存在跨层冗余，但现有后训练压缩流程要么逐层独立优化，要么依赖忽视各层激活几何特性的启发式分组策略，导致压缩效果受限。

Method: 训练无关的优化驱动框架：顺序优化跨层权重配对与共享字典分解，识别结构兼容的投影并学习共享表示，以更好保留每层独特的校准几何；结合结构化稀疏得到高效权重分解。

Result: 在多种架构、规模和模态上达到最优性能，持续优于独立结构化权重分解及采用启发式分组的成对权重分解方法。

Conclusion: 以收敛的优化驱动流水线取代启发式工程策略，为跨模态可扩展的Transformer压缩奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Transformer architectures exhibit cross-layer redundancies, yet post-training compression pipelines typically optimize layers in isolation or rely on heuristic grouping strategies that disregard layer-specific activation geometries. We introduce a principled, training-free framework that sequentially optimizes cross-layer weight pairings and shared-dictionary factorizations. Rather than forcing weights of adjacent layers to share a basis or heuristically merging activation statistics, our approach identifies structurally compatible projections and learns a shared representation that better preserves each layer's distinct calibration geometry. Coupled with structured sparsity, this yields highly efficient weight decompositions without sacrificing functional fidelity. Across diverse architectures, scales, and modalities, our method achieves state-of-the-art results, consistently outperforming independent structured weight decompositions and alternative pairwise weight factorizations, which operate under heuristic grouping strategies. By replacing heuristic engineering strategies with a convergent, optimization-driven pipeline, we establish a theoretically grounded foundation for scalable, transformer compression across different modalities.

</details>


### [255] [Interweaving Marginals into Multivariate Sample Paths: Training-Free Dependence Construction for Probabilistic Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25980)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Jinkwan Jang,Seul Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将冻结的时序基础模型（TSFM）逐坐标预测的边缘分布，通过免训练耦合重构为多变量未来轨迹的联合分布，并指出依赖关系重构应作为独立的后期处理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率型时序基础模型只给出逐坐标（coordinate-wise）的边缘预测分布，而这些边缘分布无法唯一确定多变量未来轨迹的联合分布。因此需要研究如何在不重新训练模型的前提下，把冻结模型的边缘分布耦合为合理的多变量样本路径。

Method: 采用免训练（training-free）的耦合方法，将冻结 TSFM 的边缘分布组合成多变量预测样本路径。核心实验设计是在所有通道—时间步坐标上固定实证边缘样本的多重集合（multiset），从而隔离出"耦合"这一单一因素的作用；此外还检验了去除固定边缘约束、直接采样路径的情形，以及原生多变量骨干网络推断的情形。

Result: 历史时间关系与通道关系能显著改善对应的依赖性诊断指标。该规律在去除固定边缘约束、直接采样路径时依然成立，在原生多变量骨干推断下也同样存在。

Conclusion: 依赖性重构（dependence reconstruction）应被视为概率型 TSFM 的一个独立后期处理问题，而非模型本身能力的附带产物。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series foundation models (TSFMs) provide coordinate-wise predictive distributions, but these marginals do not determine a joint distribution over multivariate future trajectories. We study training-free coupling of frozen TSFM marginals into multivariate forecast sample paths. Our primary evaluation fixes the empirical marginal sample multiset at every channel--horizon coordinate across methods, isolating the effect of coupling alone. Historical temporal and channel relations substantially improve their corresponding dependence diagnostics. The same pattern persists when the fixed-marginal constraint is removed and paths are sampled directly, and remains present under native multivariate backbone inference. These results support treating dependence reconstruction as a distinct post-processing problem for probabilistic TSFMs.

</details>


### [256] [Theory for groupoid equivariant neural networks: an approach for steerable CNNs on bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25987)
*Alberto Ibort,Maria Jimenez-Vazquez,Juan M. Perez-Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出群胚等变神经网络理论，将对称性由全局群作用推广为群胚+局部双截面的伪群，证明双截面等变核定理，并在有界平面域上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变卷积网络假设一个群全局作用于信号空间，这一假设对有界或分层区域不适用：环境刚体运动可能只在部分区域可行，边界会引入传递群作用看不见的几何类型。因此需要更一般的对称性框架来处理局部对称与边界效应。

Method: 构建群胚等变神经网络理论：对称性数据由群胚、选定的局部双截面伪群、测度以及输入输出表示丛组成。对对象空间上的积分通道，证明双截面等变核定理——等变性等价于两点核上的传输约束，其解由每条点对轨道上的一个联合稳定化子缠结算子分类。以有界平面域为案例，用离线零空间基与稀疏 gather–transform–scatter 操作实现该架构，并用 Poisson–Dirichlet 核研究评估边界感知归纳偏置。

Result: 精确逆算子保持矩形的全局对称性，但不保持一般真局部双截面；数值结果显示，当对称性无法由群作用全局实现时，所提架构具有显著优势，相较所测试模型精度提升至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 群胚等变框架能统一刻画局部对称与边界几何，为有界/分层域上的等变网络提供理论基础与可实现的架构，在全局群作用失效的场景中明显优于现有方法。

Abstract: Equivariant convolutional neural networks are usually built from a group acting globally on the space of signals. This hypothesis is inappropriate for many bounded or stratified domains: an ambient rigid motion may be admissible only on part of the domain, and the boundary introduces geometric types that are invisible to a transitive group action. We develop a theory of groupoid-equivariant neural networks in which the symmetry datum consists of a groupoid, a selected pseudogroup of local bisections, a measure, and input and output representation bundles. For integral channels on the object space, we prove a bisection-equivariant kernel theorem: equivariance is equivalent to a transport constraint on the two-point kernel, and its solutions are classified by one joint-stabilizer intertwiner on each orbit of pairs. As a case study we apply the theory to bounded planar domains.
  The resulting architecture is implemented through offline nullspace bases and sparse gather--transform--scatter operations. A Poisson--Dirichlet kernel study is used separately to assess boundary-aware inductive bias; the exact inverse is shown to preserve the global symmetries of the rectangle but not general proper local bisections. The numerical results show that the proposed architectures provide significant advantages when symmetries cannot be globally implemented by group actions and provide an accuracy improvement of at least one order of magnitude with respect to the models tested.

</details>


### [257] [The Dynamics of Quasiregular Neural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26018)
*Matthia Sabatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在拟正则回归任务中揭示了神经网络学习的主导规律与系统性例外之间的竞争，观察到"过度正则化"（U形学习）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习问题同时包含主导规律与系统性例外，受语言习得中U形学习现象启发，作者希望在受控的拟正则回归问题中研究这种规律与例外的交互作用。

Method: 设计拟正则回归问题，其中正则解与例外解均显式已知，用神经网络进行学习，并在不同例外稀有程度和不同规律类型下考察学习动态。

Result: 网络能部分习得例外，随后回退到主导规律（过度正则化），最终又恢复；当例外稀少时，尽管早期可习得，过度正则化显著增强；该现象并非在所有规律上都同等出现。

Conclusion: 结果分离出神经学习中规律与例外之间一种简单的竞争形式。

Abstract: Many learning problems combine a dominant regularity with systematic exceptions. Motivated by U-shaped learning in language acquisition, we study this interaction in controlled quasiregular regression problems where regular and exceptional solutions are explicitly known. Neural networks can partially acquire exceptions, subsequently regress toward the dominant regularity, and finally recover. This overregularization becomes substantially stronger when exceptions are rare, despite their early acquisition, but does not emerge equally across all regularities considered. Our results isolate a simple form of competition between regularities and exceptions during neural learning.

</details>


### [258] [BOBA: Dynamic Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26021)
*Merlin Angel Kelly,Rishan Patel,Alexander Thomas,Ziyue Zhu,Zikun Quan,Tom Carlson,Youngjun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOBA采集函数，基于主动推断的自由能原理显式最小化对未来状态的不确定性，用于动态黑箱优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态贝叶斯优化中目标函数随时间演化，最优位置持续漂移。现有DBO方法采用UCB等标准采集函数，未显式考虑时间变化，导致样本分配次优、难以跟踪移动最优解。

Method: 提出BOBA（Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference），受主动推断中自由能原理启发的采集函数。它在传统采集函数基础上引入前瞻性不确定性量化，估计函数变化中的不确定性，从而在非平稳环境中实现更合理的探索-利用权衡。同时分析BOBA在不同探索策略下的变体。

Result: 在合成动态基准上对比最先进DBO方法：BOBA在查询受限场景下显著改善regret，在时间受限场景下保持竞争力；不同探索策略变体可针对不同类型动态函数调节探索-利用平衡。

Conclusion: 贡献了一种基于自由能的DBO采集函数，并揭示了主动推断原理如何增强非平稳环境下的优化，对需要持续适应的实时应用具有意义。

Abstract: Dynamic black-box optimization presents significant challenges for Bayesian Optimization (BO), as the objective function evolves over time, causing optimal locations to shift continuously. Existing dynamic BO (DBO) methods using standard acquisition functions such as Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) fail to explicitly account for temporal variations, leading to suboptimal sample allocation and poor tracking of moving optima. Here, we propose BOBA (Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference), a novel acquisition function inspired by free energy principles from active inference that explicitly minimizes predictive uncertainty about future states in dynamic environments. BOBA extends traditional acquisition functions by incorporating a forward-looking uncertainty quantification that estimates uncertainty in function changes, enabling more informed exploration-exploitation trade-offs in non-stationary settings. We evaluate BOBA on synthetic dynamic benchmarks, comparing against state-of-the-art DBO methods. Our experiments demonstrate that BOBA significantly improves regret in query-restricted settings, while remaining competitive in time-limited settings. We further analyze variants of BOBA with different exploration strategies, showing how the exploration-exploitation balance can be tuned for different types of dynamic functions. This work contributes both a free energy-based acquisition function for DBO and insights into how active inference principles can enhance optimization in non-stationary environments, with implications for real-time applications requiring continuous adaptation.

</details>


### [259] [MICRO: Multi-Fidelity Active Search for Severe Error Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26025)
*Orlando Leone,Niclas Pokel,Pehuén Moure,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Boehringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MICRO框架，将低成本评分与高成本标注纳入统一预算，用于主动搜索严重错误并最大化其发现数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈在成本与信息量上存在差异：强反馈（标注）能揭示严重错误但昂贵，而廉价的评分可辅助判断哪些样本值得标注。因此需要一个能联合利用多保真反馈、共享预算并最大化严重错误发现的主动搜索框架。

Method: MICRO（Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout）将评分与标注损失在样本特征条件下联合建模以指导采集；按预测的严重性概率影响对采集项进行聚类以选出多样候选；再用rollout估计其发现价值。

Result: 在WMT20英德数据集上，评分同时改善了损失重建与严重性预测。MICRO在四种预算与评分成本设置中取得最高平均发现数；在其中一种设置下与适配的MF-ENS表现相近，在其余三种设置下显著优于全部六种对比策略（含两种rollout对照），p<.001。

Conclusion: 联合建模多保真反馈并结合影响聚类与rollout估计，可在共享预算下高效进行主动搜索，最大化严重错误发现。

Abstract: Human feedback can vary in cost and informativeness. Strong feedback can reveal severe errors but is costly, so cheaper quality ratings can help decide which items to annotate. We propose MICRO (Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout), an active search framework that allocates a shared budget to these feedback types to maximise confirmed severe error discoveries. MICRO jointly models ratings and annotation losses conditional on item features to steer acquisition. It clusters acquisitions by their predicted impact on severity probabilities to select diverse candidates, then uses rollout to estimate their discovery value. Experiments on WMT20 English-German show that ratings improve both loss reconstruction and severity prediction. MICRO achieves the highest mean discovery count across four budget and rating cost settings, with similar performance to adapted MF-ENS in one and significant gains over all six comparison policies, including two rollout controls, in the other three $(p<.001)$.

</details>


### [260] [xWhyL: Causal Interactive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26037)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Florian Peter Busch,Moritz Willig,Matej Zečević,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 xWhyL 框架，将解释作为溯因学习信号与观测数据互补，用于学习因果模型，并给出拒绝错误解释的理论条件与 CIL 实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学表明人类解释驱动是学习因果的机制，但 AI 领域基本忽视了从溯因信号中学习；XAI 常借助因果模型生成解释，反向问题（解释能为因果做什么）却鲜有研究。为填补该空白，作者研究如何从解释中学习因果模型。

Method: 提出 xWhyL 形式化框架，连接因果性与 XAI；建立数学理论，将解释转化为与观测数据互补的学习信号；针对解释可能源自错误信念并与数据冲突的"因果拔河"（Causal Tug-of-War）张力，证明框架在何种条件下拒绝错误设定的解释而非吸收它们；给出实际实例 Causal Interactive Learning (CIL)。

Result: 理论证明该框架可突破观测因果发现的局限，并在特定条件下拒绝错误设定的解释；CIL 实例表明专家解释能有效支持因果发现，并区分正确与错误的解释。

Conclusion: 解释可作为与观测数据互补的因果学习信号，xWhyL 与 CIL 为"解释服务于因果"这一反向方向提供了理论与可实践方案。

Abstract: Explanations are central to causal reasoning, and cognitive science has long established that the human drive to explain is itself a mechanism for learning about causality. Despite this, learning from those abductive signals is largely ignored in artificial intelligence. While explainable AI (XAI) increasingly draws on causal models to generate explanations, the converse direction about what explanations can do for causality remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose xWhyL, a formal framework connecting causality and XAI by learning causal models from explanations. We develop a mathematical theory that translates explanations into a learning signal complementary to observational data, and demonstrate how it enables overcoming the limits of observational causal discovery. As explanations can be derived from incorrect beliefs and clash with data, a tension we call the Causal Tug-of-War, we prove conditions under which our framework rejects misspecified explanations rather than absorbing them. Our practical instantiation, Causal Interactive Learning (CIL), shows how expert explanations can efficiently support causal discovery and distinguish correct from incorrect explanations.

</details>


### [261] [Towards Adaptive Federated Graph Clustering: A Global Community-aware Contrastive Learning-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26063)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Wang Luo,Guocong Quan,Miao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaFGC，一种基于全局社区感知对比学习的自适应联邦图聚类框架，通过过完备全局社区锚点自适应估计聚类数目并实现跨客户端的社区级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习多聚焦有监督任务，而现实中图数据往往大规模且无标签，联邦图聚类尚不成熟。该任务的核心难点在于客户端间子图异质性导致社区结构各不相同。现有方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 依赖不现实的预定义簇数目假设；(2) 社区间分离不充分。

Method: AdaFGC 引入一组过完备（over-complete）的全局社区锚点来建模全局社区结构，并通过跨客户端锚点精炼自适应估计聚类数目；同时采用全局社区感知对比学习方案，以共享锚点作为对比原型，显式地在跨客户端间施加社区级吸引与排斥，并辅以节点级与拓扑级目标以稳定本地表示。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AdaFGC 在多项聚类指标上持续优于现有的有监督与无监督联邦图学习基线。

Conclusion: AdaFGC 通过全局社区锚点与社区感知对比学习，有效解决了联邦图聚类中簇数目需预定义与社区分离不足的问题，验证了自适应联邦图聚类框架的有效性。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train graph models without sharing their private graph data, providing a promising paradigm for mining knowledge from distributed graph repositories. While most existing FGL methods focus on supervised tasks, real-world graphs are often massive and unlabeled, making federated graph clustering an important yet still immature research direction. Notably, this task is particularly challenging due to the inherent subgraph heterogeneity across clients, which leads to client-specific community structures. In this work, we identify two critical limitations in existing federated graph clustering methods: (1) unrealistic pre-defined cluster cardinality assumptions and (2) incomplete inter-community separation. To address these challenges, we propose AdaFGC, an Adaptive Federated graph clustering framework based on Global community-aware Contrastive learning. AdaFGC introduces an over-complete set of global community anchors to model the global community structure and adaptively estimate clustering cardinality via cross-client anchor refinement. In addition, it employs a global community-aware contrastive learning scheme that uses the shared anchors as contrastive prototypes to explicitly enforce community-level attraction and repulsion across clients, complemented by node-level and topology-level objectives that stabilize local representations. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that AdaFGC consistently outperforms existing supervised and unsupervised FGL baselines across multiple clustering metrics.

</details>


### [262] [FuncCode: Compressing Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in Function Space with Hardware-Aware Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26067)
*Kazi Ahmed Asif Fuad,Lizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuncCode是一种基无关的KAN压缩方法，通过对边响应采样构建共享码本，分别编码基分支与base分支并量化位打包，实现高压缩率与低精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN用可学习的一元函数替代标量边权重，提升了灵活性，但每条边需存储多个系数（常伴随独立base分支），导致参数内存显著增加，亟需高效压缩方法。

Method: 从采样边响应构建共享码本；对基分支与base分支独立编码；以量化、位打包格式导出码本与每条边的索引；方法为基无关（basis-agnostic），适用于样条与多项式KAN。

Result: 采样边响应的有效秩比系数表示低13–35%；函数空间聚类与系数空间聚类统计上相当，增益来自保留两分支各异共享结构。十种子MNIST上样条与GRAM KAN压缩31.6×/17.6×，精度损失0.31/0.34 pp；6.1M边卷积KAGN压缩19.9×，CIFAR-10/100分别损失0.54/1.89 pp。压缩后边索引占存储权重比特最高99.4%，表示受索引约束；九款位精确FPGA加速器上相对dense INT4降低SplineKAN布线后权重内存3.87×，且不增加周期数与延迟。

Conclusion: FuncCode通过函数空间共享码本与分支独立编码，显著压缩KAN权重内存并保持精度，在硬件部署中有效降低内存占用而不牺牲性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace scalar edge weights with learnable univariate functions, increasing flexibility but also parameter memory because each edge stores multiple coefficients, often together with a separate base branch. We introduce FuncCode, a basis-agnostic compression approach that forms shared codebooks from sampled edge responses, codes the basis and base branches independently, and exports the resulting codebooks and per-edge indices in a quantized, bit-packed format. Across spline and polynomial KANs, sampled edge responses exhibit $13$--$35\%$ lower effective rank than their coefficient representations. Further replicated controls show that function-space clustering alone is statistically tied with coefficient-space clustering; the consistent accuracy gain comes from preserving the distinct sharing structure of the two branches. On a ten-seed MNIST benchmark, FuncCode compresses spline and GRAM KANs by $31.6\times$ and $17.6\times$ with only $0.31$ and $0.34$ pp accuracy loss. On a 6.1M-edge convolutional KAGN, it achieves $19.9\times$ compression while remaining within $0.54$ pp of dense accuracy on CIFAR-10 and $1.89$ pp on CIFAR-100. After compression, per-edge indices account for up to $99.4\%$ of stored weight bits, making the representation index-bound. Across nine bit-exact FPGA accelerators, FuncCode reduces SplineKAN post-route weight memory by $3.87\times$ relative to dense INT4, without increasing cycle count or latency. The FuncCode implementation is available at https://github.com/OSU-STARLAB/FuncCode.

</details>


### [263] [Fast Matrix Multiplication in fp8: Certified Coefficient Optimization and Measured Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26077)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在每个Strassen型算法实现上附加系数泛函Φ，将低精度算法实现的选择转化为Hadamard流形上的Kempf–Ness问题，从而全局最优地认证fp8误差最小的实现，并在实测中验证其预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strassen型算法存在多个具有相同精确乘积和乘法次数的实现，但因基变换改变系数几何结构而导致不同的fp8浮点误差，因此需要回答"该运行哪个实现"的问题。现有理论无法解决：经典稳定性理论控制的是最坏情况ℓ1增长而非期望误差幅度，而Dumas–Pernet–Sedoglavic优化器只能被称为"可能最优"，其全局最优性未被证明。

Method: 为每个实现附加一个系数泛函Φ（系数几何的标量摘要），并在基变换轨道上最小化Φ。利用Hadamard流形上的Kempf–Ness理论，通过精确的矩映射零点认证全局Φ最优值（Φ_min=200/9），并借助de Groote分类将该最优性推广到所有精确实数秩-7的2×2分解。随后引入显式块缩放e4m3模型，其中Φ是相对期望均方误差的领先阶系数，并用实际fp8误差检验Φ预测的排序。

Result: 矩映射零点给出Φ_min=200/9；每个精确实数秩-7实现具有的Φ预测RMS常数至少为固定噪声系数下三次算法常数的5/3倍。排序与重基实验在测试的融合块缩放体制内支持该预测；在来自四个架构族的真实matmul分块上，Φ最优实现落在fp8低误差区域；在两个约70B模型上使用真实deep_gemm内核，同一实现消除了经典Strassen相对干净模型超额NLL的10%至55%。

Conclusion: 算法实现由此从调参选择转变为具有数学认证的设计问题：它是一个独立的低精度轴，具有全局Φ最优性和经实测的fp8相关性。

Abstract: A Strassen-type algorithm has many realizations with the same exact product and multiplication count yet different fp8 error because basis changes reshape coefficient geometry, posing the question of which to run. No current account settles this: classical stability controls worst-case $\ell_1$ growth, not the expected-error magnitude, and the Dumas--Pernet--Sedoglavic optimizer could only be called probably optimal, its global optimality unproved. To settle this, we attach to each realization a coefficient functional $Φ$, a scalar summary of its coefficient geometry, which we minimize over the change-of-basis orbit. This Kempf--Ness problem on a Hadamard manifold lets us certify the global $Φ$ optimum rather than merely search for it: an exact moment-map zero fixes $Φ_{\min} = 200/9$, and de Groote's classification extends that optimality to every exact real rank-7 $2\times2$ decomposition. Every exact real rank-7 realization therefore has a $Φ$-predicted RMS constant at least $5/3$ times that of the cubic algorithm, at fixed noise coefficient. We then introduce an explicit block-scaled e4m3 model in which $Φ$ is the leading-order coefficient of relative expected mean-squared error, and we test the resulting $Φ$-predicted ordering against realized fp8 error. Ordering and re-basing experiments support that prediction within tested fused block-scaled regimes, and on real matmul tiles from four architecture families the $Φ$-optimal realization falls in the fp8 low-error region. Across two $\sim$70B models on real deep_gemm kernels, the same realization removes 10 to 55% of classic Strassen's excess NLL over the clean model. Algorithm realization thus becomes a mathematically certified design problem rather than a tuning choice: an independent low-precision axis with a global $Φ$ optimum and measured fp8 relevance.

</details>


### [264] [Margin-Drop Coordinates for Cross-Budget Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26081)
*Yanliang Huang,Zhen Zhang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Sitong Zhu,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 浅层固定预算鲁棒性评估无法可靠区分冻结视觉编码器的深层鲁棒性；作者提出按干净边际归一化的三个边际下降坐标（干净余量、单步不足、漂移），发现浅层漂移坐标能高精度预测后续深度攻击(如PGD-10到PGD-200)的崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算(浅层)鲁棒性评估可能选出错误的冻结视觉编码器：在浅层攻击下表现稳健的编码器，在同等评估被加强后可能丧失大部分鲁棒性。作者希望探究浅层评估信息是否足以识别这种预算脆弱性，即能否在低成本评估下预判深度攻击下的崩溃。

Method: 对每个干净且分类正确的样本，记录四个量：干净成对边际、一阶线性化边际下降尺度、干净起始单步攻击的边际下降、迭代攻击达到的下降。按该尺度归一化后得到三个边际下降坐标：干净边际余量、单步不足(shortfall)、漂移(drift，即迭代攻击超出单步扰动的额外归一化边际下降)。三者可重构归一化攻击后边际，从而恢复通过/失败结果。在42个预训练冻结视觉编码器上比较浅层存活率与中位数浅层漂移坐标对后续PGD-10至PGD-200崩溃的排序能力。

Result: 浅层存活率对后续崩溃几乎不含排序信息(Spearman -0.006)，而中位数浅层漂移坐标排序同一崩溃达 +0.811；结论在留出编码器池及$\ell_\infty$评估下仍成立。在限制为11个编码器的深度评估中，按浅层漂移排序可召回17个高崩溃编码器中的11个，而存活率排序仅召回5个。完整坐标分解还能区分固定预算残差相同但深预算下分化的情形，并在干预下分离边际修复与漂移修复，揭示端点鲁棒性无法识别的不同修复路径。

Conclusion: 固定的浅层鲁棒性评估不足以选出真正稳健的编码器，存活率几乎无预测力；归一化边际下降分解(尤其是浅层漂移坐标)能有效预测深度预算下的崩溃，并能刻画不同修复路径，为更可靠的鲁棒性评估与选择提供了依据。

Abstract: Fixed-budget robustness evaluation can select the wrong frozen vision encoder. An encoder that survives a shallow attack may lose most of that robustness when the same evaluation is strengthened. We ask whether the shallow evaluation contains enough information to identify this budget fragility. For each clean-correct sample, the evaluation records the clean pairwise margin, the first-order linearized margin-drop scale, the margin drop from a clean-start one-step attack, and the drop reached by an iterative attack. Normalizing by that scale gives three margin-drop coordinates capturing clean margin slack, one-step shortfall, and drift, where drift is the additional normalized margin drop the iterative attack reaches beyond the one-step perturbation. Together, they reconstruct the normalized post-attack margin and therefore the pass-or-fail outcome. Across 42 pretrained frozen vision encoders, the shallow survival rate carries essentially no rank information about subsequent PGD-10 to PGD-200 collapse, at Spearman -0.006, while the median shallow drift coordinate ranks the same collapse at +0.811. The result persists in a held-out encoder pool and under an $\ell_\infty$ evaluation. With deep evaluation limited to 11 encoders, ranking by shallow drift recovers 11 of the 17 high-collapse encoders, compared with 5 under survival-rate ranking. The full coordinate decomposition further distinguishes cases that share the same fixed-budget residual but diverge at deeper budgets, and separates margin repair from drift repair under interventions, revealing distinct repair paths that endpoint robustness alone does not identify.

</details>


### [265] [CoEvo: Oracle-Grounded Self-Evolution of a Single Model for Multi-Step Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26094)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoEvo——一种以确定性外部 oracle 为过程监督来源的自进化框架。单个模型在 Proposer 与 Solver 两个角色间交替:Solvers 生成竞争性推理链并通过组内辩论暴露分歧步骤,Solver 由 oracle 裁决为过程级监督;Proposer 在同一模型内构造逐步加难、受 oracle 约束的场景。两角色联合更新使训练压力与模型协同演化,在工业、临床、法律多步因果推理基准上使 8B 模型路径正确率达 82.1%,超越蒸馏基线与最强闭源参考(71.4%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步因果推理中,早期错误会沿链隐性传播,而"错误逻辑却得到正确答案"使结果级检测失效。专业领域内教师 LLM 在中间步骤上出错、云蒸馏受安全约束限制、条件持续变化,使自我演化成为现实可行路径。但朴素自我演化会崩溃:仅用结果奖励会让模型利用分布捷径,而弱自评估会把虚假推理路径固化为稳定的失败模式。

Method: 利用"生成正确链难、验证单步易"的非对称性:许多高风险领域存在确定性、可查询的 oracle(物理模拟器、法规约束规则引擎),它无需教师级能力即可校验模型断言的步骤,并在规则之外弃权。基于此构建 CoEvo:单一模型交替担任 Solver 与 Proposer。作为 Solver 生成竞争链,组内辩论暴露分歧步骤(作为能力边界的代理),由 oracle 裁决为过程级监督;作为 Proposer 在 oracle 约束内构造渐进更难场景,将课程导向深度多跳链。两角色联合更新,使训练压力与模型协同演化。

Result: 在工业、临床、法律多步因果推理基准上,CoEvo 使 8B LLM 能够持续自我演化,在路径正确率上超越蒸馏基线与最强闭源参考(82.1% vs. 71.4%),并能泛化到未见类别与系统,保持根因准确率。

Conclusion: 以可查询确定性 oracle 提供过程级监督、配合 Solver–Proposer 双角色协同演化,可在无教师蒸馏条件下实现稳定的自我进化,并把多步因果推理的改进从结果正确性转向路径正确性,兼具跨类别与跨系统的泛化性。

Abstract: Multi-step causal reasoning requires chaining inferences where each step constrains the next. An early error propagates silently, and a correct answer reached via flawed logic evades outcome-level detection. In specialized domains, teacher LLMs err on intermediate steps, safety constraints restrict cloud distillation, and shifting conditions demand adaptation, leaving self-evolution as the practical route. Naive self-evolution can collapse: outcome-only rewards let the model exploit distributional shortcuts, and weak self-evaluation reinforces spurious paths into stable failure patterns. We exploit a key asymmetry: generating a correct chain is hard, but verifying a single step is easy. Many high-stakes domains admit a deterministic, queryable oracle, a physics simulator or rule engine over codified constraints. It checks asserted steps without teacher-level ability and abstains beyond its rules; it can check what the model asserts, never replace it. This enables CoEvo, an oracle-grounded self-evolution framework where a single model alternates between Proposer and Solver. As Solver, the model generates competing chains; intra-group debate exposes disagreement steps, a proxy for the capability boundary, and the oracle adjudicates them into process-level supervision. As Proposer, the same model constructs progressively harder scenarios inside oracle constraints, steering the curriculum toward deep multi-hop chains. Both roles are updated jointly, so training pressure co-evolves with the model. On industrial, clinical, and legal multi-step causal reasoning benchmarks, CoEvo enables an 8B LLM to sustain self-evolution, surpassing distillation baselines and the strongest proprietary reference on path correctness (82.1% vs. 71.4%). The trained model generalizes to unseen categories and systems, preserving root-cause accuracy.

</details>


### [266] [Certified Mechanistic Interpretability: Lifting Single-Input Findings to Bounded Neighbourhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26112)
*Zhen Zhang,Yanliang Huang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约束多项式zonotope（CPZ）传播的框架，将机械可解释性从单输入观测提升为对扰动邻域的认证陈述，并在注意力机制上实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性方法逐输入逆向工程Transformer电路，所得机制仅在单点成立，无法对输入邻域提供保证，导致经验启发式可能误导下游决策。

Method: 采用约束多项式zonotope（CPZ）传播，将top-k稳定性、证据质量和注意力熵三项内部注意力查询表述为注意力权重单纯形上的可解程序；证明CPZ通过Transformer块时精确保持softmax单纯形与LayerNorm零均值恒等式；并提出递归Jacobian zonotope构造，通过在输入处线性化块堆栈将认证扩展至层深，避免逐层生成元增长。

Result: 在Transformer注意力上实例化该框架，得到能够将单输入检查无法分辨的机械性陈述加以锐化的认证，并为经验启发式可能失效的场景提供决策依据。

Conclusion: 该框架弥补了机械可解释性缺乏邻域保证的空白，将单点观测升级为有界扰动集合上的认证陈述，为Transformer内部机制分析提供更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reverse-engineers transformer circuits one input at a time, leaving observed mechanisms without guarantees over bounded input neighbourhoods. We address this gap with a framework based on constrained polynomial-zonotope (CPZ) propagation that lifts mechanistic-interpretability observations from a single input to certified statements over a bounded set of perturbations. Three internal-attention queries (top-$k$ stability, evidence mass, and attention entropy) are formulated as tractable programs over the simplex of attention weights, and CPZ propagation through transformer blocks is shown to preserve the softmax simplex and the LayerNorm zero-mean identity exactly. A recursive Jacobian zonotope construction extends the same certificates across layer depth by linearising the block stack at the input and avoids per-layer generator growth. We instantiate the framework on transformer attention; the resulting certificates offer a way to sharpen mechanistic statements that single-input inspection cannot resolve on its own, and to inform downstream decisions in regimes where empirical heuristics may be misleading.

</details>


### [267] [From Risk Scoring to Risk Allocation: A Density-Driven Framework for Diverse Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26146)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将多智能体系统风险监控从"风险打分"转向"风险分配"，揭示Crowding Paradox（密度而非异常分数才是有效风险信号），并将监控重构为QUBO子集选择问题，在金融、交通和多智能体强化学习中验证，并在超导量子处理器上验证了兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体风险监控基于逐状态打分并取Top-K的primitive。在"拥挤"场景下（多智能体共享相同脆弱性），该方式会选出风险联合相关的冗余告警，即"监控中的羊群效应"，导致监控失效。

Method: 1) 提出Crowding Paradox：P(risk|x)∝p(x)而非1/p(x)，表明密度是操作性风险信号；2) 基于密度导出的脆弱性分数，将监控重构为相互依赖状态上的组合子集选择，映射为带λ控制的风险-多样性权衡的QUBO目标；3) 引入学习型λ策略。

Result: 密度打分在金融数据5d/10d/20d崩盘窗口达AUROC≥0.94，五个异常基线均<0.80；Pareto前沿包含MMR、k-DPP等固定操作点；相对贪心增益随规模单调增长，n=15时+24%，n=200时+66%；学习型λ策略达oracle网格搜索目标的99.5%；方法迁移到交通与MARL；同一QUBO实例无需修改即可在Rigetti超导QPUs（Ankaa-3、Cepheus-1-108Q，经Amazon Braket）上运行。

Conclusion: 监控应从风险打分转向风险分配。QUBO公式统一了多样性方法并具备可扩展性与跨域迁移能力；量子硬件运行仅作为公式的兼容性属性报告，而非此规模下的量子优势声明。

Abstract: Risk monitoring in multi-agent systems is commonly built on a per-state primitive that scores each state independently and selects the top K. Under crowding, where many agents share the same fragility, this approach picks redundant alerts whose risks are jointly correlated, a pattern we describe as ``herding in monitoring.'' We propose a paradigm shift from risk scoring to risk allocation, supported by two contributions. First, we identify the Crowding Paradox, namely that P(risk | x) $\propto$ p(x) rather than 1/p(x), so density rather than anomaly score is the operative risk signal; on financial data, density-based scoring reaches AUROC $\geq$ 0.94 at 5d/10d/20d crash horizons, while five anomaly baselines all fall below 0.80. Second, given a density-derived fragility score, we recast monitoring as combinatorial subset selection over interdependent states and map it to a QUBO objective with a $λ$-controlled risk--diversity tradeoff. The resulting Pareto frontier contains standard diverse-subset methods (MMR, k-DPP) as fixed operating points; the gain over greedy grows monotonically with scale, from +24% at n=15 to +66% at n=200; a learned $λ$ policy reaches 99.5% of an oracle grid-search objective; and the formulation transfers to traffic and multi-agent reinforcement learning. The same QUBO instances execute without modification on Rigetti superconducting QPUs (Ankaa-3 and Cepheus-1-108Q via Amazon Braket), which we report as a compatibility property of the formulation rather than a claim of quantum advantage at this scale.

</details>


### [268] [Block-Level Weight-Space Structure Persists Under Post-Training: An Empirical Study Across LLM Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26147)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究后训练对LLM权重空间几何的影响，发现后训练虽改变每个张量却保持块级结构，并据此构建LinkerLLM实现跨变体块共享，节省18-48% GPU显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型常以同源预训练权重派生出base、instruct、chat、code等后训练变体族部署。理解后训练如何改变权重空间几何，既能揭示后训练的本质特性，也可为多变体共驻部署的内存优化提供依据。

Method: 对四个架构族（Qwen2.5、Llama-3.1/3.2、Mistral、Gemma-2）的八种配置进行实证分析，逐张量哈希比对、块级余弦相似度与相对Frobenius距离度量，并对比独立训练专用模型。基于发现构建LinkerLLM惰性加载器，在共驻变体间别名共享块，保持模型质量并评估基准表现。

Result: 发现粒度差距：291-339个张量无一字节相同，哈希去重0%收益，但块级平均余弦相似度超0.99、相对Frobenius距离低于0.13；独立训练的专用模型（如Qwen2.5-Coder）与通用base余弦相似度约0.64，处于权重空间中不连通区域；扰动幅度随规模、架构族和后训练配方系统变化。LinkerLLM实现18-48% GPU显存节省，单张24GB消费级GPU可容纳最多五个7B参数变体；八配置中五个在MMLU、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag、WinoGrande上保留至少94%质量，其余三个（Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.2-1B）各有一项基准为87-91%，作者如实报告而不以单一阈值限制块共享决策。

Conclusion: 后训练可视为结构化扰动：参数级全变而块级几何保持；该性质在同源变体间成立但不适用于独立训练专用模型。块级共享是一种有效且可实用的显存优化手段，其质量损失需多基准透明评估而非单纯阈值判定。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are deployed as families of post-trained variants (base, instruct, chat, code) derived from a shared set of pre-trained weights. We present an empirical study of how post-training transforms weight-space geometry, covering eight configurations across four architecture families (Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1/3.2, Mistral, Gemma-2). We identify a granularity gap: post-training modifies every tensor (zero of 291-339 tensors remain byte-identical, so hash-based deduplication achieves 0% savings), yet preserves block-level structure (mean cosine similarity exceeds 0.99 and relative Frobenius distance stays below 0.13). Post-training therefore acts as a structured perturbation that shifts every parameter while leaving block-level geometry intact. The property is not universal: independently trained specializations (for example, Qwen2.5-Coder) attain cosine similarity around 0.64 with the general base, indicating a disconnected region of weight space. Perturbation magnitude varies systematically with model scale, architecture family, and post-training recipe. As a practical application, we build LinkerLLM, a lazy loader that aliases shareable blocks across co-resident variants, achieving 18-48% GPU memory savings and enabling up to five 7B-parameter variants on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. Five of eight configurations retain at least 94% of the unshared variant's quality on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, and WinoGrande; the remaining three (Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B) have one below-threshold benchmark each (87-91%), which we report transparently rather than gate the block-sharing decision on a single threshold.

</details>


### [269] [Spectral Tail Interventions in Decoder-Only Language Models: Reasoning-Sensitive Weight Structure from Controlled Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26165)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjida Akhter,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过受控干预研究解码器Transformer中上谱尾部集中现象，建立有限宽度条件界与QK乘积尾部目标，证明学习到的尾部编辑在20个模型-任务单元中均比谱匹配Haar控制更具破坏性，并发现逆参与比先于准确率转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间结构与语言模型行为常存在相关性，但相关性本身无法确立计算层面的因果参与。作者希望超越单纯的相关性观察，通过受控干预来检验集中上谱尾部是否真正参与模型计算，从而建立权重谱结构与模型行为之间的因果桥梁。

Method: 在固定相对偏移下推导有限宽度条件界，将平方奇异值的逆参与比与中心pre-softmax logit峰度联系起来；定义逐点query-key（QK）乘积尾部目标，比较独立因子手术与保持原生注意力计算的产品目标因式分解；在三个基础检查点、五个推理基准及一个指令微调的Phi检查点上进行实验，使用五个固定谱匹配Haar控制作为基线，并进行Holm校正与组件隔离分析。

Result: 在所有20个模型-任务单元中，学习到的尾部编辑比五个固定谱匹配Haar控制的均值更具破坏性；18个配对对比经Holm校正后仍显著，2个方向一致但不具决定性。产品目标因子获得更高的留出尾部子空间比例，为乘积级与因子级干预提供经验桥梁。组件隔离识别出QK、value-output和多层感知机模块的贡献，尽管定理仅覆盖QK。在独立研究中，逆参与比在匹配交叉规则下先于池化准确率转变，残差化尾部感知低秩适配（LoRA）比标准LoRA和PiSSA更早达到目标，但最终分数区间重叠。

Conclusion: 集中上谱尾部在解码器Transformer中确实具有计算参与性，而非仅与行为相关。结论限定于所评估的检查点、层、任务、干预和控制条件，强调了在有限实验范围内建立权重谱结构与模型行为因果联系的谨慎性。

Abstract: Weight-space structure often correlates with language-model behavior, but correlation alone does not establish computational involvement. We study concentrated upper spectral tails in decoder-only transformers through controlled interventions. At a fixed relative offset, we derive a finite-width conditional bound linking the inverse participation ratio of squared singular values to central pre-softmax logit kurtosis. We then define a pointwise query--key ($QK$) product-tail target and compare independent factor surgery with a product-targeted factorization that preserves native attention computation. Across three base checkpoints and five reasoning benchmarks, plus an instruction-tuned Phi checkpoint analyzed separately, the learned-tail edit is more damaging than the mean of five fixed spectrum-matched Haar controls in all 20 model--task cells. Eighteen paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction, while two are directional but inconclusive. Product-targeted factors attain higher held-out tail-subspace fractions, providing an empirical bridge between product- and factor-level interventions. Component isolation identifies contributions from $QK$, value--output, and multilayer-perceptron blocks, although the theorem covers only $QK$. In separate studies, inverse participation precedes pooled accuracy transitions under a matched crossing rule, and residualized tail-aware low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reaches targets earlier than standard LoRA and PiSSA while final-score intervals overlap. Conclusions are restricted to the evaluated checkpoints, layers, tasks, interventions, and controls.

</details>


### [270] [Activation-Energy Pruning for Spiking Neural Networks: Unsupervised Personalization via Spike-Count Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26167)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将激活能剪枝（权重幅度×累计突触前脉冲数）应用于脉冲神经网络（SNN），发现梯度类剪枝方法在SNN上失效，而激活能剪枝在高稀疏度下反而优于源模型（N-MNIST 98.4% vs 97.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活能剪枝已被证明是常规深度网络的有效无监督个性化策略。作者欲探究：当同一准则应用于SNN时会发生什么——在SNN中激活能不仅是启发式指标，而是与每个突触代谢成本成正比的实际物理量。

Method: 在多种SNN架构与数据集上，将激活能剪枝与梯度类剪枝方法（SNIP、GraSP、幅度剪枝）进行对比，考察不同稀疏度σ下的表现，并在神经形态基准（N-MNIST）上评估激活能剪枝。

Result: 梯度类剪枝方法在SNN上持续表现不佳，在σ=0.2稀疏度时所有架构与数据集上均退化至接近随机水平，作者将其归因于代理梯度显著性估计与二值脉冲序列表示之间的系统性不兼容（但不排除替代代理或超参数可能部分缓解）。激活能剪枝在高稀疏度下反而超过源模型：N-MNIST上σ=0.8时为98.4±0.4% vs 97.2±0.7%。

Conclusion: 激活能剪枝在SNN上的增益在常规网络中没有对应现象，作者将其解释为与经验依赖性皮层特化一致：去除仅对非目标类活跃的连接可减少跨类干扰，产生更干净的目标表示（但强调这是解释性类比而非机制性证明）。

Abstract: Activation-energy pruning -- removing weights whose product of magnitude and cumulative pre-synaptic spike count falls below a threshold -- was established as an effective unsupervised personalization strategy for conventional deep neural networks~\citep{BINGHAM2025101242}. This paper asks what happens when the same criterion is applied to spiking neural networks (SNNs), where activation energy is not merely a useful heuristic but a literal physical quantity proportional to the metabolic cost of each synapse. The answer is surprising on three counts.
  First, gradient-based pruning methods that perform competitively on conventional networks (SNIP, GraSP, magnitude pruning) consistently underperform on SNNs, collapsing to near-chance accuracy by $σ= 0.2$ sparsity across all tested architectures and datasets. We trace this to a systematic incompatibility between surrogate-gradient saliency estimation and the binary spike-train representation, though we cannot rule out that alternative surrogate choices or hyperparameter settings might partially mitigate the effect.
  Second, activation-energy pruning applied to a neuromorphic benchmark \emph{improves} over the source model at high sparsity ($98.4 \pm 0.4\%$ vs.\ $97.2 \pm 0.7\%$ at $σ= 0.8$ on N-MNIST), a phenomenon with no counterpart in the conventional network setting. We interpret this result as consistent with experience-dependent cortical specialisation: removing connections active only for non-target classes may reduce cross-class interference and produce a cleaner target representation, though we note this is an interpretive analogy rather than a mechanistic demonstration.

</details>


### [271] [Component Type, Not Reconstruction Error, Predicts Attention Quantization Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26173)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了9个开源语言模型中单个注意力投影的量化敏感性，发现权重重构误差对困惑度敏感性的预测能力很弱，层级与组件类型才是主导因素，且V投影通常最敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法多依赖层级重构、二阶代理目标或激活感知变换来减小量化误差，但该误差信号能否预测单个注意力投影量化后的下游功能影响，尚未被直接刻画。作者希望厘清重构误差与功能敏感性之间的关系，以指导混合精度位宽分配。

Method: 对9个开源权重语言模型（1.3B–8B；OPT、GPT-J、LLaMA-1/2/3、Mistral、Qwen 2.5）进行扫描，每次仅量化一个注意力投影，采用RTN，并对其中7个模型在3-bit与4-bit下使用GPTQ；记录重构误差、困惑度变化及逐投影激活加权量化误差，共获得3,808个独立测量。

Result: (1) RTN下同一组件类型（Q/K/V/O）内，重构误差在36种情形中的27种解释的困惑度敏感性方差不足10%，中位R²=0.044；(2) 全部9个模型中，组件类型与层身份解释的方差均高于重构误差，层身份在7/9模型中为最强预测因子；(3) V投影最常成为主导组件，在9个模型中的7个占正ΔPPL的38–51%；(4) 主导组件在RTN与GPTQ间大体保持（7例中5例）；(5) 对V投影，激活加权量化误差的组件内预测能力优于重构误差（中位R² 0.20 vs 0.06）。

Conclusion: 相对权重重构误差本身不足以支撑敏感性感知的位宽分配；组件类型与层身份是更强的敏感性预测因子，V投影在混合精度方案中应被单独重点考虑。

Abstract: Many post-training quantization (PTQ) methods use layer-wise reconstruction, second-order proxy objectives, or activation-aware transformations to reduce quantization-induced error. Whether that error signal predicts the downstream functional impact of quantizing an individual attention projection has not been directly characterized. We sweep nine open-weight language models (1.3B--8B parameters; OPT, GPT-J, LLaMA-1/2/3, Mistral, Qwen 2.5) and quantize one attention projection at a time under round-to-nearest (RTN) and, for seven models, GPTQ at 3 and 4 bits, recording reconstruction error, perplexity change, and per-projection activation-weighted quantization error for 3,808 distinct measurements. We find: (1) within a given component type (Q, K, V, or O), reconstruction error explains less than 10% of the variance in perplexity sensitivity in 27 of 36 cases under RTN, with median R^2 = 0.044; (2) both component type and layer identity explain more variance than reconstruction error in all 9 models, with layer identity the strongest predictor in 7 of 9 models and component type strongest in the remaining 2; (3) value (V) projections are the most commonly dominant component, accounting for 38--51% of total positive Delta PPL in seven of nine models; (4) the dominant component is broadly preserved between RTN and GPTQ (5 of 7 cases); and (5) activation-weighted quantization error is a moderately better within-component predictor than reconstruction error for V projections specifically (median R^2 of 0.20 vs. 0.06). These findings indicate that relative weight reconstruction error alone is insufficient for sensitivity-aware bit allocation, and that V projections merit dedicated consideration in mixed-precision schemes.

</details>


### [272] [Partially Observed Sparse Graphs: The Unknown Sampling Rate is a Tail Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26199)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在稀疏可交换图（graphex）模型下，若总体节点数已知而采样率s未知，非孤立节点占比满足 n_s/n_1→s^{1+σ}，据此可反推s并构造边数估计量 e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}；该估计量本质上是尾指数估计，二次graphon（σ=0）是其特例，故二次估计量因恒等式而非拟合失败。用闭式尾指数估计量在13个网络、39个采样预算下达到21.7%误差且无需拟合；但rank-one graphex与snowball/随机游走等爬取方式下方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模图常仅能获取部分样本（爬虫预算终止、面板、部分转储）。当采样率s为已知设计量时，可用 \hat e=e_s/s^2 估计总边数而无需模型；但现实中s往往未知。论文针对"总体节点数已知、采样率s未知"这一情形，寻求无需额外假设的边基数估计方法。

Method: 在稀疏可交换（graphex）模型下建立归约：证明期望非孤立节点占比满足 n_s/n_1→s^{1+σ}，其中σ为尾指数，从而可将未知采样率表示为尾指数的函数并代入设计型估计量 e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}。进一步给出该代换的有限规模误差界，并论证该归约对尾指数估计量具有模块化（modular）特性，可替换为已发表的闭式估计量实现无拟合估计。

Result: 二次graphon估计量对应σ=0的特殊情形，误差达260%中位数，而基于尾指数代换的方法仅27%；使用已发表闭式尾指数估计量时，在13个网络、39个采样预算下达到21.7%的误差且完全无需拟合。拟合完整graphex还能返回任意规模下的度分布与生成式对象，其表示中稀疏性是一个坐标且插值路径由模型决定而非人为选择。两个精确极限：rank-one graphex的传递性由度分布固定，故高聚类图不在该类内；在snowball或随机游走爬取方式下所有方法均失效，设计型oracle最差（7.8%到588%误差）。

Conclusion: 在稀疏图的期望意义下，全局边基数估计等价于尾指数估计；论文给出从采样率代换到模块化尾指数估计的归约框架，并阐明其适用边界（要求常用采样设计，排除rank-one与随机游走类爬取）。

Abstract: A large graph is often available only in part: a crawl stopped by its budget, a panel, a partial dump. When the sampled fraction $s$ is known by design the total edge count follows from $\hat e=e_s/s^2$ and no model is needed. We treat the case where $s$ is unknown and the population size is known. Our main result is a reduction: under a sparse exchangeable (graphex) model the expected non-isolated fraction obeys $n_s/n_1\to s^{1+σ}$, so the sampling rate becomes estimable once the tail index $σ$ is, and substituting it back gives $e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}$ -- the same estimator, with the design quantity inferred. Estimating global edge cardinality in a sparse graph is therefore, in expectation, tail-index estimation, and the quadratic graphon estimator is the case $σ=0$: it fails by an identity rather than by a fit ($260\%$ median error against $27\%$). We bound the finite-size error of the substitution and show the reduction is \emph{modular} in the tail-index estimator --- filled with a published closed-form one it reaches $21.7\%$ over $13$ networks and $39$ sampling budgets with no fitting at all. Fitting a full graphex additionally returns the degree distribution at any size and a generative object, in a representation where sparsity is a coordinate and the interpolation path is dictated rather than chosen. Two limits are exact: rank-one graphexes have transitivity fixed by the degree profile, so high-clustering graphs lie outside the class; and under snowball or random-walk crawls every method here fails, the design-based oracle worst of all ($7.8\%$ to $588\%$).

</details>


### [273] [Bridging the Data Gap: Digital Twin as a New Paradigm for AI-based Radio Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26214)
*Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Ali Al Khansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以3D数字孪生（DT）作为世界模型，为射线追踪引擎提供几何、材质与收发机布局，生成大规模场景的时变信道冲激响应（CIR），并据此训练序列神经网络，将CIR时间序列映射为空间占用估计，实现无设备定位（DFL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电感知的规模化发展受限于真实数据采集成本高、场景覆盖有限，且难以获得无设备定位所需的大规模标注数据，因此需要一种可扩展的感知范式。

Method: 以站点特定的3D数字孪生为条件，驱动射线追踪引擎生成大量合理场景（人员与物体移动、布局变化、季节与天气条件等）的时变CIR；基于这些合成序列训练序列神经网络，将CIR时间序列映射为空间占用估计，实现无设备定位；并提出将感知建模为环境条件化学习问题，训练或微调面向特定站点DT的局部模型，而非追求单一全局模型。实验采用一种新的状态空间模型（SSM）架构，在多种房间几何结构中训练与评估。

Result: 在多种房间几何结构下的定位性能结果表明了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的合成数据与站点特定局部模型相结合，为可扩展的无线电无设备感知提供了可行路径，初步实验验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: We present a methodology that places a 3D digital twin (DT) of the environment as the main enabler behind the development of radio sensing at scale. The DT acts as a world model, providing geometry, materials, and transmitter/receiver placements to a ray-tracing engine that generates time-indexed channel impulse responses (CIRs) for large numbers of plausible scenes (moving people and objects, layout variants, seasonal/weather conditions, etc). From these synthetic sequences, we train a sequential neural network that maps CIR time series to spatial occupancy estimates, enabling device-free localization (DFL) without instrumented targets. We posit that sensing is best approached as an environment-conditioned learning problem: rather than seeking a single global model, we advocate training or fine-tuning local models specialized to a site-specific DT. As a first experiment, we introduce a novel State Space Model architecture, trained and evaluated across multiple room geometries. The localization performances obtained demonstrate the potential of the approach.

</details>


### [274] [Beyond Imitation: Auditing the Recoverability of Reasoning in Distilled Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26216)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"前缀恢复"（prefix recovery）指标，用于衡量教师解对学生推理的可用性，并将其与交叉熵和反向KL梯度间的余弦冲突联系起来。在Qwen3相邻师生对上发现，容量约2B以下的较小学生从反向KL蒸馏中获益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确的教师解只有在学生能够延续其推理时才是有效监督。现有方法缺少衡量这种"可兼容性"的实用诊断手段，也无法预判分布级蒸馏（如反向KL）在何种容量条件下才有收益。

Method: 定义前缀恢复：揭示已验证解的25%/50%/75%后，测试学生能否正确补全。将其与全词表上交叉熵梯度与反向KL梯度的余弦冲突相联系。在0.6B–8B的相邻Qwen3师生对上测量，并在固定的1000条轨迹上统计平均前缀恢复与"稳健-脆弱"恢复差距；另做独立的目标函数干预实验。

Result: 反向KL蒸馏在2B以下的两个学生上取得最稳定的数学与代码提升。学生从0.6B增至4B时，平均前缀恢复由71.0%升至91.9%，稳健-脆弱恢复差距由46.0个百分点收窄至14.4个百分点。教师固定为8B时，冲突分离度从0.993降至0.233。目标函数干预在脆弱轨迹上产生最大的反向KL增益。

Conclusion: 三种测量指向同一容量依赖的迁移区间：当正确轨迹的可恢复性不均衡时，分布匹配的改进空间最大。前缀恢复可作为选择高成本分布级蒸馏的实用诊断工具。

Abstract: A correct teacher solution becomes useful supervision when the receiving student can continue its reasoning. We measure this compatibility with prefix recovery: after revealing 25%, 50%, or 75% of a verified solution, we test whether the student completes it correctly. We connect recovery to the cosine conflict between cross-entropy and reverse-KL gradients over the full vocabulary. Across adjacent Qwen3 teacher-student pairs from 0.6B to 8B parameters, reverse-KL distillation delivers its most consistent mathematical and code improvements for the two students below 2B parameters. On a fixed cohort of 1,000 trajectories, average prefix recovery rises from 71.0% to 91.9% as student size increases from 0.6B to 4B, and the robust-fragile recovery gap contracts from 46.0 to 14.4 percentage points. With the teacher fixed at 8B, conflict separation falls from 0.993 to 0.233. An independent objective intervention finds the largest reverse-KL rescue on fragile trajectories. The three measurements locate the same capacity-dependent transfer regime: distribution matching has the greatest headroom when correct traces remain unevenly recoverable. Prefix recovery provides a practical diagnostic for selecting costly distribution-level distillation.

</details>


### [275] [MSA-CITE: A Co-Adapted LoRA Specialist Ecology for Fixed-Budget Small-Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26217)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将单一后训练检查点部署视为可组合资产：保留四个冻结LoRA分支，各采样一条补全，通过校准先验评分与等价类归并选择答案，在数学任务上超越最强单分支基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型语言模型部署通常只保留单一后训练检查点并重复采样，浪费了训练过程中被丢弃的多个检查点。作者质疑这一惯例，认为不同后训练轨迹产生的分支可作为可组合的部署资产加以利用。

Method: 以Qwen3-4B为骨干，保留四个冻结的LoRA分支（各来自不同后训练轨迹）。不从一个分支采样四次，而是每个分支采样一条补全，构成固定四代预算的组合。MSA-CITE将终端答案归并为等价类，用校准导出的源先验求和评分各类，并按确定性平局规则选代表。读出阶段不学习评估结果，也不引入额外生成、验证器或重排序。

Result: 在200道留出数学题上，四路径组合达65.5%准确率，最强单分支基线为62.0%；在100题学科不相交分布偏移上为42.0%对40.0%。同分布条件下，相对同质SFT与Online-OPD重复的改进稳健；相对最强基线和偏移条件下的结果尚不具结论性。

Conclusion: 后训练分支即使未进行协同训练，也能在部署中集体获益，这是一个有限但具体的贡献。

Abstract: Compact language models are typically deployed by retaining a single post-training checkpoint and sampling it repeatedly. In this work, we challenge this practice by treating multiple discarded checkpoints as composable assets for deployment. Starting from a single Qwen3-4B backbone, we preserve four frozen LoRA branches, each derived from a different post-training trajectory. stead of drawing four generations from one branch, we allocate a fixed four-generation budget by sampling one completion from each branch. Our method, Multi-path Specialist Adaptation with Calibrated Inference-Time Evidence (MSA-CITE), processes the resulting portfolio by grouping terminal answers into equivalence classes, scoring each class via summed calibration-derived source priors, and selecting a representative under deterministic tie-breaking rules. The readout stage does not learn from evaluation results, nor does it introduce additional generations, verifiers, or reranking steps. On 200 held-out mathematics items, the four-path portfolio achieves 65.5% accuracy, compared with 62.0% for the strongest single-branch baseline. On a 100-item subject-disjoint shift, it attains 42.0% versus 40.0%. Under in-distribution conditions, the improvements over homogeneous SFT and Online-OPD repetition are robust; results against the strongest baseline and under shifted conditions are not conclusive.
