Our findings offer a narrow but concrete contribution: post-training branches, even without co-training, can be collectively beneficial for deployment.

</details>


### [276] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Modeling for Continuous and Subtle GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26231)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将连续微弱的GNSS欺骗检测建模为因果序列证据建模问题，提出高阶液态证据检测器（HO-LLN-Spoofing）。方法通过GNSS位移与车载非GNSS运动观测推断位移的残差构造不确定性归一化证据，提取当前不一致、局部演化、超常水平、累积持续性和位移有效性五类因果弱证据，经有界Kirchhoff对称交换映射到瞬时/演化/持续隐状态，再以三阶交互建模协同支持，并利用二阶液态动力学跟踪证据的时间演化输出因果欺骗概率。在AV-GPS数据集族上验证了受控与外部泛化能力及告警的序列行为，Dataset-1上AUROC 0.9932、AUPRC 0.9843，学习类基线中误报率最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续且微弱的GNSS欺骗对自动驾驶车辆威胁严重：伪造位置在局部看似合理，却会逐渐与车载非GNSS传感器观测到的车辆运动不一致。现有面向AV的检测器多依赖残差阈值或特征级分类，对微弱GNSS-运动不一致如何随时间发展并持续缺乏建模，难以捕捉其渐进性与累积性。因此作者将微弱GNSS欺骗检测重新表述为因果序列证据建模问题。

Method: 1) 证据构造：比较连续GNSS位置隐含的位移与独立车载运动观测推断的位移，将二者差异转换为不确定性归一化残差证据。2) 因果弱证据表示：提取当前不一致、局部演化、超出正常水平的过量、累积持续性、位移有效性五类线索。3) 状态映射与对齐：将线索映射为瞬时、演化、持续三类隐状态，并通过有界Kirchhoff启发的对称交换进行对齐。4) 显式三阶交互：组合三类隐状态以捕获其对欺骗的协同支持。5) 时间建模与告警：用二阶液态动力学跟踪该协同证据的记忆与演化，估计因果欺骗概率，并通过验证集选取的阈值与持续性参数转化为确认告警。

Result: 在AV-GPS数据集族上的实验表明，该方法具有较强的受控泛化与外部泛化能力，并表现出清晰的序列告警行为。在Dataset-1上取得AUROC 0.9932、AUPRC 0.9843，且在学习类基线中获得最低的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 连续微弱GNSS欺骗可被有效建模为随时间发展的因果序列证据，而非孤立残差或静态特征；通过弱证据的隐状态映射、三阶协同交互与二阶液态动力学记忆，能够在不牺牲泛化性的前提下显著提升检测精度并降低误报，为自动驾驶场景下的渐进式欺骗检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous and subtle GNSS spoofing poses a serious threat to autonomous vehicles because forged positions may remain locally plausible while gradually becoming inconsistent with vehicle motion observed by non-GNSS onboard sensors. Existing AV-oriented detectors commonly rely on residual thresholds or feature-level classification and provide limited modeling of how weak GNSS--motion inconsistency develops and persists over time. This paper formulates subtle GNSS spoofing detection as a causal sequential evidence-modeling problem and proposes a high-order liquid evidence detector. The method first compares the displacement implied by consecutive GNSS positions with that inferred from independent onboard motion observations and converts their difference into uncertainty-normalized residual evidence. It then represents the current inconsistency, its local evolution, excess above the normal level, accumulated persistence, and displacement validity as causal weak evidence. These cues are mapped into instantaneous, evolutionary, and persistent latent states, aligned through a bounded Kirchhoff-inspired symmetric exchange, and combined through an explicit third-order interaction to capture their coordinated support for spoofing. To model how this coordinated evidence develops over time, second-order liquid dynamics track its memory and evolution to estimate causal spoofing probabilities, which are converted into confirmed alarms using validation-selected threshold and persistence parameters. Experiments on the AV--GPS dataset family demonstrate strong controlled and external generalization, together with clear sequential alarm behavior. On Dataset-1, the proposed detector achieves an AUROC of 0.9932 and an AUPRC of 0.9843, while obtaining the lowest false-positive rate among the learning-based baselines. Code: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/HO-LLN-Spoofing.

</details>


### [277] [Can You Delete a Year of Market Data? Machine Unlearning Against Exact Retraining Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26242)
*Junyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在S&P 500波动率面板上系统基准测试了时间序列机器遗忘：用5种架构、4个滚动折、5个可删除年份、3种删除强度共3200组配对参照（全量训练）与重训预言机（剔除目标期）对比，发现2020年COVID是记忆缺口最大的年份，且近似遗忘的目标应是逼近预言机而非在删除期上追求低准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据许可到期后仅删除存储记录，无法消除已训练预测模型中所编码的影响；完全重训成本过高，因此需要机器遗忘。但时间序列场景下的遗忘缺乏系统评估标准，现有做法常误把"在删除期上预测变差"当作遗忘成功的标志。

Method: 构建配对基准：3200组参照模型（全量数据训练）与预言机（剔除指定时间段重训）配对；网格覆盖5种架构、4个滚动折、5个可删除年份、3种实验性删除强度，数据为S&P 500波动率面板。以预言机为遗忘目标，跨12组可部署"架构-方法"配对评估近似遗忘方法（含hinge方法等），并考察窗口级损失比较与股票级窗口独立性假设对统计推断的影响。

Result: 2020年COVID年对所有架构均产生最大记忆缺口；删除该年后3个可部署模型在每个折上均改善，2022年熊市中改善最大，而2个不可部署模型表现不一致。某Transformer单元中，从未训练2020的预言机对其信息系数仍达0.51（参照为0.55），把预测推向噪声反而损害测试技能。12组可部署配对中仅TSMixer+hinge方法在每个折上都接近预言机，弥合参照到预言机差距的74-118%且无测试技能损失。方法排名随架构与滚动窗口变化；审计分离度随先前记忆增强但精确删除后可能仍很小；窗口级损失比较最高仅0.69；将股票级窗口视为独立会使绝对t统计量中位数膨胀1.9倍。

Conclusion: 时间序列遗忘需明确删除范围、针对相关架构与窗口做预言机验证，并采用功率感知的审计；遗忘正确目标是对齐重训预言机，而非在删除期上压低预测准确率。

Abstract: When a data license expires, deleting stored records does not remove influence encoded in a trained forecaster. Machine unlearning seeks to remove this influence without retraining. We benchmark temporal unlearning with 3,200 paired references trained on all data and oracles retrained without the requested period. The grid covers five architectures, four rolling folds, five deletable years, and three experimental deletion levels on an S&P 500 volatility panel. The 2020 COVID crisis year produces the largest memorization gap for every architecture. Removing it improves all three deployable models in every fold, with the largest improvement in the 2022 bear market, while the two non-deployable models respond inconsistently. The target for approximate unlearning is the oracle, not low predictive accuracy on the deleted period. In one Transformer cell, an oracle that never trained on 2020 still predicts it at an information coefficient of 0.51, compared with 0.55 for the reference; pushing predictions toward noise reduces test skill. Across twelve deployable architecture-method pairs, only TSMixer with the hinge method remains near the oracle in every fold, closing 74-118% of the reference-to-oracle gap without a measurable loss of test skill. Method rankings vary across architectures and rolling windows. Audit separation rises with prior memorization but can remain small after exact deletion. The window-level loss comparison reaches at most 0.69, and treating stock-level windows as independent inflates the absolute t-statistic by a median factor of 1.9. These results call for an explicit deletion scope, oracle validation for the relevant architecture and window, and power-aware auditing.

</details>


### [278] [Information-Theoretic Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26257)
*Yunfei Zhang,Wen Wen,Tieliang Gong,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦提示微调框架DPT4CL，将CLIP文本提示分解为任务共享提示分布与类别特定提示，以缓解检索依赖和分类器偏置，并在理论与实验上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示微调范式在持续学习中普遍存在两个问题：一是检索依赖，即模型适应效果对提示选择高度敏感；二是分类器偏置，即预测结果偏向新到达的类别。这两点限制了预训练模型在持续学习场景下的有效适配。

Method: 提出DPT4CL（Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning），将CLIP的文本提示解耦为两部分：任务共享提示分布通过优化信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）目标获得，用于促进跨任务知识迁移并缓解分类器偏置；类别特定提示用于增强类间可分性，且无需依赖显式提示检索。此外，从信息论角度建立统一的超额风险界，为框架的稳健泛化与遗忘抑制提供理论支撑。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准上的大量实验表明，DPT4CL取得了当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能；同时所建立的信息论超额风险界为框架的泛化性与抗遗忘能力提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 通过解耦提示设计结合信息瓶颈优化，DPT4CL有效克服了检索依赖与分类器偏置问题，实现了持续学习中的稳健泛化与遗忘缓解，兼具理论保证和实证优势，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to incrementally acquire knowledge from sequential data while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Recently, prompt tuning has attracted increasing attention as an efficient approach for adapting pre-trained models to CL tasks. However, existing prompt design paradigms commonly suffer from retrieval dependence and classifier bias, which make model adaptation sensitive to prompt selection and bias predictions toward newly arrived classes. To address these challenges, we propose Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning (DPT4CL), which decouples the CLIP textual prompt into a task-shared prompt distribution and class-specific prompts. The task-shared prompt distribution is derived by optimizing an Information Bottleneck objective to facilitate cross-task knowledge transfer and alleviate classifier bias, while class-specific prompts enhance inter-class separability without relying on explicit prompt retrieval. Furthermore, we establish a unified excess risk bound from an information-theoretic perspective, providing theoretical support for the robust generalization and forgetting mitigation of the proposed framework. Extensive experiments on standard CL benchmarks demonstrate that DPT4CL achieves state-of-the-art performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/Cloudfly-Z/DPT4CL

</details>


### [279] [Mode Collapse Is Cheap to Detect: A Ground-Truth-Free Pre-Flight Check for Neural Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26272)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经采样器的"预检"诊断方法：仅用能量函数E及其一、二阶导数，检测目标分布中未被采样器覆盖的缺失质量，成本约为训练预算的2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经采样器针对未归一化目标π̃=e^{-E}训练且无π样本，训练者无法判断昂贵的训练是否已悄然遗漏目标的某部分。常用诊断量（如自归一化ESS）由模型自身采样计算，局限于模型支撑集内：作者构造出一个自归一化ESS=0.99却遗漏87%目标质量的采样器，说明现有诊断存在严重盲区。

Method: 作者主张"检测缺失质量"严格易于"采样该质量"：检测只需每个遗漏盆地各一个点加上局部曲率估计，而修正需要重新训练采样器。据此设计预检流程，仅消耗采样器训练预算的百分之几，只使用E、∇E和∇²E。该检查可原样应用于无解析密度的受控SDE采样器，并带有自诊断机制。

Result: 在高斯混合、Many-Well及旋转各向异性Many-Well目标（真值可精确计算）上，检查在2.7%训练成本下将缺失质量估计到10^{-3}以内；调优的退火SMC参考需70%–280%训练成本却表现更差。对无解析密度的受控SDE采样器同样适用，而ESS与ELBO此时无法计算。自诊断在60个配置中通过16个，其中15个精度达10^{-2}或更好。

Conclusion: 该检查能低成本地产生"存在缺失质量"的证据，有时能证明搜索已稳定，但无法认证训练运行，其阈值是启发式的。作者明确界定方法边界，并在真实物理势能面LJ-13上标出失效位置及原因。

Abstract: Neural samplers are trained against an unnormalised target $\tildeπ=e^{-E}$ with no samples from $π$, which leaves the practitioner with no way to tell whether an expensive training run has silently dropped part of the target. The diagnostics in common use are computed from the model's own draws and are therefore confined to the model's support: we exhibit a sampler whose self-normalised effective sample size is $0.99$ while it misses $87\%$ of the target mass. We argue that \emph{detecting} missing mass is a strictly easier problem than sampling it: detection needs one point per missed basin plus a local curvature estimate, whereas correction needs the sampler retrained. We turn this into a pre-flight check that consumes a few percent of the sampler's own training budget and uses only $E$, $\nabla E$ and $\nabla^2 E$. On Gaussian-mixture, Many-Well and rotated anisotropic Many-Well targets with exactly computable ground truth, the check estimates the missing mass to within $10^{-3}$ at $2.7\%$ of training cost, where a tuned annealed SMC reference needs $70$--$280\%$ of training cost to do worse. It also applies unchanged to a controlled-SDE sampler that has no tractable density, where ESS and the ELBO cannot be formed at all. The estimator carries a \emph{self-diagnostic} that, without ground truth, is conservative in the safe direction: across $60$ configurations it clears $16$, of which $15$ are accurate to $10^{-2}$ or better. We are explicit about what this does and does not license: the check cheaply produces evidence of missing mass, and sometimes evidence that the search has stabilised, but it cannot certify a run, and its thresholds are heuristic. We then map the boundary of the method on a real physical landscape, LJ-13, and report where it fails and why.

</details>


### [280] [JAMPR+/L2D: scalable neural heuristic for constrained vehicle routing problems in dynamic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26275)
*Andrew Soroka,Alex Meshcheryakov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型在车辆路径问题中的泛化与适配能力，证明其在多类约束和距离矩阵变化场景下性能优越，并提出快速微调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实物流约束（车辆容量、时间窗、取送货多仓库CPDPTW）带来显著计算挑战。传统精确与启发式方法对中小规模（N≲100）问题有效，但缺乏对大规模物流任务的适应性与可扩展性，且面对距离矩阵变化时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型求解大规模CPDPTW问题，考察其在图距离矩阵显著变化下的表现；在CVRPLIB基准上测试中规模CVRP与VRPTW；针对距离矩阵较大变化，提出对JAMPR+进行快速微调的方法。

Result: 在CVRPLIB中规模CVRP与VRPTW上，JAMPR+/L2D在超过85%的实例中优于最先进启发式算法HGS，缩小了目标差距；在CPDPTW上训练的模型对更简单约束（CVRP、VRPTW）、不同问题规模及距离矩阵中等变化均具有良好泛化。在ORTEC数据（CPDPTW）上，快速微调策略显著降低目标差距，无需完整重训练。

Conclusion: JAMPR+/L2D在复杂约束车辆路径问题上具备强泛化能力，快速微调策略可在距离矩阵变化场景下兼顾精度与推理速度，适用于实际物流路径规划。

Abstract: The vehicle routing problems with real-world constraints (we consider vehicles capacity limits, time windows constrains, pickup-and-delivery multi-depo --- CPDPTW) pose significant computational challenges. While classical exact and heuristic methods remain effective to solve problems of small/medium size ($N\lesssim100$), they often lack adaptability and scalability for larger logistics tasks. In this work, we show how JAMPR+/L2D RL deep learning model, proposed in to solve large CPDPTW problems can be adopted in the case of substantial changes of graph distance matrix. We test performance of JAMPR+/L2D model for medium-sized CVRP and VRPTW problems on CVRPLIB benchmarks: JAMPR+/L2D outperforms the state-of-the-art heuristic HGS in over 85\% of instances, achieving improvement in objective gap. We show that the JAMPR+/L2D model trained on CPDPTW problem, generalizes well for tasks with simpler constraints (CVRP, VRPTW), for different problem sizes and for moderate changes in distance matrixes. For more substantial changes in distance matrixes, we propose here to make fast finetuning of JAMPR+: on ORTEC data (for CPDPTW) the proposed strategy remarkably reduces the objective gap without full model retraining, what will give both accuracy and rapid inference of the model in the practical routing scenarios with distance matrix changes.

</details>


### [281] [On the Effect of Bit-Level Parameter Perturbations in Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26280)
*Akanksha Raghapur,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较经典机器学习模型（HMM、SVM）与深度学习模型（MLP、LSTM）在参数扰动敏感性及隐写容量上的差异，发现在Drebin安卓恶意软件数据集上经典模型更脆弱、隐写容量更低，而神经网络因参数冗余具备更高隐写容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解不同类别学习模型对参数微小、定向修改的响应差异，并探索模型参数作为信息隐藏载体的潜力，从而为模型鲁棒性与隐蔽信息嵌入提供统一分析框架。

Method: 在Drebin安卓恶意软件数据集上，对HMM、SVM及MLP、LSTM开展参数定向修改实验，对比经典模型与深度模型的参数敏感性；同时通过测量可被覆写且不显著损害性能的参数比特比例，评估各模型的隐写容量。

Result: 经典模型表现出脆弱性，少量选定参数即可显著改变模型行为；其参数结构紧凑、参数高效且相对敏感，因此隐写容量有限。神经网络则具有参数冗余，修改可分散到众多参数上，性能影响极小，因而隐写容量更高。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了经典模型与神经模型在参数变化响应上的本质差异，对模型鲁棒性和隐蔽信息嵌入均具启示，并为跨模型类别的参数敏感性与隐写容量分析提供了框架。

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate how classical machine learning models respond to small, targeted modifications in their parameters. We compare and contrast these results to analogous experiments on deep learning models. For classical learning models, we consider Hidden Markov Models (HMM) and Support Vector Machines (SVM), and for comparison, we conduct analogous experiments involving Multilayer Perceptrons (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. When applied to the Drebin Android malware dataset, our results show that classical models are brittle, in the sense that a limited set of selected parameters can have a dramatic effect on model behavior. In a related set of experiments, we investigate the steganographic capacity of these same learning models, that is, the proportion of bits in model parameters that can be overwritten without having a significant adverse affect on a model. We find that classical models offer limited steganographic capacity due to their compact, parameter-efficient, and relatively sensitive parameter structure. In contrast, neural networks are parameter-redundant, enabling higher steganographic capacity, where modifications can be distributed across many parameters with minimal impact on performance. These results highlight differences in how classical and neural models respond to parameter changes, with clear implications for both robustness and hidden information embedding. Overall, this work provides a framework for understanding parameter sensitivity and steganographic capacity across different classes of learning models.

</details>


### [282] [Quantifying Protocol-Induced Uncertainty in Comparative Predictive-Model Evaluation: Evidence from Large-Scale Daily PM10 Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26288)
*Rafael da Silva,Kiersten Monahan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将评估协议差异带来的模型排名变化形式化为"协议诱导的排名不确定性"，提出协议敏感度评分（PSS）框架，并在欧洲425个与美国365个监测站的PM10序贯预测中验证：跨协议换模率（31.5%–35.3%）远高于协议内扰动（0.8%–4.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型的比较研究通常以对候选模型排名告终，但这些排名依赖评估协议，而协议本身的影响很少被当作不确定性来源加以考察。作者希望在报告模型优越性时，量化"合法的评估选择"能在多大程度上改变结论。

Method: 提出 Protocol Sensitivity Score (PSS) 与完全重拟合的协议内参照（full-refit intraprotocol reference），将"切换协议造成的排名位移"与"固定协议内常规选择造成的位移"进行对比。验证实验为大尺度日PM10序贯预测：比较静态划分与滚动原点（rolling-origin）两类协议，覆盖425个欧洲背景站与365个美国EPA监测站，并扩展候选模型集（3→9），另在冻结协议下检验留出站点的稳健性。

Result: 跨协议切换的PSS均值分别为0.801（欧洲）与0.772（美国），导致35.3%与31.5%的站点更换最优模型。协议内扰动在评分目标相同时的PSS仅为0.072与0.230，换模率为0.8%与4.9%，显著小于跨协议位移。候选集由3个扩至9个后，欧洲跨协议换模率升至60.2%。该模式在冻结协议下对留出的背景站与非背景站依然存在。

Conclusion: 模型选择结论会实质性依赖合法的评估协议选择，因此仅报告单一协议下的排名不足以支撑"模型更优"的主张。作者建议在声明模型优越性时，同时报告在一小组可辩护的协议内扰动下的排名稳定性。

Abstract: Comparative studies of predictive models often end by ranking candidate models, yet these rankings depend on evaluation protocols whose influence is rarely treated as a source of uncertainty. We formalize this problem as protocol-induced ranking uncertainty and introduce a framework that compares ranking displacement caused by switching protocols with displacement produced by conventional choices within a fixed protocol. We quantify these effects using the Protocol Sensitivity Score (PSS) and a full-refit intraprotocol reference.
  We validate the framework in a large-scale sequential prediction study of daily PM10. Static-split and rolling-origin evaluation are compared across 425 European background stations and 365 US EPA monitors. Switching protocols produces mean PSS values of 0.801 and 0.772 and changes the selected model at 35.3% and 31.5% of stations, respectively. In Europe, intraprotocol perturbations with identical scored targets produce PSS values of 0.072 and 0.230, with winner-swap rates of 0.8% and 4.9%. Between-protocol displacement is therefore substantially larger than the selected within-protocol references. Expanding the candidate set from three to nine models increases the between-protocol winner-swap rate to 60.2% in Europe. The pattern also persists under a frozen protocol applied to held-out background and non-background stations. These results show that model-selection conclusions can depend materially on legitimate evaluation choices. We recommend reporting ranking stability under a small set of defensible intraprotocol perturbations alongside claims of model superiority.

</details>


### [283] [CompKV: Compensation-Aware KV Selection for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26300)
*Zhen Huang,Ruizhe Yao,Danyi Liu,Xinrui Chen,Shuwei Li,Siru Zhong,Zijian Cao,Yushan Lai,Mingming Guo,Weijie Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CompKV提出了首个补偿感知的稀疏注意力框架，将token分块并让选择过程显式面向下游补偿机制优化，理论证明块级均值补偿的残差由块注意力质量与块内logit变化共同决定，据此用紧凑块级统计得到可部署的选择准则，并设计异步实现，在RULER与LongBench-Pro上优于对比稀疏基线，自注意力最高提速6.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存访存是LLM性能瓶颈，稀疏注意力通过只对部分token精确计算来加速，但被排除token的贡献需要补偿。现有方法先用注意力质量选token、再对未选token补偿，这种解耦设计忽略了选择与补偿的相互作用：选择本应优先保留那些一旦被省略会带来最大补偿误差的token。

Method: 提出CompKV，将token划分为块，并把选择显式对齐到下游补偿机制进行端到端优化。通过理论分析表明块级均值补偿残留的误差由块注意力质量与块内logit变异共同支配；用紧凑的块级统计量近似该残差，导出可部署的选择准则；并实现高效的异步执行。

Result: 在RULER和LongBench-Pro上，CompKV在所评估的稀疏基线中表现最佳，同时相对全注意力实现最高6.85倍的自注意力加速。

Conclusion: 将稀疏注意力的token选择与补偿机制耦合设计，可有效降低补偿误差，在不牺牲（甚至提升）长上下文任务性能的前提下显著降低KV缓存访存开销。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models (LLMs) are bottlenecked by KV cache memory traffic during long-context inference. Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate LLM inference by computing exact attention over a selected subset of tokens. To recover the contribution of tokens excluded from exact attention, recent methods apply coarse-grained compensation to the omitted attention tail. However, existing methods typically select tokens based on attention mass and only then compensate for the unselected tokens. This decoupled design overlooks their interaction: selection should prioritize tokens that would leave the largest compensation error if omitted. To address this limitation, we introduce CompKV, the first compensation-aware sparse attention framework that divides tokens into blocks and explicitly optimizes selection for the downstream compensation mechanism. Our theoretical analysis shows that the residual left by block-level mean compensation is governed by both block attention mass and within-block logit variation. We approximate this residual using compact block-level statistics, yielding a deployable selection criterion. We further develop an efficient asynchronous implementation. Experiments on RULER and LongBench-Pro show that CompKV performs best among the evaluated sparse baselines while delivering up to a $6.85\times$ self-attention speedup over full attention.

</details>


### [284] [PreGS: A Parameter-Transfer-Based Multi-Expert Graph Neural Network for Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26310)
*Zhicong Cai,Yinglong Zhang,Xiaoying Hong,Xuewen Xia,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参数迁移多专家框架PreGS及PreGSv2，在八个图数据集上取得有竞争力的节点分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一聚合机制难以刻画不同图数据集的多样结构模式；独立训练多个结构分支会带来显著开销且未必产生稳定的节点表示。

Method: PreGS先预训练多头GAT，将其第一层注意力头的线性变换权重迁移到多个GraphSAGE专家，冻结这些专家作为互补结构分支；将原始节点特征、GAT头表示与GraphSAGE专家表示融合后输入MLP，其输出再与预训练GAT的logits融合。PreGSv2进一步引入源级加权与结构门控机制实现自适应多源特征集成。

Result: 在八个公开图数据集上，PreGS与PreGSv2相对代表性图神经网络基线取得有竞争力的性能；消融、参数迁移、敏感性、聚合器、可视化与训练时间分析验证了框架的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过参数迁移构建互补结构分支并融合多源表示，可在控制开销的同时提升节点分类的稳定性与性能。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have achieved strong performance in node classification by aggregating information from graph neighborhoods. However, a single aggregation mechanism may be insufficient to capture diverse structural patterns across graph datasets. Moreover, independently training multiple structural branches can introduce substantial overhead without necessarily producing stable node representations. To address these issues, this paper proposes PreGS, a parameter-transfer-based multi-expert graph neural network framework. PreGS first pretrains a multi-head graph attention network (GAT) and transfers the linear transformation weights of its first-layer attention heads to multiple GraphSAGE experts. The transferred experts are frozen and used as complementary structural branches. The fused raw node features, GAT head representations, and GraphSAGE expert representations are fed into a multilayer perceptron (MLP), whose output is further fused with the pretrained GAT logits. Based on PreGS, we further develop PreGSv2, which introduces source-level weighting and a structural gating mechanism for adaptive multi-source feature integration. Experiments on eight public graph datasets show that PreGS and PreGSv2 achieve competitive performance against representative graph neural network baselines. Ablation, parameter-transfer, sensitivity, aggregator, visualization, and training-time analyses further validate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/LH-Czc/PreGS.

</details>


### [285] [Disaggregated Quantization: Specializing LLM Prefill and Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26333)
*Andrei Panferov,Maximilian Kleinegger,Sweta Priyadarshi,Tijmen Blankevoort,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"解耦量化"（DQ），为预填充与解码两阶段分别定制计算格式、权重与存储位置；在Qwen 3与Gemma 3上验证了精度与效率收益，并通过ODP实现1.78倍TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预填充（prefill）与解码（decode）对量化的需求不同：低精度算术加速提示处理，而紧凑权重减少生成时的内存流量。现有统一量化方案无法同时最优地满足这两个阶段的需求。

Method: 1) 提出解耦量化（DQ），分别针对prefill与decode定制计算格式、权重与存储放置；2) 在decode阶段移除激活量化；3) 训练独立的compute-native prefill权重量化版本（如NVFP4 prefiller）以配合已发布的GGUF低比特decoder；4) 提出卸载式解耦预填充（ODP），将prefill权重从SSD流式加载，按提示长度摊薄加载开销；5) 在vLLM的解耦服务下评估精度，并通过PTQ在最多2.8T参数模型上验证共享权重格式的解耦。

Result: - 在decode-heavy任务上移除decode阶段激活量化提升精度且不增加推理成本；
- 训练独立的compute-native prefill权重相对weight-only推理加速提示处理，并在2-3-bit解码下于decode-heavy与prefill-heavy任务上达到或超过其精度；
- 使用Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoder时，训练NVFP4 prefiller在MMLU-Pro上提升1-bit精度32.5点、MMMU-Pro提升35.3点，且不修改decode checkpoint；
- ODP在27B模型、8K提示长度下于llama.cpp中相较weight-only基线实现1.78倍TTFT加速。

Conclusion: 通过将预填充与解码的量化策略解耦，可在不牺牲（甚至提升）精度的前提下显著改善两阶段效率；ODP进一步解决了单设备容纳额外checkpoint的问题，方案在vLLM解耦服务与高达2.8T参数模型上得到验证，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Prefill and decode reward different approaches to quantization: low-precision arithmetic accelerates prompt processing, while compact weights reduce memory traffic during generation. We propose "disaggregated quantization" (DQ), which specializes computation formats, weights and storage placement to both of these phases. On Qwen 3 and Gemma 3, removing activation quantization specifically on decode improves accuracy on decode-heavy tasks without increasing inference cost. Training separate compute-native prefill weights accelerates prompt processing relative to weight-only inference while matching or exceeding its accuracy at 2-3-bit decode on both decode-heavy and prefill-heavy tasks. With released Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoders, training an NVFP4 prefiller improves 1-bit accuracy by 32.5 points on MMLU-Pro and 35.3 on MMMU-Pro without modifying the decode checkpoint. To accommodate the additional checkpoint on a single device, offloaded disaggregated prefill (ODP) streams its weights from SSD, amortizing loading over prompt length. On the same 27B model, ODP delivers a 1.78x time-to-first-token speedup over the weight-only baseline at 8K prompt length in llama.cpp. We evaluate accuracy under disaggregated serving in vLLM and further validate shared-weight format disaggregation through post-training quantization on models up to 2.8T parameters.

</details>


### [286] [Geometry-Aware Hyperbolic Residual Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26342)
*Alessio Colombo,Melika Ayoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出几何感知的双曲残差量化方法，在前向和反向传播中分别解决双曲加法非结合性与直通梯度估计忽略流形几何的问题，在多个层级任务中提升双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差向量量化（RVQ）可将连续表示转为离散多层级 token 序列，且其码本具有由粗到细的结构，许多数据域也存在潜在层级。然而主流方法在欧氏空间操作，未利用层级结构；双曲几何虽天然适合层级表示，但直接扩展会带来几何不一致：双曲加法非结合导致残差聚合不一致，标准直通估计忽略隐空间几何。

Method: 提出几何感知的双曲残差量化，同时修正前向与反向传播。前向：Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation，在 Poincaré 球上恢复残差量化的望远镜（telescoping）行为，保证残差聚合一致。反向：引入 discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator，将重建梯度作为单一几何块通过量化器，避免跨残差阶段的不稳定递归梯度传输。

Result: 在层级预测、推荐、图像 tokenization 与神经音频编码任务上，相比朴素双曲基线，本方法提升了双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。同时观察到明显的结构-压缩权衡：纯压缩任务中欧氏 RVQ 仍更优，而几何感知双曲量化更适合层级化组织的离散隐空间。

Conclusion: 几何感知双曲残差量化有效缓解了双曲 RVQ 的几何不一致问题，适合层级化离散隐空间；但并非通用替代方案，纯压缩场景下欧氏 RVQ 仍占优，方法价值取决于任务对结构组织的需求。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization turns continuous representations into discrete, multi-level token sequences. Yet most methods operate in Euclidean space, despite the coarse-to-fine structure of the resulting codes and the latent hierarchies present in many data domains. Hyperbolic geometry offers a natural alternative for hierarchical representations, but naive hyperbolic extensions introduce geometric inconsistencies: non-associative hyperbolic addition prevents consistent residual aggregation, while standard straight-through gradient estimation ignores the geometry of the latent space. We propose a geometry-aware hyperbolic residual quantization that addresses these issues in both the forward and backward passes. In the forward pass, Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation restores the telescoping behavior of residual quantization on the Poincare ball. In the backward pass, a discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator routes the reconstruction gradient through the quantizer as a single geometric block, avoiding unstable recursive gradient transport across residual stages. Evaluations on hierarchical prediction, recommendation, image tokenization, and neural audio coding tasks show that our method improves the stability and structural organization of hyperbolic residual codes over naive hyperbolic baselines. At the same time, we observe a clear structure-compression trade-off: Euclidean residual quantization remains preferable for pure compression, while geometry-aware hyperbolic quantization is most useful for hierarchically organized discrete latent spaces.

</details>


### [287] [PACT: From Credit Assignment to Critic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26355)
*Jiayan Fu,Hang Xu,Yong Zhang,Zhaokai Luo,Yao Hu,Dongyan Zhao,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为token级信用分配建立了公理化数学刻画（完备性、前缀一致性、中性性三条件唯一确定），并据此提出PACT算法（Actor-then-Critic更新顺序+重要性采样校正），在智能体数学推理上平均准确率72.87%，在SWE-bench Verified上通过率67.4%，均显著超越GRPO、PPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为大语言模型后训练的核心环节，但token级信用（token-level credit）缺乏公认的数学定义，导致其与常用训练信号（如OPD教师信号、RLOO、GAE等）之间的关系不清晰。作者希望给出统一的数学基础，以解释现有算法现象并指导改进的actor-critic训练流程。

Method: 1) 提出三个正则性条件——完备性（Completeness）、前缀一致性（Prefix Consistency）、中性性（Neutrality），并证明这三条唯一确定了token级信用；2) 用该框架统一分析现有信号：证明OPD中的理想教师等价于隐式critic，其期望策略梯度正比于token级信用诱导的梯度；证明response级RLOO在期望策略梯度贡献上与token级信用一致；3) 在有限结果奖励下证明近似的信用稀疏性，并说明GAE中critic的中间误差可与真实信用相当；4) 据此提出PACT（Policy Aligned Critic Training），采用先Actor后Critic（Actor-then-Critic）的更新顺序，对critic训练施加重要性采样校正，使critic更好地对齐更新后的策略。

Result: 在智能体数学推理任务上，PACT在四个基准上取得72.87%的平均准确率，分别比GRPO和PPO高出8.80和13.16个百分点；在SWE-bench Verified上，PACT通过率达67.4%，分别比PPO、GRPO和SAO高出2.4、2.0和3.8个百分点。

Conclusion: token级信用可由完备性、前缀一致性、中性性三条件唯一刻画，该公理化视角统一解释了OPD、RLOO、GAE等现有信号的性质。基于此提出的PACT通过调整更新顺序并校正critic训练，在数学推理与代码修复任务上均取得显著优于主流基线的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central component of large language model (LLM) post-training, yet token-level credit lacks a generally accepted mathematical definition, leaving its relationship to commonly used training signals unclear. We formulate three regularity conditions, namely Completeness, Prefix Consistency, and Neutrality, and prove that they uniquely determine token-level credit. This characterization provides a unified basis for explaining phenomena across existing algorithms and guides the development of an improved actor-critic training procedure. Through this lens, an ideal teacher in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) acts as an implicit critic, yielding an expected policy gradient proportional to that induced by token-level credit. Response-level REINFORCE Leave-One-Out (RLOO) signals match the expected policy-gradient contribution of token-level credit despite their coarser granularity. We further establish approximate credit sparsity under bounded outcome rewards and show how intermediate critic errors in Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) can become comparable to the underlying credit. These motivate Policy Aligned Critic Training (PACT), which adopts an Actor-then-Critic update order to apply importance sampling correction to critic training and better align the critic with the updated policy. In agentic mathematical reasoning, PACT achieves 72.87% average accuracy across four benchmarks, outperforming GRPO and PPO by 8.80 and 13.16 percentage points, respectively. On SWE-bench Verified, PACT achieves a pass rate of 67.4%, outperforming PPO, GRPO, and SAO by 2.4, 2.0, and 3.8 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [288] [FairMean: Promoting Fairness in Distributed Learning under Label Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26377)
*Huigan Zheng,Jiaojiao Zhang,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FairMean：以有界非减的局部损失函数加权客户端梯度，在无标签投毒时更利于解公平，在标签投毒下给出与投毒客户端比例平方成正比的平均稳定性界，实验在提升公平性的同时改善最差客户端精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平感知分布式学习通常优先优化高损失客户端以缩小性能差距，但标签投毒同样会制造高损失，从而把"优先高损失"的机制变成攻击入口，形成公平性与鲁棒性之间的冲突。

Method: FairMean用局部损失的有界非减函数对客户端梯度加权：权重随损失递增，从而优先高损失客户端以促进公平；同时权重存在上界，抑制投毒客户端因损失被过度放大。理论上分别分析无投毒情形下FairMean目标相对标准平均损失目标的解公平性，以及投毒情形下的平均稳定性界。

Result: 无标签投毒时，最小化FairMean目标比最小化标准平均损失目标更有利于解公平；在标签投毒下，平均稳定性界的攻击相关项与投毒客户端比例的平方成正比。实验显示FairMean通过降低精度方差提升公平性，并改善最差客户端精度。

Conclusion: 通过损失加权与权重上界的耦合设计，FairMean在公平性与抗投毒鲁棒性之间取得折中，为公平感知分布式学习提供了一种兼顾二者的加权聚合方案。

Abstract: Fairness-aware distributed learning prioritizes clients with large losses to reduce performance disparities, but label poisoning can create large losses, thereby inducing a fairness--robustness conflict. We propose FairMean to manage this conflict. FairMean weights client gradients using a bounded, nondecreasing function of local loss. The increasing weights prioritize high-loss clients to promote fairness, while the upper bound prevents excessive loss-induced amplification of poisoned-client gradients. In the absence of label poisoning, we show that minimizing the FairMean objective is more conducive to solution fairness than minimizing the standard average-loss objective. Under label poisoning, we establish an average-stationarity bound whose attack-dependent term is proportional to the square of the poisoned-client fraction. Experiments show that FairMean promotes fairness by reducing accuracy variance while improving worst-client accuracy.

</details>


### [289] [Learning to Defer with Guidance on Real World Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26384)
*Emma Sun,Joshua Strong,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在真实医学影像数据集上验证两阶段Learning to Defer（L2D），并提出含AI指导的三选项路由新设定，结果显示其性能优于经典L2D及单独AI/人工等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像解读量大耗时，全自动AI部署存在安全隐患且低特异度可能加重医生负担；L2D虽具理论保证，但尚未在带人工标注的真实医学数据集上验证，且现有任务普遍忽视AI辅助人工这一中间选项。

Method: 采用AI预测器固定、路由（rejector）模型可训练的两阶段L2D框架，在Collab-CXR（多标签胸片、每例多人工标注）上评估；提出L2D with Guidance，将决策空间扩展为自主预测、转交专家、转交专家并附AI指导三选项；比较多种rejector架构、损失函数与输入特征可得性，并在VinDr-CXR、CheXpert上复现。

Result: 含指导的两阶段L2D优于经典两阶段L2D，以及人工单独、AI单独和AI指导人工等基线；且该性能可用比现有文献中形式化L2D代理损失更简单的损失函数达成。

Conclusion: 首次在真实医学影像人工标注数据上开展L2D研究，验证了其可行性与优越性；提出的三选项L2D with Guidance框架在多个数据集上稳定提升，表明简化损失即可实现有效路由，为L2D落地医学场景提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Medical image interpretation is high-volume and time-consuming, and while AI interpretation can reduce workload, fully autonomous deployment carries potential safety concerns and low specificity may in practice lead to increased clinician workload. Learning to Defer (L2D) addresses this by selectively routing cases between autonomous prediction and human experts by learning from input features and AI model and human performance. While theoretical guarantees have been proven for L2D, its performance has not been validated on real-world medical datasets with human reader annotations. We evaluate the predictor-rejector formulation of two-stage L2D, where the AI predictor model is fixed and separate from the trainable routing or rejector model, on Collab-CXR, a multilabel chest X-ray dataset with multiple human annotations per case. This is the first work to look at L2D in the context of real-world medical imaging data with human annotations. We further introduce a new setup, L2D with Guidance, where the decision space is extended to three choices: predict autonomously, defer to a human expert, or defer to a human expert and provide AI guidance. We compare multiple rejector architectures and loss functions, and different input feature availabilities. This is reproduced on two larger datasets, VinDr-CXR and CheXpert. Our results show that two-stage L2D with Guidance outperforms classic two-stage learning to defer, as well as human-alone, AI-alone and AI-guided human baselines. Notably, this performance is achieved with simpler loss functions compared to formally defined L2D surrogate loss functions in current literature.

</details>


### [290] [TimeInteract: Towards Real-Time Interactive Intelligence for Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26389)
*Sheng Pan,Yongli Gu,Yiqing Guo,Warren Jin,Bo Du,Shirui Pan,Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"时间序列交互"新范式，并开发TimeInteract模型实现边接收流式观测边响应的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列是连续演化的，有意义的变化可能在任何时刻出现。但现有时间序列语言模型（TSLMs）本质上是静态的：要么对完整序列做离线处理，要么在流式输入与响应生成之间交替，导致交互过程中无法处理新观测。

Method: 提出TimeInteract，包含三项关键设计：(1) 双视角流式TS编码器，捕捉局部变化与历史动态；(2) 响应控制机制，学习何时触发响应；(3) 解耦式流式推理机制，将控制与响应生成分离，避免阻塞后续观测。同时构建交互能力层级（从理解到自适应性），并据此构建StreamTSI-34K数据集（34,588个episode、77,505条响应，涵盖合成与真实时间序列、单轮与多轮设置）。

Result: 在全部四个交互层级上，TimeInteract稳定优于现有LLMs、VLMs和TSLMs，在困难任务上提升最高达23.92分；同时改善了响应触发，实现近零流阻塞（near-zero stream stall）及最高2.15倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了"时间序列交互"这一新范式，使模型能够在生成响应期间持续处理新观测，并通过新数据集与三项关键设计验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Real-world time series evolve continuously, with meaningful changes potentially emerging at any moment. However, existing time-series language models (TSLMs) remain inherently static. They either receive complete sequences for offline processing or alternate between streaming input and response generation, which prevents processing of new observations during interaction. We introduce a new regime, Time-Series Interaction: a model continuously perceives incoming time-series observations and user intent, autonomously decides when to remain silent or respond, and continues processing new observations during response generation. To realize this, we develop TimeInteract with three key designs: a dual-view streaming TS encoder that captures local variations and historical dynamics, a response control mechanism that learns when to trigger a response, and a decoupled streaming inference mechanism that separates control from response generation to avoid blocking subsequent observations. We further formulate a hierarchy of interaction capabilities, progressing from Understanding to Adaptivity. Based on this hierarchy, we construct StreamTSI-34K, a large-scale streaming TS interaction dataset with 34,588 episodes and 77,505 responses across synthetic and real-world time series in single- and multi-turn settings. Across all four interaction levels, TimeInteract consistently outperforms existing LLMs, VLMs, and TSLMs, with gains of up to 23.92 points on challenging tasks. It also improves response triggering while achieving near-zero stream stall and up to $2.15\times$ inference speedup.

</details>


### [291] [Double Descent and Malign Overfitting in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26392)
*Raphaël Urfin,Tony Bonnaire,Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型训练虽等价于二次评分匹配回归，却出现灾难性过拟合与记忆化，而非回归中的良性过拟合。作者用CelebA上的U-Net实验与可解析的随机特征模型揭示：插值峰值出现在p~nm而非p~n，测试损失上升始于p~n，机制为偏差在p~n起增长且饱和于大值；但配以最优正则化（岭惩罚或早停）后，过参数化大模型仍优于任何无正则化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习"常识"认为过参数化（p>n）是良性的：模型更大泛化更好，无正则化的插值模型也能泛化，测试误差呈双下降曲线。扩散模型训练可归约为回归——最小化二次评分匹配损失——理应同样良性。但经验观察恰恰相反：扩散模型的过拟合是灾难性的，会把模型推入记忆化（memorization）状态。本文旨在解决这一悖论。

Method: 结合两类手段：一是在CelebA上训练的U-Net实验；二是可推导闭式学习曲线的随机特征（random-features）模型。通过引入每个训练样本的噪声实现数m，分析插值峰值位置与测试损失上升点，并进行偏差-方差分解以定位机制。

Result: 在固定每个训练样本m个噪声实现时，插值峰值确实出现，但位于p~nm而非标准回归的p~n；然而测试损失的上升远早于此，在p~n处即已开始，且与m无关。过拟合是恶性的（malign）：尽管训练的隐式正则化完全在起作用，它却把模型推向记忆训练集的"经验评分"而非"真实评分"。偏差-方差分解表明：评分估计器的偏差从p~n起开始增长；越过峰值后方差如回归中一样衰减，而偏差持续增长，二者最终饱和于较大值。由于扩散模型通常取m≫1，峰值被推至极大的模型规模，因而实际处于峰值前的上升分支，此时恶性过拟合已发生。

Conclusion: 该悖论得到解决：扩散模型过参数化的恶性过拟合源于偏差在p~n起增长并饱和，而非标准回归的方差主导机制。不过，过参数化在配合正则化时仍有益：在随机特征理论与U-Net实验中，经最优正则化（分别为岭惩罚与早停）的大模型优于任何无正则化模型。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom in deep learning holds that overparameterization---having more parameters $p$ than training samples $n$---is benign: larger models generalize better and, even without regularization, interpolating models generalize well, the test error following a double-descent curve. One might expect the same benign overfitting for diffusion models, whose training reduces to regression, i.e. to minimizing a quadratic score-matching loss. Yet the opposite is observed: overfitting here is catastrophic, driving the model into a memorization regime. We resolve this paradox by combining experiments on U-Nets trained on CelebA with a random-features model for which we derive closed-form learning curves. We show that with a fixed number $m$ of noise realizations per training sample, an interpolation peak does occur, but at $p\sim nm$ rather than at $p\sim n$ as in standard regression. The rise of the test loss, however, sets in much earlier, at $p\sim n$, independently of $m$. This overfitting is malign because, although the implicit regularization of training is fully at work, it drives the model toward the empirical score, which memorizes the training set, rather than toward the true score. A bias-variance decomposition pinpoints the mechanism: the bias of the score estimator starts to grow at $p\sim n$; past the peak the variance decays, as in regression, whereas the bias keeps growing and both saturate at a large value. Since diffusion models are trained with $m\gg1$, the peak is pushed to very large model sizes, and therefore sit on the rising branch that precedes it, where malign overfitting is already in play. Nevertheless, overparameterization remains beneficial when paired with regularization: in the random-features theory and in U-Net experiments, optimally regularized large models---via a ridge penalty or early stopping, respectively---outperform any unregularized models.

</details>


### [292] [OMatG-flash: An All-Atom Flow Map with Reinforce Adjoint Matching for Scalable Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26402)
*Thomas Egg,Harry Winston Sullivan,Ellad B. Tadmor,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OMatG-flash——首个用于无机晶体结构预测与从头生成的全原子流映射（flow map）模型，推理步数与墙钟时间较流/扩散模型降低一个数量级，同时保持与最先进方法相当的基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机材料发现对计算与储能等领域至关重要，生成式 AI 可加速材料发现流程，但现有流模型与扩散模型受限于候选材料采样（提议）成本过高，成为流程瓶颈，因此需要更高效的采样推理引擎。

Method: 提出 OMatG-flash，一种面向无机晶体结构的全原子流映射（all-atom flow map），同时支持晶体结构预测（CSP）与 de novo 生成（DNG）；为实现后训练微调，将 Reinforce Adjoint Matching 应用于流映射，以进一步优化性能。

Result: OMatG-flash 是面向材料的帕累托最优推理引擎：推理步数减少一个数量级、墙钟时间更短，同时在基准上达到与最先进模型相当的性能；经 Reinforce Adjoint Matching 微调后，在无条件 CSP 任务上的匹配率（match rate）与 RMSE 进一步提升。

Conclusion: 流映射在加速生成高质量候选无机材料方面具有潜力，其采样吞吐量的提升为数据饥渴型的材料发现工作流提供了必要支撑，是向高效材料生成迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: The discovery of novel inorganic materials drives technological breakthroughs in critical fields such as computing and energy storage. Generative AI has promised to accelerate the materials discovery pipeline, but state-of-the-art flow and diffusion models remain bottlenecked by the cost of proposing candidate materials. To address this, we introduce OMatG-flash, an all-atom flow map for inorganic crystal structure prediction (CSP) and de novo generation (DNG). OMatG-flash is a Pareto-optimal inference engine for materials, sampling candidate materials with an order of magnitude fewer inference steps and less wall-clock time than existing flow and diffusion models while demonstrating benchmark performance on par with the state-of-the-art. To enable post-training fine-tuning we apply Reinforce Adjoint Matching to flow maps, further improving match rates and RMSE on the unconditional CSP task. OMatG-flash showcases the potential of flow maps to accelerate generation of high-quality candidate inorganic materials and demonstrates a step forward in sample throughput necessary for data-hungry materials discovery workflows.

</details>


### [293] [DeepFEAv2: Deep Learning for Transient Finite Element Analysis Beyond Structured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26426)
*Georgios Triantafyllou,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepFEAv2是一个深度学习代理框架，可跨不同有限元网格拓扑与单元类型预测瞬态FEA仿真，联合预测节点与单元输出，精度与速度均显著优于DeepFEA和传统FEA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）广泛用于瞬态力学仿真，但计算成本高，限制实时与高分辨率应用；深度学习代理模型可降低成本，然而多数现有方法仅限稳态预测，或无法在时间维度上联合预测节点与单元输出（NEO）。最新的DeepFEA框架虽解决了上述问题，但仅适用于结构化有限元网格，亟需扩展到不同网格拓扑与单元类型。

Method: 提出DeepFEAv2框架，核心包含三部分：(a) 利用有限元连接矩阵按单元组织输入特征，并依据网格拓扑将其排列为输入序列的模块；(b) 一种新颖的神经网络架构，用于处理该输入序列并联合预测随时间变化的NEO；(c) 一种FEA信息驱动的优化策略，用于对NEO预测进行正则化。模型在结构化与无结构3D线弹性数据集以及压力驱动的主动脉瓣数据集上进行了评估。

Result: DeepFEAv2取得最高R^2达0.99、归一化误差低至0.38%；相比DeepFEA，R^2相对提升最高38.0%，归一化误差降低最高87.1%；推理速度比传统FEA快至多三个数量级。

Conclusion: DeepFEAv2能够高效建模日益复杂的有限元设置下的瞬态FEA仿真，为瞬态FEA提供了一个可扩展的代理框架。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) is widely used for transient mechanical simulations, but its high computational cost limits real-time and high-resolution applications. Deep learning surrogate models can reduce this cost; however, many existing approaches are restricted to steady-state prediction or cannot jointly predict Node- and Element-based Outputs (NEO) over time. The state-of-the-art DeepFEA framework has addressed these issues but remains limited to structured finite element (FE) meshes. To overcome this limitation, this study proposes DeepFEAv2, a deep learning surrogate framework that enables prediction of transient FEA simulations across different FE mesh topologies and element types. The main contributions of DeepFEAv2 are: (a) a module that uses the FE connectivity matrix to organize input features by element and arrange them into an input sequence guided by the mesh topology; (b) a novel neural network architecture designed to process the input sequence and jointly predict NEO over time; and (c) a FEA-informed optimization strategy for regularizing these NEO predictions. DeepFEAv2 was evaluated on structured and unstructured 3D linear elastic datasets, as well as on a pressure-driven aortic valve dataset. DeepFEAv2 achieved R^2 values up to 0.99 and normalized errors as low as 0.38%. Compared with DeepFEA, it achieved up to 38.0% relative increase in R^2 and up to 87.1% reduction in normalized error. DeepFEAv2 also performed inference up to three orders of magnitude faster than traditional FEA. These results demonstrate that DeepFEAv2 can efficiently model transient FEA simulations across increasingly complex FE settings, providing a scalable surrogate framework for transient FEA.

</details>


### [294] [One-Step Generative Surrogate Models via Block-Triangular Joint Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26435)
*Nicholas Geissler,Shreya Jha,Ricardo Baptista,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出块三角联合漂移（block-triangular joint drifting），将投影漂移场作用于连续状态的可经验获取的联合分布，在保持当前状态边缘分布的同时使第二分量直接采样下一状态的条件分布，实现单步随机轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移（drifting）可直接构造一步生成模型，但用于随机转移建模时需在同一当前状态下对下一状态多次采样以估计条件分布；而标准轨迹数据每个当前状态通常只对应一个实际观测的下一状态，无法提供条件分布的经验近似，导致直接应用受限。

Method: 提出块三角联合漂移：不直接对条件分布做漂移，而是在连续状态的可经验获取的联合分布上施加投影漂移场。块三角结构在保持当前状态边缘分布不变的同时，令其第二分量成为下一状态条件分布的直接采样器。所得代理模型每时间步仅需一次模型评估即可生成随机轨迹，时间步之间无需额外的辅助生成步骤。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能准确复现边缘统计量与轨迹相关统计量，并与确定性、扩散、流及蒸馏类生成代理模型相比，在精度—成本权衡上更具优势。

Conclusion: 块三角联合漂移绕开了对条件分布多采样经验近似的需求，实现了单步评估的随机轨迹生成，在统计精度与计算成本间取得良好折衷，为随机转移建模提供了有效的生成代理方案。

Abstract: Drifting provides a direct route to one-step generative models, but applying it directly to stochastic transition modeling requires multiple samples of the next state conditioned on the same current state. Standard trajectory data, however, typically provide only one realized next state for each observed current state and therefore do not provide an empirical approximation of the corresponding conditional distribution over possible next states. We introduce block-triangular joint drifting, which instead applies a projected drift field to the empirically accessible joint distribution of consecutive states. Importantly, the block-triangular architecture preserves the current-state marginal while making its second component a direct sampler of the conditional distribution of possible next states. The resulting surrogate generates stochastic trajectories with one model evaluation per time step, without auxiliary generative steps between time steps. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate marginal and trajectory-dependent statistics and favorable accuracy-cost tradeoffs compared with deterministic, diffusion-, flow-, and distillation-based generative surrogate models.

</details>


### [295] [Can We Predict Anomaly Detection Performance from Embedding-Space Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26460)
*Kevin Wilkinghoff,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无异常数据条件下的异常检测性能预测与模型选择，针对kNN检测器推导出AUC下界，并提出伪异常探针方法，在DCASE 2022-2025基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测系统通常仅用正常数据训练，但模型选择与评估却依赖标注异常样本，这在现实中难以获取。因此作者试图探究：在完全没有异常数据的情况下，能否预测异常检测的性能并完成模型选择。

Method: 针对kNN检测器，推导出AUC的下界，将检测性能与内点分数、外点分数之间的分离度及其各自方差联系起来；在局部缩放模型下，用该下界刻画密度变化、内在维度异质性以及跨域不匹配对分数变异性的影响。随后研究无异常模型选择，指出仅用内点分数方差不足以可靠预测不同表示下的性能，进而引入简单的伪异常探针（pseudo-anomaly probes），为估计相对分数分离度提供参考。

Result: 在DCASE 2022-2025基准上，涵盖4种嵌入模型和208个候选系统，基于伪异常的估计器显著提升了无异常模型选择效果；尤其是多样化的伪异常使无异常模型选择在域偏移情形下优于传统的开发集选择方法。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间几何结构蕴含关于异常检测性能的预测信息，同时仅依赖内点数据的性能估计具有表示依赖性，需借助伪异常探针加以补充。该工作为无异常数据下的模型选择提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Anomaly detection systems are often trained using normal data alone, while model selection and evaluation typically require labeled anomalies. We study whether anomaly detection performance can be predicted without access to anomalous data. For kNN-based detectors, we derive a lower bound on the area under the ROC curve (AUC) that relates detection performance to the separation between inlier and outlier scores and to their respective variances. Under a local scaling model, we use this bound to characterize how density variation, intrinsic-dimensional heterogeneity, and cross-domain mismatch contribute to score variability. We then investigate anomaly-free model selection and show that inlier score variance alone does not reliably predict performance across different representations. To address this limitation, we introduce simple pseudo-anomaly probes that provide a reference for estimating relative score separation. Experiments on the DCASE 2022-2025 benchmarks, spanning four embedding models and 208 candidate systems, show that pseudo-anomaly-based estimators substantially improve anomaly-free model selection. In particular, diverse pseudo-anomalies enable anomaly-free model selection to outperform conventional development-set selection under domain shift. These results show that embedding-space geometry contains predictive information about anomaly detection performance while also highlighting the representation-dependent nature of inlier-only performance estimates.

</details>


### [296] [When Recursive Models Finish Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26487)
*Hare Krishna,Shubham Singh,Stephen Ebert,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Tiny Recursive Models在困难数独上的推理完成动力学，将递归步数从16扩展到512，准确率大幅提升，并发现"轨迹条件各向异性稳定性"这一完成状态的动力学标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归模型可以在名义推理预算之外继续更新隐状态，因此在名义预算下输出错误时，无法判断是计算尚未完成，还是已进入持续失败的机制。作者希望区分"预算不足导致的失败"与"计算已完成"这两种情形。

Method: 在1000个困难数独上，对基于注意力与MLP的Tiny Recursive Models（TRM）进行动力学分析：把递归步数从名义的16步扩展到512步；分析隐状态运动幅度；计算沿轨迹方向的局部雅可比谱；并进行扰动实验，检验方向性稳定性及其多步命运。

Result: 扩展到512步后，累积精确求解准确率从59.2%提升到87.5%（注意力模型），从74.4%提升到91.9%（MLP模型），解决了名义预算下未解谜题的三分之二以上。两种架构中，隐状态运动在首次精确求解后急剧下降。完成状态通常沿轨迹方向局部收缩，但同一局部雅可比仍保留强扩张方向，即"轨迹条件各向异性稳定性"。扰动实验证实了这种方向性稳定性。最大扩张方向的多步命运不同：注意力模型中16步内被吸收，MLP模型中持续更久。该模式在第二个注意力检查点上同样成立。

Conclusion: 结果区分了名义预算下的失败与计算完成，并识别出跨两种递归架构共有的完成动力学标志：轨迹条件各向异性稳定性。

Abstract: Recursive models can continue updating their latent states beyond their nominal inference budget, so an incorrect output at that budget does not show whether computation is unfinished or has entered a persistently unsuccessful regime. We study the dynamics of completion in attention- and MLP-based Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) on 1,000 hard Sudoku puzzles. Extending recurrence from the nominal 16 steps to 512 steps increases cumulative exact-solve accuracy from 59.2% to 87.5% for the attention model and from 74.4% to 91.9% for the MLP model, solving more than two-thirds of the puzzles unsolved in the nominal budget. Across both architectures, latent-state motion drops sharply after the first exact solution. Completed states are typically locally contractive along the trajectory direction, even though the same local Jacobian retains strongly expanding directions. We characterize this phenomenon as trajectory-conditioned anisotropic stability. Perturbation experiments confirm this directional stability across both models. The multi-step fate of the maximally expanding direction differs: it is absorbed within 16 steps in the attention model but persists longer in the MLP model. The anisotropic-stability pattern also holds for a second attention checkpoint. Together, these results distinguish nominal-budget failure from completed computation and identify a common dynamical signature of completion across two recurrent architectures.

</details>


### [297] [Gap-Free Streaming PCA Beyond Rank-One Updates: Near-Optimal Rates and Applications to Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26508)
*Anming Gu,Syamantak Kumar,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对Oja算法在无特征间隙（gap-free）流式PCA问题下给出了新分析，获得接近最优的收敛率，并配以近乎匹配的下界，同时扩展到Rayleigh商近似PCA及差分隐私PCA应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式PCA旨在单遍扫描数据流中恢复主导谱子空间。已有近最优结果要么依赖特征间隙假设，要么仅限秩一更新，无法处理最一般的无间隙情形，因此需要更普适的分析。

Method: 作者重新分析经典的Oja算法，用于无特征间隙的一般均值矩阵情形；证明仅依赖单个随机更新的二阶矩界，避免已有近最优分析所需的几乎必然界及离线矩阵Bernstein界；并将结果推广至Rayleigh商定义的近似PCA。

Result: 在无间隙设定下得到接近最优的收敛率，并给出近乎匹配的下界；解决了[JJK+16]提出的Rayleigh商近似PCA开放问题；对亚高斯数据给出无间隙差分隐私PCA保证，在对数因子内解决了[Bro26]的猜想1.1。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了流式PCA的Oja算法分析，突破了间隙假设与秩一限制，为无间隙流式PCA及差分隐私PCA提供了更紧的理论保障。

Abstract: Streaming principal component analysis (PCA) seeks to recover a leading spectral subspace in a single pass over a data stream. We give a new analysis of the ubiquitous Oja's algorithm [Oja82] for the most general, gap-free variant of this problem, where no eigengap assumptions are made on the underlying mean matrix, complemented by a nearly-matching lower bound. Prior works achieving near-optimal rates for streaming PCA either required gap assumptions [JJK+16, HNWW21], or were limited to rank-one updates [AZL17, Lia23]. Our proof only uses a second moment bound on the individual stochastic updates, bypassing the almost sure bounds needed by prior near-optimal analyses, and the analogous offline matrix Bernstein bound. We also extend our result to a Rayleigh quotient notion of approximate PCA, addressing an open question of [JJK+16]. As our main application, we give gap-free differentially private PCA guarantees for sub-Gaussian data, settling Conjecture 1.1 of [Bro26] up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [298] [Notes on Fourier-Bessel wavelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26537)
*Marcel Venturotti,Georgios Exarchakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套Fourier-Bessel圆盘小波族的数学基础与构造方法，灵感来自Shaqfa等人的圆盘谐波，涵盖Bessel函数性质、Helmholtz方程解、高斯包络小波构造、归一化及频域闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于径向特征值间距近似线性并收敛于π，希望探索更均匀的径向频率分配，用于需要宽广且均衡频率覆盖的应用，而非取代传统二进小波组织方式。

Method: 从Bessel与修正Bessel函数性质出发，将Fourier-Bessel圆盘谐波推导为单位圆盘上带Neumann边界条件的Helmholtz方程解；通过施加高斯空间包络并引入零阶角向的零均值修正构造小波族，推导L2归一化常数，讨论L1归一化以保证频域峰值一致性，并导出频域闭式表示。

Result: 得到了Fourier-Bessel圆盘谐波解、完整的Fourier-Bessel小波族构造、对应的L2与L1归一化常数，以及小波的闭式Fourier频域表示。

Conclusion: 该构造为探索更均匀的径向频率分配奠定了基础，可能适用于需要宽广且均衡频率覆盖的应用，而不替代传统二进小波组织。

Abstract: These notes develop the mathematical foundations and construction of a Fourier-Bessel wavelet family inspired by the disk harmonics of Shaqfa et al.[9]. We begin with the relevant properties of Bessel and modified Bessel functions and introduce the wavelet properties required for the construction. We then derive the Fourier-Bessel disk harmonics as solutions to the Helmholtz equation on the unit disk subject to a Neumann boundary condition.
  Building on this basis, we construct a wavelet family by applying a Gaussian spatial envelope and introducing a zero-mean correction for the zeroth angular order. We derive the corresponding normalisation constants for $L^2$-based applications and discuss $L^1$-based normalisation for frequency-domain peak consistency. Finally, we derive a closed-form Fourier-domain representation of the resulting wavelets.
  The main motivation is the approximately linear spacing, which converges to $π$ between consecutive radial eigenvalues. Rather than replacing the conventional dyadic organisation of wavelet families, this construction lays out the foundation to explore whether a more uniform radial frequency allocation can be useful for applications in which broad and balanced frequency coverage is desirable.

</details>


### [299] [Towards Hierarchical GNNs for multi-grid power flow: generalization across operating scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26603)
*Carmine Delle Femine,Leire Garin Atxaga,Asier Diaz-Iglesias,Juan Pablo Maroto Herrera,Ane Miren Florez-Tapia,Marco Quartulli. Izaro Goienetxea Urziku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分层潜在通信模块借助GENC0校正网络中的两个约简图进行信息交换，在三种电网拓扑、每种三个初始化种子下训练200轮。KRON方法将宏族平衡电压误差从5.660±0.899降至0.851±0.110，较Flat GENCO降低85.0%、较Quotient降低31.0%，证明模型可在所研究拓扑内跨运行场景泛化，但尚未实现跨拓扑泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升多电网潮流模型对新运行场景的泛化能力，探索将分层潜在通信作为多电网潮流学习的组件。

Method: 在基于GENCO的校正网络中通过两个约简图交换信息；对比Kron导出传输、同锚点Quotient构造与扁平骨干三种结构。在三种电网拓扑上进行200轮初步训练，每种模型使用三个初始化种子；每种电网用200个新生成并预筛选的场景进行评估。

Result: 训练拓扑上，Kron将宏族平衡电压误差从5.660±0.899降至0.851±0.110，较Flat GENCO降低85.0%，较Quotient（1.235±0.225）降低31.0%；两个分层模型在全部三种训练拓扑和三个种子上均优于按母线均值拟合的训练解参考。在另外两种拓扑上，模型尚未超过拟合参考。

Conclusion: 结果表明在所研究拓扑内实现了跨运行场景泛化，且多电网共享一套学习参数；但这是场景泛化而非跨拓扑泛化，当前模型在校准迁移设置下尚不能超越拟合参考。下一步目标为泛化到未见拓扑。

Abstract: Hierarchical latent communication improves the generalization of a multi-grid power-flow model to new operating scenarios. The module exchanges information through two reduced graphs within a GENCO-based corrective network. We compare Kron-derived transports, a same-anchor Quotient construction and a flat backbone in preliminary trainings of 200 epochs on three grid topologies, with three initialization seeds per model. Evaluation uses 200 newly generated, preselected scenarios per grid. On the training topologies, Kron reduces the macro family-balanced voltage error from 5.660 +- 0.899 to 0.851 +- 0.110: an 85.0% reduction relative to Flat GENCO and 31.0% relative to Quotient, which reaches 1.235 +- 0.225. Both hierarchical models outperform a per-bus mean fitted on training solutions on every training topology in all three seeds. These results demonstrate generalization across operating scenarios within the studied topologies, with one set of learned parameters shared across grids. Evaluation on two additional topologies distinguishes this achievement from cross-topology generalization: the current models do not yet outperform the fitted reference in that calibrated- transfer setting. This preprint presents the architecture and preliminary evidence for hierarchical communication as a component of multi-grid power-flow learning, with generalization to unseen topologies as the next development objective.

</details>


### [300] [Greedy Decoding Is Not Precision-Invariant: Cross-Precision Output Divergence in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26621)
*Gaoyuan Du,Anam Nawaz Khan,Rex Zhou,Xiaoyang Liu,Deepayan Chakrabarti,Fnu Suya,Xueping Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 贪心解码并非精度不变：同一模型、提示与算法在BF16与FP16下输出不同，49-100%提示发散。作者提出误差传播分析，指出翻转取决于LM头前二logit margin与方向扰动，并给出选择性FP32 LM头重算的低开销部分缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正"贪心解码是确定性的"这一普遍认知，揭示其在数值精度层面并不具备精度不变性，并解释其内在机制。

Method: 在六模型（1.1B-7B，四族，12B补充刻画）、三基准上对比BF16与FP16输出；构建经验性误差传播分析，考察累积body error与LM头top-two logit margin相对方向扰动的作用；提出选择性FP32 LM头重算干预并做五项可检验预测。

Result: 49-100%提示出现发散，单token翻转可级联为轨迹级发散；22层累积body error无法区分翻转与非翻转步，结果主要由top-two margin与方向扰动决定；五项预测全部验证；选择性FP32 LM头重算在A10G提升+22-36 pp精确一致率（L4/A100 +12-21 pp），延迟开销<4%（batch≤4）；batch≥8及端到端FP8下收益消失。

Conclusion: 贪心解码存在精度依赖的发散，其机制可由LM头margin与扰动关系刻画；选择性FP32 LM头重算是有效的部分缓解，但非普遍确定性保证，训练期精度稳定性可能是决定因素。

Abstract: Greedy decoding from large language models is commonly treated as deterministic. We show it is not precision-invariant: the same model, prompt, and decoding algorithm produce different outputs in BF16 versus FP16 on identical hardware. Across our evaluations of six models (1.1B-7B parameters, four families; divergence additionally characterised at 12B) and three benchmarks, 49-100\% of prompts diverge; a single token flip often cascades into trajectory-level divergence. We develop an empirical error-propagation analysis and find that 22 layers of accumulated body error do not distinguish flipping from non-flipping steps; the outcome depends primarily on the top-two logit margin at the LM head relative to the directional perturbation between the top-two candidates. The analysis makes five testable predictions about intervention outcomes, including that applying more FP32 compute (broader scope) makes agreement worse. The experiments match all five predictions. The best-performing low-overhead intervention we evaluate, selective FP32 LM head recomputation, triggered only when the margin falls below a threshold, delivers +22-36 pp exact agreement on A10G (+12-21 pp on L4 and A100) at less than 4\% latency overhead in low-batch (batch size <=4) single-stream inference. We map the applicability boundary across six models and four batch sizes, and hypothesise that training-time precision stability is a determining factor. The method is a partial mitigation rather than a universal determinism guarantee: its benefit vanishes when body-originated error dominates, including at batch size >=8 and under end-to-end FP8 in our tests.

</details>


### [301] [Label-Efficient Learning for Ground-Based Sky-Image Classification: A Benchmark of Transfer Learning, Active Learning, and Pseudo-Labeling on GCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26631)
*Esther Bou Dagher,Viktoriya Bu-Dager,Boguslaw Zegarlinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对地基云分类任务，在有限标注预算下系统评估了监督迁移学习、不确定性主动学习与高置信伪标签三种策略的标签效率。结果表明，基于ImageNet预训练ResNet50的监督迁移学习已高度标签高效，1%标签即达0.635准确率，40%标签达0.730，接近全标签的0.735；主动学习与伪标签仅带来有限且不稳定的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基云分类对大气监测、太阳能预测、航空气象评估和气候观测等应用意义重大，但天空图像标注耗时费力，尤其当云类型视觉相似或混合时更困难。因此研究在有限标注预算下深度学习方法的标签效率具有实际价值。

Method: 不提出新架构，而是在Ground-based Cloud Dataset (GCD)上基准测试三种策略：(1)监督迁移学习，(2)基于不确定性的主动学习，(3)高置信伪标签。以ImageNet预训练的ResNet50作为统一的冻结骨干网络，在训练标签的1%至100%多个预算下重复五个随机种子进行实验。

Result: 监督迁移学习已高度标签高效：1%标签测试准确率为0.635±0.018，40%标签升至0.730±0.002，接近全标签的0.735±0.003。主动学习和伪标签与监督采样具有竞争力，并在某些指标和预算下有小幅提升，但均未带来大且一致的总体增益。诊断分析显示：被接受的伪标签可靠，准确率为0.946至0.977，但偏向更容易的高置信天空类型组；不确定性采样则优先查询视觉上具有挑战性的组，包括Mixed及易混淆的Stratocumulus和Cumulonimbus组，但这些针对性采样仅带来有限增益。

Conclusion: 总体而言，迁移学习显著降低了GCD的标注需求，而简单的主动学习和半监督策略相比强监督基线仅提供有限的额外收益。

Abstract: Accurate ground-based cloud classification is important for atmospheric monitoring, solar-energy forecasting, aviation weather assessment, and climate observation systems. However, reliable sky-image annotation is time-consuming, especially when cloud types are visually similar or mixed. We study the label efficiency of deep learning for ground-based cloud classification using the Ground-based Cloud Dataset (GCD). Rather than proposing a new architecture, we benchmark three practical strategies under limited annotation budgets: supervised transfer learning, uncertainty-based active learning, and high-confidence pseudo-labeling. An ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 is used as a common frozen backbone, with experiments repeated over five random seeds for label budgets from $1\%$ to $100\%$ of the training labels. Supervised transfer learning is already highly label-efficient: test accuracy increases from $0.635 \pm 0.018$ with $1\%$ labels to $0.730 \pm 0.002$ with $40\%$ labels, approaching the full-label result of $0.735 \pm 0.003$. Active learning and pseudo-labeling are competitive with supervised sampling and provide small improvements for some metrics and budgets, but neither gives a large or consistent aggregate gain. Diagnostic analyses show that accepted pseudo-labels are reliable, with accuracy from $0.946$ to $0.977$, but biased toward easier high-confidence sky-type groups. In contrast, uncertainty sampling preferentially queries visually challenging groups, including Mixed and the confusable Stratocumulus and Cumulonimbus groups, but these targeted acquisitions yield only modest gains. Overall, transfer learning substantially reduces annotation requirements for GCD, while simple active and semi-supervised strategies provide limited additional benefit over a strong supervised baseline.

</details>


### [302] [MAGIC: Mixed-Granularity Agent Graphs via Incremental Construction with Dense-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26667)
*Kairui Yang,Ziheng Yi,Xunkai Li,Minghao An,Zhanke Liu,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGIC，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的混合粒度协作图生成方法，通过稠密奖励强化学习在局部为每个功能角色选择粒度，兼顾细粒度控制与可复用协作模式，在八个基准上超越SOTA并展现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作拓扑直接影响LLM多智能体系统的性能与执行成本，但现有任务特定协作图生成方法要么全程使用单智能体，要么全程使用预定义群组，忽视了不同子任务间协作需求的差异。

Method: MAGIC通过顺序选择功能角色、将其实例化为单智能体或可复用群组、并连接到既有单元来构造混合粒度智能体图；采用稠密奖励强化学习框架，直接以当前策略采样轨迹的回报优化构造策略，并使用基于势能的奖励塑形（potential-based reward shaping），从探针效用与结构信号提供中间反馈，同时保持累积任务奖励不变。

Result: MAGIC在八个基准测试上超越当前最先进基线，并在效率研究中展现出强劲的推理效率。

Conclusion: 局部选择角色粒度可在单一组织内结合细粒度控制与可复用协作模式，且结合稠密奖励与奖励塑形的RL框架能有效应对组合构造空间大、中间反馈稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Collaboration topology shapes both the performance and execution cost of LLM-based multi-agent systems. Because tasks differ in complexity and required capabilities, recent approaches generate task-specific collaboration graphs that specify agent participation and information flow. However, representative topology generators use either individual agents or predefined groups throughout an organization, overlooking differing collaboration needs across subtasks. Our key insight is to select granularity locally for each functional role, combining fine-grained control with reusable collaboration patterns within one organization. Learning such organizations requires exploring a combinatorial construction space with limited intermediate feedback from final-answer rewards. Therefore, we propose MAGIC, a dense-reward reinforcement learning framework for mixed-granularity graph generation. Specifically, MAGIC constructs a mixed-granularity agent graph by sequentially selecting a functional role, instantiating it as a single agent or reusable group, and connecting it to existing units. We directly optimize the construction policy using returns from trajectories sampled under the current policy and use potential-based reward shaping to provide intermediate feedback from probe-based utility and structural signals while preserving the cumulative task reward. MAGIC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across eight benchmarks and demonstrates strong inference efficiency in our efficiency study.

</details>


### [303] [A Spectral Theory of Grokking: Weight Decay induces Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26679)
*Lenz Pracher,Pascal de Jong,Oskar Lieshaus,Alan Jeffares,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对grokking现象给出了定量理论：在平方损失与L2权重衰减下，均匀网络从lazy学习向rich学习转变时，记忆后残留的误差沿NTK小特征值方向累积并反馈到NTK动力学，投影后形成"残差驱动核增长与权重衰减竞争"的约化系统，预测泛化时间由学习率与权重衰减之积控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking表现为训练拟合与泛化之间存在显著延迟，且该延迟期间网络从固定NTK的lazy regime转向任务相关特征方向持续演化的rich regime。已有工作多停留在现象描述，缺乏从拟合后特征学习到泛化onset及其超参数相图的机制性定量推导，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对均匀网络、平方损失与L2权重衰减，分析记忆完成后仍存在的有限残差，发现残差占比在对应NTK较小特征值的任务分量上更大；将残差反馈进NTK自身动力学，并把结果投影到任务相关谱方向，得到一个约化系统，其中残差驱动的核增长与权重衰减相互竞争。随后在模加法任务上用均匀MLP（84×90学习率-权重衰减网格）与单块Transformer（42×45网格）进行验证。

Result: 理论预测：泛化时间尺度由学习率与权重衰减之积控制；存在临界衰减，超过后任务对齐的NTK结构不再支持泛化，特征学习在该临界点附近对数式减慢；更强的衰减可导致完全无法拟合。实验上，均匀MLP在训练精度饱和后任务对齐的傅里叶结构仍继续在NTK中形成，网格实验复现了预测的相几何及泛化时间随学习率与权重衰减的逆乘积缩放；单块Transformer虽不严格满足均匀性，仍呈现相似的宏观相结构与相同的转变时间缩放。

Conclusion: 论文给出了连接拟合后特征学习与泛化onset及其在学习率-权重衰减平面相结构的机制性推导，说明延迟泛化可由残差驱动NTK演化与权重衰减的竞争统一解释，并在两类架构上得到一致的相图与缩放规律。

Abstract: In grokking an early fit to the training data separates from a much later improvement in generalization. During this delay, training can move from a fixed neural tangent kernel (NTK) regime to one in which task-relevant kernel eigendirections continue to evolve. We provide a quantitative theory for how this transition from lazy to rich learning can produce delayed generalization. For homogeneous networks trained with squared loss and $L_2$ weight decay, we show that a finite residual remains after memorization, with larger residual fractions in target components associated with smaller NTK eigenvalues. These residuals feed back into the dynamics of the NTK itself, and projecting the resulting dynamics onto task-relevant spectral directions yields a reduced system in which residual-driven kernel growth competes with weight decay. This system predicts that the grokking timescale is controlled by the product of learning rate and weight decay, that feature learning slows logarithmically near a critical decay above which task-aligned NTK structure can no longer support generalization, and that stronger decay can prevent fitting altogether. We test these predictions in modular addition. In a homogeneous MLP, task-aligned Fourier structure continues to emerge in the NTK after training accuracy has saturated, and an 84$\times$90-grid of trained networks across varying learning rate and weight decay recovers the predicted phase geometry and inverse-product scaling of the generalization time with learning rate and weight decay. A one-block Transformer shows similar macroscopic phase structure in a 42$\times$45-grid, as well as the same transition-time scaling despite violating exact homogeneity. Together, these results provide a mechanistic derivation connecting post-fit feature learning to both the onset of generalization and its phase structure in the learning rate and weight decay plane.

</details>


### [304] [Train Where the Quantized Model Goes: On-Policy Distillation for Low-Bit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26708)
*Yuanteng Chen,Zhilei Liu,Peisong Wang,Yuantian Shao,Chuangyi Li,Weining Wang,Shuang Qiu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对量化感知蒸馏（QAD）在数学与代码推理上的性能退化问题，提出在策略蒸馏（OPD）阶段，通过让学生模型在量化前向路径上自主生成并从全精度教师获取反馈，结合token级指导与任务验证奖励，从而恢复长文本推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAD虽能恢复大部分短文本问答性能，但在低于3比特量化时数学与代码推理严重受损，长生成常退化为重复循环，耗尽解码预算却无法完成解答。作者将这一差距归因于量化放大的暴露偏差：QAD在固定语料前缀上训练，而量化导致的偏差会沿模型自身的自回归轨迹累积。

Method: 提出在策略蒸馏（OPD）阶段，将教师监督放置在量化模型实际到达的状态上。从QAD检查点出发，学生模型通过部署时使用的量化前向路径生成，并在自身前缀上接收冻结的全精度教师的反馈，融合密集的token级指导与任务验证器奖励。

Result: 在四个模型、2.79与1.88有效比特下，OPD将MATH-500上的平均BF16性能保持率从35%提升至70%，HumanEval上从66%提升至91%，同时保持短文本性能；在同等预算比较中，推理增益显著超过持续使用教师强制式QAD。

Conclusion: 通过耦合QAD稳定的低比特初始化与OPD的在策略推理恢复，该框架提供了一个全面的低于3比特量化方案，在保留广泛能力的同时恢复长文本推理能力。

Abstract: Quantization-aware distillation (QAD) restores much of the short-form question-answering performance lost to sub-3-bit quantization, yet leaves mathematical and code reasoning substantially impaired. Long generations often degenerate into repetitive loops, exhausting the decoding budget without completing a solution. We trace this gap to quantization-amplified exposure bias: QAD trains on fixed corpus prefixes, while quantization-induced deviations compound along the model's own autoregressive trajectories. To address this mismatch, we introduce an on-policy distillation (OPD) stage that places teacher supervision where the quantized model actually goes. Starting from a QAD checkpoint, the student generates through the quantized forward path used at deployment and receives feedback from a frozen full-precision teacher on its own prefixes, combining dense token-level guidance with task-verifier rewards. Across four models at 2.79 and 1.88 effective bits, OPD raises average BF16 performance retention from 35% to 70% on MATH-500 and from 66% to 91% on HumanEval while preserving short-form performance, with reasoning gains substantially exceeding those of continued teacher-forced QAD in matched-budget comparisons. By coupling QAD's stable low-bit initialization with OPD's on-policy reasoning recovery, our framework provides a comprehensive sub-3-bit solution that preserves broad capabilities while restoring long-form reasoning.

</details>


### [305] [The Sirens' Song: When Proximal Background Context Overshadows Distant Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26718)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Jie Lu,Wei Duan,En Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出长上下文LLM中"邻近陷阱"问题，即注意力不足源于近端无关背景的累积竞争而非距离本身，并提出LYRA机制重塑检索分布以对齐远距离证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM研究主要致力于克服"距离"本身带来的困难，但作者发现对远距离证据注意力不足的深层原因并非距离，而是与大量任务无关的近端背景的累积竞争。这一被忽视的机制阻碍了远距离证据的有效利用。

Method: 提出LYRA（Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment），一种t分布定向匹配机制。它通过重塑上下文检索分布，将更多注意力质量导向任务相关证据，同时保留已编码的相对位置信息。此外构建ProxBench多层级细粒度基准，用于评估在近端背景干扰递增条件下远距离证据的利用能力。

Result: 在LongBench-v2、RULER和LongBench上的大量实验表明，LYRA在不同上下文长度和任务类别上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 通过将问题重新定义为近端背景竞争而非单纯距离，并采用重尾定向匹配对齐相关性，本文为长上下文检索提供了新视角与有效方法，并配套提出ProxBench以系统评估该问题。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs focus on retrieving distant evidence from extensive context, yet existing work has largely focused on overcoming distance alone. In this work, we identify the Proximity Trap, insufficient attention to distant evidence often arises less from distance itself than from cumulative competition with abundant, task-irrelevant proximal background. To address the Proximity Trap, we introduce LYRA (Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment), a t-distributed directional matching mechanism that reshapes the context retrieval distribution, directing more attention mass toward task-relevant evidence, while preserving the relative positional information encoded. Extensive experiments on LongBench-v2, RULER, and LongBench demonstrate consistent improvements across context lengths and task categories. We further introduce ProxBench, a multi-level fine-grained benchmark for evaluating distant evidence utilization under increasing proximal background interference. Project page: https://xiaoyuyoung.github.io/LYRA/

</details>


### [306] [EquivSVA: A Formally Verified Dataset of Behavioral Assertions Across Equivalent RTL Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26751)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EquivSVA，一个基于“行为族”组织形式、经过形式化验证的 SystemVerilog 断言数据集，用于研究生成断言是否捕捉外部可观测行为而非特定 RTL 实现细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集与基准主要支持大规模训练、形式化评估、规范到断言生成及变异测试等目标，但缺乏对“生成断言是否捕捉外部可观测行为、是否依赖某一 RTL 实现的偶然细节”这一补充性问题的研究支持。因此需要一个围绕行为族组织、能控制实现变体的数据集。

Method: 提出 EquivSVA：以行为族为组织单元，每个族包含同一外部可观测行为的四个结构不同 RTL 实现、共享的接口级 gold 属性、三个受控变异体以及形式化验证证据。数据集覆盖 12 个类别共 120 个行为族、480 个参考 RTL 实现、914 个 gold 属性、360 个变异体。每个最终行为族通过固定 17 项验证套件，涵盖 RTL 等价性、gold 属性证明、属性可达性、变异体可区分性及变异体上的 gold 属性检查，并提供固定的族安全训练/开发/测试划分。作为分析示例，在留出测试集上评估开源 Apache-2.0 许可的 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct 模型。

Result: 在测试集上，模型生成的 293 个仅接口属性中有 93 个形式化可靠；在 24 个测试族中有 14 个族，其可靠属性数量在不同等价实现之间存在变化。这说明行为族组织可用于在不改变预期功能的前提下，对断言生成鲁棒性进行受控研究。

Conclusion: EquivSVA 通过行为族组织与形式化验证，支持对断言生成鲁棒性的受控研究，且数据集、生成器、验证脚本与案例研究产物已在 https://github.com/aditigupta96/EquivSVA 公开释放。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to generate SystemVerilog Assertions from natural-language specifica- tions and register-transfer-level designs. Existing datasets and benchmarks support important goals such as large- scale training, formal evaluation, specification-to-assertion generation, and mutation-based testing. A complemen- tary need is to study whether a generated assertion cap- tures externally observable behavior or depends on inci- dental details of one RTL implementation. We present EquivSVA, a formally verified dataset organized around behavior families. Each family contains four structurally distinct RTL implementations of the same externally ob- servable behavior, shared interface-level gold properties, three controlled mutants, and formal-validation evidence. EquivSVA contains 120 behavior families across 12 cat- egories, 480 reference RTL implementations, 914 gold properties, and 360 mutants. Every final family passes a fixed 17-job validation suite covering RTL equivalence, gold-property proofs, property reachability, mutant dis- tinguishability, and gold-property checks on mutants. We also provide fixed family-safe train, development, and test splits. As a small demonstration of the analyses en- abled by the dataset, we evaluate the publicly released, Apache-2.0-licensed Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct model on the held-out test split. Of 293 interface-only generated properties, 93 are formally sound, and the number of sound properties varies across equivalent implementations for 14 of 24 test families. These results illustrate how behavior-family organization can support controlled stud- ies of assertion-generation robustness without requiring changes in intended functionality. The dataset, generators, validation scripts, and case-study artifacts are publicly released at https://github.com/aditigupta96/EquivSVA.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于 Upworthy 档案的 sim-to-real 效度研究显示：在预测聚合点击行为时，无角色设定的零样本 LLM 基线显著优于人口统计角色面板，角色扮演反而注入偏差与噪声，降低预测效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销者日益依赖 LLM"合成人格"预测受众对文案的反应，其前提是角色条件化 LLM 能模拟人类样本。然而该预测对真实行为是否有效、人格机制是否真正有用，缺乏严格的 sim-to-real 验证。

Method: 以 Upworthy Research Archive（数千个共享真实流量的标题 A/B 测试，含实测点击率）作为留出真值。对比两种方法：(1) 基于真实受众人口统计的十人人格面板；(2) 无角色的零样本基线，直接询问模型典型读者的点击可能性。效度只能在有统计可区分赢家的可靠子集（n=399）上测量。通过三个独立 Upworthy 划分、跨域新闻数据集、以及种子、提示措辞、模型选择（三个 Gemini 层级与 OpenAI gpt-4.1）进行稳健性检验。

Result: 真值可靠性是核心约束：多数 A/B 测试无可统计区分的赢家。与人格模拟前提相反，角色条件化降低预测效度——无角色基线排序显著更优（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1 准确率 49.2%），人格面板更差（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），置信区间不重叠。结论在三个 Upworthy 划分中复现，在另一领域新闻数据集上方向一致，且对种子、措辞与模型选择稳健（含 gpt-4.1 的显著配对差距）。

Conclusion: 预测聚合参与度时，普通 LLM 排序器优于人格模拟；合成人格不仅预测能力弱，而且比不使用更差。直接询问模型可获取准确的人群级先验，而强制其扮演特定人格会引入偏差与噪声。所有数字均可由公开的 artifact-first 复现包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [308] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕TabPFN v2.5在生物医学表格数据微调中的优化问题，系统比较了五种基于AdamW的预条件策略，覆盖59个生物医学数据集，发现原始AdamW表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在结构化生物医学数据分析中展现出潜力，其中TabPFN适合低数据量表格分类任务；然而优化与预条件策略对该类模型生物医学微调的影响尚缺乏系统研究，尤其是曲率感知预条件器在真实医疗场景下的有效性不明确。

Method: 对TabPFN v2.5在59个生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、SMC、KEEL及UCI生物医学基准）上微调，系统评估五种基于AdamW的预条件策略，从预测性能、计算效率与统计显著性三个维度进行比较分析。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在整体预测性能和统计排名上始终最佳；现有曲率感知预条件器在多样化的生物医学学习场景中未能提供稳定可靠的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法难以充分刻画生物医学表格学习的优化特性，提示未来需针对医疗导向的表格基础模型开发具备生物医学感知能力的专用预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis.
